    // =========================================================
    // PRAYTCLIENT GOD MODE V9.0 AUDIO & HUD HELPERS
    // =========================================================
    const praytAudio = {
        ctx: null,
        getCtx() {
            if (!this.ctx) {
                const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
                if (AudioCtx) this.ctx = new AudioCtx();
            }
            if (this.ctx && this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {
                this.ctx.resume();
            }
            return this.ctx;
        },
        playSwarmConnected() {
            if (!state.prayt || !state.prayt.soundEnabled) return;
            try {
                const ctx = this.getCtx();
                if (!ctx) return;
                const now = ctx.currentTime;
                const playChime = (freq, start, duration) => {
                    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
                    const gain = ctx.createGain();
                    osc.type = 'sine';
                    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, start);
                    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.18, start);
                    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, start + duration);
                    osc.connect(gain);
                    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
                    osc.start(start);
                    osc.stop(start + duration);
                };
                playChime(587.33, now, 0.35);        // D5
                playChime(880.00, now + 0.16, 0.55); // A5
            } catch (_) {}
        },
        playClick() {
            if (!state.prayt || !state.prayt.soundEnabled) return;
            try {
                const ctx = this.getCtx();
                if (!ctx) return;
                const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
                const gain = ctx.createGain();
                osc.type = 'sine';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(820, ctx.currentTime);
                osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(410, ctx.currentTime + 0.04);
                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.06, ctx.currentTime);
                gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.04);
                osc.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(ctx.destination);
                osc.start();
                osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.04);
            } catch (_) {}
        },
        playStart() {
            if (!state.prayt || !state.prayt.soundEnabled) return;
            try {
                const ctx = this.getCtx();
                if (!ctx) return;
                const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
                const gain = ctx.createGain();
                osc.type = 'triangle';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(280, ctx.currentTime);
                osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(740, ctx.currentTime + 0.12);
                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, ctx.currentTime);
                gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.12);
                osc.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(ctx.destination);
                osc.start();
                osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.12);
            } catch (_) {}
        },
        playStop() {
            if (!state.prayt || !state.prayt.soundEnabled) return;
            try {
                const ctx = this.getCtx();
                if (!ctx) return;
                const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
                const gain = ctx.createGain();
                osc.type = 'triangle';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(650, ctx.currentTime);
                osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(220, ctx.currentTime + 0.14);
                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, ctx.currentTime);
                gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.14);
                osc.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(ctx.destination);
                osc.start();
                osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.14);
            } catch (_) {}
        },
        playAlert() {
            if (!state.prayt || !state.prayt.soundEnabled || !state.prayt.radarSound) return;
            const now = Date.now();
            if (now - (state.prayt.lastBeepAt || 0) < 380) return;
            state.prayt.lastBeepAt = now;
            try {
                const ctx = this.getCtx();
                if (!ctx) return;
                const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
                const gain = ctx.createGain();
                osc.type = 'sawtooth';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, ctx.currentTime);
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(440, ctx.currentTime + 0.08);
                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.18, ctx.currentTime);
                gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.01, ctx.currentTime + 0.18);
                osc.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(ctx.destination);
                osc.start();
                osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.18);
            } catch (_) {}
        }
    };

    function praytShowToast(msg, type = 'info') {
        let toast = document.getElementById('prayt-toast');
        if (!toast) {
            toast = document.createElement('div');
            toast.id = 'prayt-toast';
            document.body.appendChild(toast);
        }
        const colors = {
            info: '#00e676',
            warn: '#ff9800',
            danger: '#ff4d5a',
            neon: '#00e5ff'
        };
        const c = colors[type] || '#00e676';
        toast.innerHTML = `<span style="margin-right:6px;">⚡</span>${msg}`;
        toast.style.borderColor = c;
        toast.style.boxShadow = `0 0 16px ${c}55`;
        toast.classList.add('prayt-toast-show');
        clearTimeout(toast._timer);
        toast._timer = setTimeout(() => {
            toast.classList.remove('prayt-toast-show');
        }, 2200);
    }

    function praytUpdateLoupe(e) {
        if (!state.prayt || !state.prayt.pixelLoupeActive) return;
        const hud = document.getElementById('prayt-loupe-hud');
        if (!hud) return;
        const pt = getCanvasPoint(e);
        if (!pt) {
            hud.classList.remove('active');
            return;
        }
        hud.classList.add('active');
        const x = pt.x, y = pt.y;
        let hex = '#??????';
        let cid = '-';
        if (state.prayt.mapCache && x >= 0 && x < state.prayt.mapWidth && y >= 0 && y < state.prayt.mapHeight) {
            const idx = y * state.prayt.mapWidth + x;
            const currentCid = state.prayt.mapCache[idx];
            if (currentCid >= 0) {
                cid = currentCid;
                const rgb = getPaletteRgbById(currentCid);
                if (rgb !== null && rgb !== undefined) {
                    hex = '#' + rgb.toString(16).padStart(6, '0').toUpperCase();
                }
            }
        }
        hud.innerHTML = `
            <span id="prayt-loupe-color-swatch" style="background:${hex};"></span>
            <b>[${x}, ${y}]</b>
            <span style="color:#aaa;">${hex}</span>
            <span style="color:#ffd54f;">(#${cid})</span>
        `;
    }

    // =========================================================
    // HELPERS
    // =========================================================

    function clamp(value, min, max) {
        return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
    }

    function getCanvas() {
        return (
            document.querySelector('#canvas') ||
            document.querySelector('main canvas') ||
            document.querySelector('canvas')
        );
    }

    function getCanvasPoint(event) {
        const canvas = getCanvas();

        if (!canvas) return null;

        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();

        if (
            event.clientX < rect.left ||
            event.clientX > rect.right ||
            event.clientY < rect.top ||
            event.clientY > rect.bottom
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        const sx = canvas.width / rect.width;
        const sy = canvas.height / rect.height;

        return {
            x: clamp(
                Math.floor(
                    (event.clientX - rect.left) * sx
                ),
                0,
                canvas.width - 1
            ),

            y: clamp(
                Math.floor(
                    (event.clientY - rect.top) * sy
                ),
                0,
                canvas.height - 1
            )
        };
    }

    function normalizeArea(a, b) {
        return {
            x1: Math.min(a.x, b.x),
            y1: Math.min(a.y, b.y),
            x2: Math.max(a.x, b.x),
            y2: Math.max(a.y, b.y)
        };
    }

    function areaSize(area) {
        if (!area) {
            return {
                width: 0,
                height: 0,
                pixels: 0
            };
        }

        const width =
            area.x2 - area.x1 + 1;

        const height =
            area.y2 - area.y1 + 1;

        return {
            width,
            height,
            pixels: width * height
        };
    }

    // =========================================================
    // OVERLAY & ZOOM-LOCK AREA RENDER ENGINE
    // =========================================================

    function getAreaLayer() {
        let layer = document.getElementById('w3x-area-layer');
        const container = document.getElementById('painting-move') || (getCanvas() && getCanvas().parentElement) || document.body;
        if (!layer) {
            layer = document.createElement('div');
            layer.id = 'w3x-area-layer';
            container.appendChild(layer);
        } else if (layer.parentElement !== container) {
            container.appendChild(layer);
        }
        return layer;
    }

    function createOverlay() {
        getAreaLayer();

        let shield = $('#w3x-select-shield');
        if (!shield) {
            shield = document.createElement('div');
            shield.id = 'w3x-select-shield';
            document.body.appendChild(shield);

            shield.addEventListener('mousedown', areaMouseDown, true);
            shield.addEventListener('mousemove', areaMouseMove, true);
            shield.addEventListener('mouseup', areaMouseUp, true);
            shield.addEventListener('mouseleave', areaMouseLeave, true);

            shield.addEventListener('contextmenu', event => {
                event.preventDefault();
                event.stopPropagation();
            }, true);

            shield.addEventListener('click', handleShieldClick, true);

            // Zoom pass-through on mouse wheel so wheel zoom works smoothly while selecting!
            shield.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
                const canvas = getCanvas();
                if (canvas) {
                    canvas.dispatchEvent(new WheelEvent('wheel', {
                        deltaX: e.deltaX,
                        deltaY: e.deltaY,
                        deltaZ: e.deltaZ,
                        deltaMode: e.deltaMode,
                        clientX: e.clientX,
                        clientY: e.clientY,
                        screenX: e.screenX,
                        screenY: e.screenY,
                        bubbles: true,
                        cancelable: true
                    }));
                }
                if (state.areaSelecting && state.areaStart) {
                    requestAnimationFrame(drawPreview);
                }
            }, { passive: true });
        }

        let help = $('#w3x-select-help');
        if (!help) {
            help = document.createElement('div');
            help.id = 'w3x-select-help';
            help.textContent = 'Click + drag to select area — ESC to cancel';
            document.body.appendChild(help);
        }

        let textPreviewBox = $('#w3x-text-preview-box');
        if (!textPreviewBox) {
            textPreviewBox = document.createElement('div');
            textPreviewBox.id = 'w3x-text-preview-box';
            document.body.appendChild(textPreviewBox);
        }

        let imagePreviewBox = $('#w3x-image-preview-box');
        if (!imagePreviewBox) {
            imagePreviewBox = document.createElement('div');
            imagePreviewBox.id = 'w3x-image-preview-box';
            document.body.appendChild(imagePreviewBox);
        }

        document.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
            if (event.key === 'Escape') {
                if (state.areaSelecting) cancelAreaSelection();
                if (state.prayt && state.prayt.radiusAttackClicking && typeof praytToggleClickAttack === 'function') praytToggleClickAttack(false);
                if (state.prayt && state.prayt.voidModeClicking && typeof praytToggleVoidMode === 'function') praytToggleVoidMode(false);
                if (state.prayt && state.prayt.seaPicking && typeof beginSeaColorPick === 'function') beginSeaColorPick();
            }
        }, true);
    }

    // =========================================================
    // AREA SELECTION
    // =========================================================

    function beginAreaSelection() {
        if (state.areaSelecting) return;
        state.areaSelecting = true;
        state.areaStart = null;
        state.areaEnd = null;

        const shield = $('#w3x-select-shield');
        const help = $('#w3x-select-help');
        if (shield) shield.classList.add('active');
        if (help) help.classList.add('active');
        setStatus('SELECTING');
        document.body.style.cursor = 'crosshair';
    }

    function areaMouseDown(event) {
        if (!state.areaSelecting || event.button !== 0) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
        const point = getCanvasPoint(event);
        if (!point) return;
        state.areaStart = point;
        state.areaEnd = point;
        drawPreview();
    }

    function areaMouseMove(event) {
        if (!state.areaSelecting || !state.areaStart) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
        const point = getCanvasPoint(event);
        if (!point) return;
        state.areaEnd = point;
        drawPreview();
    }

    function areaMouseUp(event) {
        if (!state.areaSelecting || event.button !== 0) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
        if (!state.areaStart || !state.areaEnd) {
            cancelAreaSelection();
            return;
        }
        state.savedArea = normalizeArea(state.areaStart, state.areaEnd);
        if (state.prayt) {
            state.prayt.areaTargetMap = null;
        }
        finishAreaSelection();
        updateAreaInfo();
        renderSavedArea();

        const isMxoWanted = !!(
            (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined' && praytGodDefense.active) ||
            state.bot.protect ||
            (state.prayt && state.prayt.pendingMxoProtect)
        );
        const isHumanWanted = !!(
            (typeof praytHumanDuo !== 'undefined' && praytHumanDuo.active) ||
            state.bot.humanProtect ||
            (state.prayt && state.prayt.pendingHumanProtect)
        );

        if (state.prayt && state.prayt.pendingFlag) {
            const pf = state.prayt.pendingFlag;
            state.prayt.pendingFlag = null;
            if (pf === 'tr' || pf === 'history') setTimeout(() => praytShowTurkishFlagPreview(true), 40);
            else if (pf === 'tr_history') setTimeout(praytGenerateTurkishHistoryFlag, 40);
            else if (pf === 'tr_official') setTimeout(praytGenerateNormalTrFlag, 40);
            else if (pf === 'turkey_map') setTimeout(praytGenerateTurkeyMap, 40);
            else if (pf === 'lgbt') setTimeout(praytGenerateLgbtFlag, 40);
            else if (pf === 'az') setTimeout(praytGenerateAzerbaijanFlag, 40);
            else if (pf === 'kktc') setTimeout(praytGenerateKktcFlag, 40);
            else if (pf === 'canada') setTimeout(praytGenerateCanadaFlag, 40);
            else if (pf === 'heart') setTimeout(praytGenerateHeartSymbol, 40);
            else if (pf === 'star') setTimeout(praytGenerateStarEmblem, 40);
        } else if (state.prayt && state.prayt.pendingClock) {
            state.prayt.pendingClock = false;
            setTimeout(praytStartLiveClock, 40);
        } else if (isMxoWanted) {
            if (state.prayt) state.prayt.pendingMxoProtect = false;
            state.bot.protect = true;
            state.bot.humanProtect = false;
            if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') {
                praytGodDefense.active = true;
                state.prayt.godDefense = true;
            }
            captureCanvasAreaToTargetMap(state.savedArea);
            ensureAreaTargetMap();
            if (typeof praytMxoEngine !== 'undefined') {
                praytMxoEngine.markAreaAsFullyDrawn();
                praytMxoEngine.start();
            }
            if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') {
                praytGodDefense.syncButtons();
                praytGodDefense.updateUI();
            }
            if (typeof _globalSyncAreaProtectUI === 'function') _globalSyncAreaProtectUI();
            updatePraytProtectInfo();
            setStatus('⚡ MXO İMKÂNSIZ SAVUNMA NÖBETTE');
        } else if (isHumanWanted) {
            if (state.prayt) state.prayt.pendingHumanProtect = false;
            praytCaptureAndHumanProtect();
        } else if (state.prayt && state.prayt.flagPreviewActive) {
            setTimeout(() => praytRenderFlagPreview(state.savedArea), 30);
        }
    }

    function areaMouseLeave() {}

    function finishAreaSelection() {
        state.areaSelecting = false;
        state.areaStart = null;
        state.areaEnd = null;

        const shield = $('#w3x-select-shield');
        const help = $('#w3x-select-help');
        if (shield) shield.classList.remove('active');
        if (help) help.classList.remove('active');
        document.body.style.cursor = '';
        removePreview();
        if (typeof praytCloseFlagPreview === 'function') praytCloseFlagPreview();
        setStatus('CONNECTED');
    }

    function cancelAreaSelection() {
        state.areaSelecting = false;
        state.areaStart = null;
        state.areaEnd = null;

        const shield = $('#w3x-select-shield');
        const help = $('#w3x-select-help');
        if (shield) shield.classList.remove('active');
        if (help) help.classList.remove('active');
        document.body.style.cursor = '';
        removePreview();
        setStatus('CONNECTED');
    }

    // =========================================================
    // AREA RENDER (Canvas-Relative 1:1 Pixel Coordinates)
    // =========================================================

    function canvasToScreen(x, y) {
        const canvas = getCanvas();
        if (!canvas) return null;
        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        const sx = rect.width / canvas.width;
        const sy = rect.height / canvas.height;
        return {
            x: rect.left + (x + 0.5) * sx,
            y: rect.top + (y + 0.5) * sy
        };
    }

    function getAreaScreenRect(area) {
        const canvas = getCanvas();
        if (!canvas || !area) return null;
        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        const sx = rect.width / canvas.width;
        const sy = rect.height / canvas.height;
        return {
            left: rect.left + area.x1 * sx,
            top: rect.top + area.y1 * sy,
            width: (area.x2 - area.x1 + 1) * sx,
            height: (area.y2 - area.y1 + 1) * sy
        };
    }

    function createMarker(id, preview) {
        const layer = getAreaLayer();
        if (!layer) return null;
        let marker = document.getElementById(id);
        if (marker) return marker;
        marker = document.createElement('div');
        marker.id = id;
        if (preview) marker.id = 'w3x-area-preview';
        layer.appendChild(marker);
        return marker;
    }

    function positionMarker(marker, area) {
        if (!marker || !area) return;
        // Position directly in canvas coordinates inside #painting-move!
        // Browser natively scales it with the canvas during mouse wheel zoom!
        marker.style.position = 'absolute';
        marker.style.left = `${area.x1}px`;
        marker.style.top = `${area.y1}px`;
        marker.style.width = `${Math.max(1, area.x2 - area.x1 + 1)}px`;
        marker.style.height = `${Math.max(1, area.y2 - area.y1 + 1)}px`;
        marker.style.display = 'block';
        marker.style.visibility = 'visible';
        marker.style.opacity = '1';
    }

    function drawPreview() {
        if (!state.areaStart || !state.areaEnd) return;
        const area = normalizeArea(state.areaStart, state.areaEnd);
        const marker = createMarker('w3x-area-preview', true);
        if (!marker) return;
        marker.className = 'w3x-area-marker';
        marker.style.borderStyle = 'dashed';
        marker.style.opacity = '.85';
        const w = Math.max(1, area.x2 - area.x1 + 1);
        const h = Math.max(1, area.y2 - area.y1 + 1);
        const totalPx = w * h;
        marker.setAttribute('data-label', `${w}×${h} (${totalPx.toLocaleString()} px)`);
        positionMarker(marker, area);
        updatePreviewInfo(area);
        if (state.prayt && (state.prayt.flagPreviewActive || state.prayt.pendingFlag === 'tr' || state.prayt.pendingFlag === 'history') && typeof praytScheduleFlagPreview === 'function') {
            praytScheduleFlagPreview(area);
        }
    }

    function removePreview() {
        const preview = $('#w3x-area-preview');
        if (preview) preview.remove();
    }

    function renderSavedArea() {
        const layer = getAreaLayer();
        if (!layer) return;
        layer.querySelectorAll('.w3x-saved-area-marker').forEach(element => element.remove());
        if (!state.savedArea) return;
        const area = state.savedArea;
        const marker = document.createElement('div');
        marker.id = 'w3x-area-marker';
        marker.className = 'w3x-area-marker w3x-saved-area-marker';
        const w = Math.max(1, area.x2 - area.x1 + 1);
        const h = Math.max(1, area.y2 - area.y1 + 1);
        const totalPx = w * h;
        marker.setAttribute('data-label', `${w}×${h} (${totalPx.toLocaleString()} px)`);
        positionMarker(marker, area);
        layer.appendChild(marker);
    }

    // =========================================================
    // AREA INFO
    // =========================================================

    function updatePreviewInfo(area) {

        const size =
            areaSize(area);

        const badge =
            $('#w3x-badge-bot-area-px');

        if (badge) {
            badge.style.display = 'inline-block';
            badge.textContent = `${size.width}×${size.height} (${size.pixels.toLocaleString()} px)`;
        }

        const html =
            `X: <b>${area.x1}</b> → <b>${area.x2}</b><br>` +
            `Y: <b>${area.y1}</b> → <b>${area.y2}</b><br>` +
            `Size: <b>${size.width} × ${size.height}</b>`;

        const info =
            $('#w3x-area-info-bot');

        if (info)
            info.innerHTML =
                html;
    }

    function updateAreaInfo() {
        if (typeof praytSyncDimensionHubUI === 'function') praytSyncDimensionHubUI();

        const info =
            $('#w3x-area-info');

        const botInfo =
            $('#w3x-area-info-bot');

        const badge =
            $('#w3x-badge-bot-area-px');

        if (!state.savedArea) {

            const text =
                'No area selected.';

            if (info)
                info.textContent =
                    text;

            if (botInfo)
                botInfo.textContent =
                    text;

            if (badge) {
                badge.style.display = 'none';
            }

            return;
        }

        const area =
            state.savedArea;

        const size =
            areaSize(area);

        if (badge) {
            badge.style.display = 'inline-block';
            badge.textContent = `${size.width}×${size.height} (${size.pixels.toLocaleString()} px)`;
        }

        const html =
            `X: <b>${area.x1}</b> → <b>${area.x2}</b><br>` +
            `Y: <b>${area.y1}</b> → <b>${area.y2}</b><br>` +
            `Size: <b>${size.width} × ${size.height}</b><br>` +
            `Pixels: <b>${size.pixels.toLocaleString()}</b>`;

        if (info)
            info.innerHTML =
                html;

        if (botInfo)
            botInfo.innerHTML =
                html;
    }

    function clearArea() {

        state.savedArea = null;
        if (state.prayt) state.prayt.areaTargetMap = null;

        const badge = $('#w3x-badge-bot-area-px');
        if (badge) badge.style.display = 'none';

        removePreview();

        document
            .querySelectorAll(
                '.w3x-saved-area-marker'
            )
            .forEach(element =>
                element.remove()
            );

        updateAreaInfo();
        updatePraytProtectInfo();
    }

    // =========================================================
    // GO TO POSITION
    // =========================================================

    function goToPositionFromMenu() {

        const canvas =
            getCanvas();

        if (!canvas) {

            setStatus(
                'CANVAS NOT FOUND'
            );

            return;
        }

        const xInput =
            $('#w3x-x');

        const yInput =
            $('#w3x-y');

        if (!xInput || !yInput) {

            setStatus(
                'INVALID INPUT'
            );

            return;
        }

        const x =
            Number(xInput.value);

        const y =
            Number(yInput.value);

        if (
            !Number.isInteger(x) ||
            !Number.isInteger(y)
        ) {

            setStatus(
                'INVALID X/Y'
            );

            return;
        }

        if (
            x < 0 ||
            y < 0 ||
            x >= canvas.width ||
            y >= canvas.height
        ) {

            setStatus(
                'OUT OF BOUNDS'
            );

            return;
        }

        state.targetPosition = {
            x,
            y
        };

        /*
         * Mevcut URL hash'ini koruyoruz.
         * Eğer site hash üzerinden kamera konumunu
         * yönetiyorsa hedef koordinatı buraya yazıyoruz.
         */
        const params =
            new URLSearchParams(
                location.hash.slice(1)
            );

        params.set(
            'x',
            String(x)
        );

        params.set(
            'y',
            String(y)
        );

        const zoom =
            params.get('s');

        const hash =
            '#' +
            params.toString();

        /*
         * hashchange'in çalışması için
         * location.hash kullanıyoruz.
         */
        if (
            location.hash !== hash
        ) {
            location.hash = hash;
        }

        renderTargetPosition();

        setStatus(
            `TARGET ${x}, ${y}`
        );
    }

    function clearTargetPosition() {

        state.targetPosition = null;

        const marker =
            $('#w3x-target-marker');

        if (marker)
            marker.remove();

        setStatus(
            'CONNECTED'
        );
    }

    // =========================================================
    // TARGET MARKER
    // =========================================================

    function renderTargetPosition() {

        const canvas =
            getCanvas();

        if (
            !canvas ||
            !state.targetPosition
        ) {
            return;
        }

        const {
            x,
            y
        } =
            state.targetPosition;

        const point =
            canvasToScreen(
                x,
                y
            );

        if (!point)
            return;

        let marker =
            document.getElementById(
                'w3x-target-marker'
            );

        if (!marker) {

            marker =
                document.createElement('div');

            marker.id =
                'w3x-target-marker';

            document.body.appendChild(
                marker
            );
        }

        if (marker._lastX !== point.x || marker._lastY !== point.y) {
            marker._lastX = point.x;
            marker._lastY = point.y;
            marker.style.left =
                `${point.x}px`;
            marker.style.top =
                `${point.y}px`;
        }

        if (marker.style.display !== 'block') {
            marker.style.display =
                'block';
        }
    }

    // =========================================================
    // TABS
    // =========================================================

    function setupTabs() {

        document
            .querySelectorAll(
                '.w3x-tab'
            )
            .forEach(tab => {

                tab.addEventListener(
                    'click',
                    event => {

                        event.preventDefault();

                        event.stopPropagation();

                        const page =
                            tab.dataset.page;

                        if (!page)
                            return;

                        state.activePage =
                            page;

                        document
                            .querySelectorAll(
                                '.w3x-tab'
                            )
                            .forEach(item => {

                                item.classList.toggle(
                                    'active',
                                    item === tab
                                );

                            });

                        document
                            .querySelectorAll(
                                '.w3x-page'
                            )
                            .forEach(section => {

                                section.classList.toggle(
                                    'active',

                                    section.dataset
                                        .pageContent ===
                                    page
                                );

                            });
                    }
                );
            });
    }

    // =========================================================
    // PANEL DRAG
    // =========================================================

    function setupDragging() {

        const panel =
            $('#w3x-dotter');

        const header =
            $('#w3x-header');

        if (!panel || !header)
            return;

        let dragging = false;

        let offsetX = 0;

        let offsetY = 0;

        header.addEventListener(
            'mousedown',
            event => {

                if (
                    event.target.closest('button') ||
                    event.target.closest('input')
                ) {
                    return;
                }

                dragging = true;

                const rect =
                    panel.getBoundingClientRect();

                offsetX =
                    event.clientX -
                    rect.left;

                offsetY =
                    event.clientY -
                    rect.top;

                panel.style.left =
                    `${rect.left}px`;

                panel.style.top =
                    `${rect.top}px`;

                event.preventDefault();
            }
        );

        document.addEventListener(
            'mousemove',
            event => {

                if (!dragging)
                    return;

                let x =
                    event.clientX -
                    offsetX;

                let y =
                    event.clientY -
                    offsetY;

                x = clamp(
                    x,
                    0,
                    innerWidth -
                    panel.offsetWidth
                );

                y = clamp(
                    y,
                    0,
                    innerHeight -
                    52
                );

                panel.style.left =
                    `${x}px`;

                panel.style.top =
                    `${y}px`;
            }
        );

        document.addEventListener(
            'mouseup',
            () => {

                if (!dragging)
                    return;

                dragging = false;

                savePosition();
            }
        );
    }

    function savePosition() {

        const panel =
            $('#w3x-dotter');

        if (!panel)
            return;

        localStorage.setItem(
            'w3x-v42-position',
            JSON.stringify({
                left:
                    panel.offsetLeft,

                top:
                    panel.offsetTop
            })
        );
    }

    function loadPosition() {
        const panel = $('#w3x-dotter');
        if (!panel) return;
        try {
            const raw = localStorage.getItem('w3x-v42-position');
            if (!raw) {
                panel.style.left = '24px';
                panel.style.top = '24px';
                return;
            }
            const pos = JSON.parse(raw);
            if (typeof pos.left !== 'number' || typeof pos.top !== 'number') {
                panel.style.left = '24px';
                panel.style.top = '24px';
                return;
            }
            panel.style.left = `${clamp(pos.left, 10, Math.max(10, window.innerWidth - 350))}px`;
            panel.style.top = `${clamp(pos.top, 10, Math.max(10, window.innerHeight - 200))}px`;
        } catch {
            panel.style.left = '24px';
            panel.style.top = '24px';
        }
    }

    // =========================================================
    // MINIMIZE
    // =========================================================

    function setupMinimize() {

        const panel =
            $('#w3x-dotter');

        const button =
            $('#w3x-minimize');

        if (!panel || !button)
            return;

        button.addEventListener(
            'click',
            event => {

                event.preventDefault();

                event.stopPropagation();

                state.minimized =
                    !state.minimized;

                panel.classList.toggle(
                    'w3x-minimized',
                    state.minimized
                );

                button.textContent =
                    state.minimized
                        ? '+'
                        : '−';
            }
        );
    }

            $('#w3x-text-space-val');

        if (textSpace) {

            textSpace.value =
                state.text.spacing;

            textSpace.oninput =

                    textSpaceVal.textContent =

                }

        const textScale =

        const textScaleVal =
            $('#w3x-text-scale-val');

        if (textScale) {

            textScale.value =
                state.text.scale;

            textScale.oninput =
                () => {

                state.text.scale =
                    parseInt(
                        textScale.value,
                        10
                    );

                if (textScaleVal) {
                    textScaleVal.textContent =
                        `SIZE: ${state.text.scale}x`;
                }

        const textOutline =
            $('#w3x-text-outline');

        if (textOutline) {

            textOutline.onclick =
                () => {

                state.text.outline =
                    !state.text.outline;

                textOutline.textContent =
                    `OUTLINE: ${
                        state.text.outline
                            ? 'ON'
                            : 'OFF'
                    }`;

                textOutline.classList.toggle(
                    'outlinen',
                    state.text.outline
                );

                updateTextPreview();
                updateTextInfo();
            };
        }

        const imageFile =
            $('#w3x-image-file');

        const imageScale =
            $('#w3x-image-scale');

        const imageScaleVal =
            $('#w3x-image-scale-val');

        const imageOrigin =
            $('#w3x-image-origin');

        const imageStart =
            $('#w3x-image-start');

        const imageClear =
            $('#w3x-image-clear');

        if (imageFile) {
            imageFile.addEventListener(
                'change',
                () => {

                loadImageFile(
                    imageFile.files[0]
                );
            });
        }

        if (imageScale) {

            imageScale.value =
                state.image.scale;

            imageScale.oninput =
                () => {

                state.image.scale =
                    parseInt(
                        imageScale.value,
                        10
                    ) || 1;

                if (imageScaleVal) {
                    imageScaleVal.textContent =
                        `SIZE: ${state.image.scale}x`;
                }

                updateImagePreview();
                updateImageInfo();
            };
        }

        if (imageOrigin) {
            imageOrigin.onclick =
                beginImageOriginPick;
        }

        if (imageStart) {
            imageStart.onclick =
                drawImage;
        }

        if (imageClear) {
            imageClear.onclick =
                clearImage;
        }

        const imagePreviewToggle =
            $('#w3x-image-preview-toggle');

        if (imagePreviewToggle) {

            imagePreviewToggle.onclick =
                () => {

                state.image.previewOn =
                    !state.image.previewOn;

                imagePreviewToggle.textContent =
                    `PREVIEW: ${
                        state.image.previewOn
                            ? 'ON'
                            : 'OFF'
                    }`;

                imagePreviewToggle.classList.toggle(
                    'outlinen',
                    state.image.previewOn
                );

                if (!state.image.previewOn) {
                    hideImagePreview();
                } else {
                    updateImagePreview();
                }
            };
        }

        const wakeToggle =
            $('#w3x-wake-toggle');

        if (wakeToggle) {

            wakeToggle.onclick =
                () => {

                state.wake.on =
                    !state.wake.on;

                wakeToggle.textContent =
                    `WAKE: ${
                        state.wake.on
                            ? 'ON'
                            : 'OFF'
                    }`;

                wakeToggle.classList.toggle(
                    'outlinen',
                    state.wake.on
                );
            };
        }

        // === PraytClient Controls ===

        const syncImageProtectToggles = () => {
            const isMxoOn = !!(state.prayt.realtimeProtect || state.bot.protect || (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined' && praytGodDefense.active));
            const txt = `⚡ MXO 0MS KORUMA: ${isMxoOn ? "AÇIK (KAPAT)" : "OFF"}`;
            const p1 = $('#w3x-prayt-protect-toggle');
            const p2 = $('#w3x-image-mxo-protect');
            if (p1) { p1.textContent = txt; p1.classList.toggle("protecting", isMxoOn); }
            if (p2) { p2.textContent = `MXO PROTECT: ${isMxoOn ? "ON" : "OFF"}`; p2.classList.toggle("protecting", isMxoOn); }
            if (typeof _globalSyncAreaProtectUI === 'function') _globalSyncAreaProtectUI();
        };

        const toggleImageProtect = () => {
            const currentlyOn = !!(state.prayt.realtimeProtect || state.bot.protect || (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined' && praytGodDefense.active));
            if (currentlyOn) {
                state.prayt.realtimeProtect = false;
                if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') praytGodDefense.toggle(false);
                syncImageProtectToggles();
                return;
            }
            if (!state.image.hasImage || !state.image.origin) {
                if (state.savedArea && typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') {
                    praytGodDefense.toggle(true);
                    syncImageProtectToggles();
                    return;
                }
                setStatus("ÖNCE ALAN VEYA RESİM SEÇİN!");
                state.prayt.realtimeProtect = false;
                syncImageProtectToggles();
                return;
            }
            state.prayt.realtimeProtect = true;
            state.bot.protect = true;
            if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') {
                praytGodDefense.active = true;
                state.prayt.godDefense = true;
            }
            const imgW = state.image.w, imgH = state.image.h, imgData = state.image.data;
            const scale = state.image.scale || 1;
            const ox = state.image.origin.x, oy = state.image.origin.y;
            const mapW = imgW * scale;
            const mapH = imgH * scale;
            const palette = getPaletteColors();
            const targetData = new Int16Array(mapW * mapH);
            for (let sy = 0; sy < imgH; sy++) {
                for (let sx = 0; sx < imgW; sx++) {
                    const idx = (sy * imgW + sx) * 4;
                    const a = imgData[idx + 3];
                    const cid = (a >= 128) ? nearestPaletteId(imgData[idx], imgData[idx+1], imgData[idx+2], palette) : -1;
                    for (let dy = 0; dy < scale; dy++) {
                        for (let dx = 0; dx < scale; dx++) {
                            const mx = sx * scale + dx;
                            const my = sy * scale + dy;
                            const ax = ox + mx;
                            const ay = oy + my;
                            if (state.prayt.seaSkip && (isCanvasSeaPixel(ax, ay) || (cid !== null && state.prayt.seaColors.has(cid)) || isSeaRGB(imgData[idx], imgData[idx+1], imgData[idx+2]))) {
                                targetData[my * mapW + mx] = -1;
                            } else {
                                targetData[my * mapW + mx] = (cid !== null) ? cid : -1;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            state.prayt.areaTargetMap = {
                x1: ox,
                y1: oy,
                x2: ox + mapW - 1,
                y2: oy + mapH - 1,
                w: mapW,
                h: mapH,
                data: targetData,
                type: 'image'
            };
            state.savedArea = {
                x1: ox,
                y1: oy,
                x2: ox + mapW - 1,
                y2: oy + mapH - 1
            };
            state.prayt.protectImage = { w: imgW, h: imgH, data: imgData };
            state.prayt.protectOrigin = { x: ox, y: oy };
            updatePraytProtectInfo();
            scanAndQueueDamagedImagePixels();
            praytMxoEngine.start();
            setStatus("⚡ MXO IMG PROTECT AKTİF");
            if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') praytGodDefense.syncButtons();
            syncImageProtectToggles();
        };

        const praytProtectToggle = $('#w3x-prayt-protect-toggle');
        if (praytProtectToggle) praytProtectToggle.onclick = () => praytGodDefense.toggle();

        const praytHumanProtectToggle = $('#w3x-prayt-human-protect-toggle');
        if (praytHumanProtectToggle) {
            praytHumanProtectToggle.onclick = () => {
                state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect = !state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect;
                if (state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect) {
                    if (!state.image.hasImage || !state.image.origin) {
                        setStatus("RESİM/ORIGIN YOK!");
                        state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect = false;
                        praytHumanProtectToggle.textContent = "🛡️ İNSANSI KORUMA: OFF";
                        praytHumanProtectToggle.classList.remove("protecting");
                        return;
                    }
                    state.prayt.protectImage = { w: state.image.w, h: state.image.h, data: state.image.data };
                    state.prayt.protectOrigin = { x: state.image.origin.x, y: state.image.origin.y };
                    updatePraytProtectInfo();
                    scanAndQueueDamagedImagePixels(true);
                    praytMxoEngine.ensureRunning();
                    praytHumanProtectToggle.textContent = "🛡️ İNSANSI KORUMA: ON";
                    praytHumanProtectToggle.classList.add("protecting");
                    setStatus("🛡️ İNSANSI RESİM KORUMA AKTİF");
                } else {
                    praytHumanProtectToggle.textContent = "🛡️ İNSANSI KORUMA: OFF";
                    praytHumanProtectToggle.classList.remove("protecting");
                    setStatus("İNSANSI KORUMA OFF");
                }
            };
        }

        const imageMxoProtectBtn = $('#w3x-image-mxo-protect');
        if (imageMxoProtectBtn) imageMxoProtectBtn.onclick = toggleImageProtect;

        const syncSeaToggles = () => {
            const txt = `SEA SKIP: ${state.prayt.seaSkip ? "ON" : "OFF"}`;
            const t1 = $('#w3x-prayt-sea-toggle');
            const t2 = $('#w3x-bot-sea-skip-toggle');
            if (t1) { t1.textContent = txt; t1.classList.toggle("protecting", state.prayt.seaSkip); }
            if (t2) { t2.textContent = txt; t2.classList.toggle("protecting", state.prayt.seaSkip); }
        };

        const seaToggle = $('#w3x-prayt-sea-toggle');
        if (seaToggle) seaToggle.onclick = () => { state.prayt.seaSkip = !state.prayt.seaSkip; syncSeaToggles(); };

        const seaToggleQuick = $('#w3x-bot-sea-skip-toggle');
        if (seaToggleQuick) {
            seaToggleQuick.onclick = () => {
                state.prayt.seaSkip = !state.prayt.seaSkip;
                syncSeaToggles();
                if (state.prayt.seaSkip && state.prayt.seaColors.size === 0 && (!state.prayt.seaRGBs || state.prayt.seaRGBs.length === 0)) {
                    setStatus('🌊 ATLANACAK RENGİ SEÇİN (🎯 HARİTADAN SEÇ)');
                }
            };
        }

        const seaPickQuick = $('#w3x-bot-sea-pick-quick');
        if (seaPickQuick) seaPickQuick.onclick = beginSeaColorPick;

        const seaPickBtn = $('#w3x-prayt-sea-pick');
        if (seaPickBtn) seaPickBtn.onclick = beginSeaColorPick;

        const seaAdd = $('#w3x-prayt-sea-add');
        if (seaAdd) {
            seaAdd.onclick = () => {
                const colorId = getSelectedColorId();
                if (colorId !== null) {
                    state.prayt.seaColors.add(colorId);
                    const col = getPaletteColorById(colorId);
                    if (col) {
                        const hex = rgbToHex(col.r, col.g, col.b);
                        state.prayt.seaColors.add(hex);
                        if (!state.prayt.seaRGBs.some(c => c.hex === hex)) {
                            state.prayt.seaRGBs.push({ r: col.r, g: col.g, b: col.b, hex });
                        }
                    }
                    state.prayt.seaSkip = true;
                    syncSeaToggles();
                    updateSeaInfo();
                    setStatus(`🌊 ATLANACAK RENK EKLENDİ: #${colorId}`);
                } else {
                    setStatus('ÖNCE PALETTEN BİR RENK SEÇİN');
                }
            };
        }

        const seaInput = $('#w3x-prayt-sea-id-input');
        const seaAddId = $('#w3x-prayt-sea-id-add');
        if (seaAddId && seaInput) {
            seaAddId.onclick = () => {
                const val = seaInput.value.trim();
                if (/^#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}$/.test(val)) {
                    const r = parseInt(val.slice(1, 3), 16);
                    const g = parseInt(val.slice(3, 5), 16);
                    const b = parseInt(val.slice(5, 7), 16);
                    const hex = val.toUpperCase();
                    state.prayt.seaRGBs.push({ r, g, b, hex });
                    state.prayt.seaColors.add(hex);
                    const palette = getPaletteColors();
                    const colorId = nearestPaletteId(r, g, b, palette);
                    if (colorId !== null) state.prayt.seaColors.add(colorId);
                    state.prayt.seaSkip = true;
                    syncSeaToggles();
                    updateSeaInfo();
                    setStatus(`🌊 ATLANACAK RENK EKLENDİ: ${hex}`);
                } else {
                    const id = parseInt(val, 10);
                    if (!isNaN(id)) {
                        state.prayt.seaColors.add(id);
                        const col = getPaletteColorById(id);
                        if (col) {
                            const hex = rgbToHex(col.r, col.g, col.b);
                            state.prayt.seaColors.add(hex);
                            if (!state.prayt.seaRGBs.some(c => c.hex === hex)) {
                                state.prayt.seaRGBs.push({ r: col.r, g: col.g, b: col.b, hex });
                            }
                        }
                        state.prayt.seaSkip = true;
                        syncSeaToggles();
                        updateSeaInfo();
                        setStatus(`🌊 ATLANACAK RENK ID EKLENDİ: #${id}`);
                    }
                }
                seaInput.value = "";
            };
        }

        const seaClear = $('#w3x-prayt-sea-clear');
        if (seaClear) {
            seaClear.onclick = () => {
                state.prayt.seaColors.clear();
                state.prayt.seaRGBs = [];
                updateSeaInfo();
                setStatus('🌊 ATLANACAK RENKLER TEMİZLENDİ');
            };
        }

        const imageMaxDimSlider = $('#w3x-image-maxdim');
        const imageMaxDimVal = $('#w3x-image-maxdim-val');
        if (imageMaxDimSlider) {
            imageMaxDimSlider.oninput = () => {
                state.prayt.maxImageSize = parseInt(imageMaxDimSlider.value, 10);
                if (imageMaxDimVal) imageMaxDimVal.textContent = `BOYUT: ${state.prayt.maxImageSize}px`;
                applyImageDimensions();
            };
        }

        document.querySelectorAll('.w3x-dim-preset').forEach(btn => {
            btn.onclick = () => {
                const dim = parseInt(btn.dataset.dim, 10);
                if (!isNaN(dim)) {
                    state.prayt.maxImageSize = dim;
                    if (imageMaxDimSlider) imageMaxDimSlider.value = dim;
                    if (imageMaxDimVal) imageMaxDimVal.textContent = `BOYUT: ${dim}px`;
                    applyImageDimensions();
                }
            };
        });

        const ditherToggle = $('#w3x-prayt-dither-toggle');
        if (ditherToggle) {
            ditherToggle.onclick = () => {
                state.prayt.dither = !state.prayt.dither;
                ditherToggle.textContent = `DITHER: ${state.prayt.dither ? "ON" : "OFF"}`;
                ditherToggle.classList.toggle("protecting", state.prayt.dither);
            };

        document.querySelectorAll('.w3x-strategy').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                const strat = btn.dataset.strategy;
                document.querySelectorAll('.w3x-strategy').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b === btn));
                document.querySelectorAll('.w3x-mode').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.mode === strat));
                state.prayt.drawStrategy = strat;
                state.bot.mode = strat;
            });
        });

        const ignoreAdd = $('#w3x-prayt-ignore-add');
        if (ignoreAdd) {
            ignoreAdd.onclick = () => {
                const colorId = getSelectedColorId();
                if (colorId !== null) {
                    state.prayt.ignoreColors.add(colorId);
                    updateIgnoreInfo();
                }
            };
        }

        const ignoreClear = $('#w3x-prayt-ignore-clear');
        if (ignoreClear) {
            ignoreClear.onclick = () => {
                state.prayt.ignoreColors.clear();
                updateIgnoreInfo();
            };
        }

        const nudgeStep = $('#w3x-prayt-nudge-step');
        const nudgeVal = $('#w3x-prayt-nudge-val');
        if (nudgeStep) {
            nudgeStep.oninput = () => {
                state.prayt.nudgeStep = parseInt(nudgeStep.value, 10);
                if (nudgeVal) nudgeVal.textContent = `STEP: ${state.prayt.nudgeStep}`;
            };
        }

        const nudgeUp = $('#w3x-prayt-nudge-up');
        const nudgeDown = $('#w3x-prayt-nudge-down');
        const nudgeLeft = $('#w3x-prayt-nudge-left');
        const nudgeRight = $('#w3x-prayt-nudge-right');
        if (nudgeUp) nudgeUp.onclick = () => praytNudge(0, -1);
        if (nudgeDown) nudgeDown.onclick = () => praytNudge(0, 1);
        if (nudgeLeft) nudgeLeft.onclick = () => praytNudge(-1, 0);
        if (nudgeRight) nudgeRight.onclick = () => praytNudge(1, 0);

        const clipboardToggle = $('#w3x-prayt-clipboard-toggle');
        if (clipboardToggle) {
            clipboardToggle.onclick = () => {
                state.prayt.clipboard = !state.prayt.clipboard;
                clipboardToggle.textContent = `CLIPBOARD: ${state.prayt.clipboard ? "ON" : "OFF"}`;
                clipboardToggle.classList.toggle("protecting", state.prayt.clipboard);
                if (state.prayt.clipboard) setupClipboardPaste();
            };
        }

        // === PRAYTCLIENT GOD MODE V9.0 CONTROL WIRING ===
        // 0. İmkânsız Savunma Buttons Wiring
        const btnGodToggle1 = $('#w3x-god-protect-toggle');
        if (btnGodToggle1) btnGodToggle1.onclick = () => praytGodDefense.toggle();
        const btnGodToggle2 = $('#w3x-prayt-god-protect-toggle');
        if (btnGodToggle2) btnGodToggle2.onclick = () => praytGodDefense.toggle();
    

        // 1. Top Buttons Wiring
        const btnFlagHistTop = $('#w3x-flag-history-top');
        if (btnFlagHistTop) btnFlagHistTop.onclick = () => { praytAudio.playClick(); praytGenerateTurkishHistoryFlag(); };

        const btnFlagTrCleanTop = $('#w3x-flag-tr-top');
        if (btnFlagTrCleanTop) btnFlagTrCleanTop.onclick = () => { praytAudio.playClick(); praytGenerateNormalTrFlag(); };
        const btnFlagTurkeyMapTop2 = $('#w3x-flag-turkey-map-top');
        if (btnFlagTurkeyMapTop2) btnFlagTurkeyMapTop2.onclick = () => { praytAudio.playClick(); praytGenerateTurkeyMap(); };

        const btnTopSelectArea = $('#w3x-top-select-area');
        if (btnTopSelectArea) {
            btnTopSelectArea.onclick = () => {
                praytAudio.playClick();
                beginAreaSelection();
            };
        }

        document.querySelectorAll('.prayt-speed-preset').forEach(btn => {
            btn.onclick = () => {
                const spd = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-speed'), 10);
                if (!isNaN(spd)) {
                    state.bot.speed = spd;
                    if (botSpeed) botSpeed.value = spd;
                    if (botSpeedVal) botSpeedVal.textContent = spd + 'ms';
                    const rSpeedSlider = $('#w3x-radius-speed-slider');
                    const rSpeedVal = $('#w3x-radius-speed-val');
                    if (rSpeedSlider) rSpeedSlider.value = spd;
                    if (rSpeedVal) rSpeedVal.textContent = spd + ' ms';
                    document.querySelectorAll('.prayt-speed-preset').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b === btn));
                    setStatus('⚡ HIZ: ' + spd + 'ms');
                }
            };
        });

        const btnProtectOff = $('#w3x-top-protect-off');
        if (btnProtectOff) {
            function updateProtectOffBtn() {
                const off = !!(state.prayt && state.prayt.noProtectWhileDrawing);
                if (off) {
                    btnProtectOff.textContent = '🚫 ÇİZİMDE KORUMA: KAPALI';
                    btnProtectOff.style.background = 'rgba(255,50,50,0.32)';
                    btnProtectOff.style.color = '#ff5252';
                    btnProtectOff.style.border = '1px solid #ff5252';
                } else {
                    btnProtectOff.textContent = '⚖️ DENGELİ KORUMA: AÇIK';
                    btnProtectOff.style.background = 'rgba(0,230,118,0.18)';
                    btnProtectOff.style.color = '#00e676';
                    btnProtectOff.style.border = '1px solid #00e676';
                }
            }
            updateProtectOffBtn();
            btnProtectOff.onclick = () => {
                praytAudio.playClick();
                if (!state.prayt) return;
                state.prayt.noProtectWhileDrawing = !state.prayt.noProtectWhileDrawing;
                updateProtectOffBtn();
                const msg = state.prayt.noProtectWhileDrawing
                    ? '🚫 Çizimde Koruma KAPALI (Sadece çizer, bitince korur)'
                    : '⚖️ Dengeli Çizim-Koruma AÇIK (Çizilen yeri saldırı gücüne göre dengeli korur)';
                setStatus(msg);
                if (typeof praytShowToast === 'function') praytShowToast(msg, state.prayt.noProtectWhileDrawing ? 'warn' : 'neon');
            };
        }

                    if (praytAltSocket.connected) {

                        const tokenVal = tokenInp ? tokenInp.value.trim() : null;
                        praytAltSocket.connect(tokenVal || null);

            };

        // 2. Sanat Page Controls & Dimensions Wiring

        const btnSelectOff = $('#w3x-btn-select-official-flag');

        if (btnSelectHist) {

                btnSelectHist.style.background = 'linear-gradient(135deg, #b71c1c, #4a0007)';
                btnSelectHist.style.borderColor = '#ff4d5a';
                btnSelectHist.style.color = '#fff';

                if (btnSelectOff) {
                    btnSelectOff.classList.remove('active');

                    btnSelectOff.style.borderColor = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)';

                if (state.savedArea) praytRenderFlagPreview(state.savedArea);

        if (btnSelectOff) {
            btnSelectOff.onclick = () => {
                state.prayt.currentArtTemplate = 'tr_official';
