                [18, '#CE2939'], [19, '#9F0000'], [20, '#6B0000'], [21, '#A06A42'], [22, '#633C1F'], [23, '#99530D'],
                [24, '#FFDFCC'], [25, '#FFA7D1'], [26, '#CF6EE4'], [27, '#EC08EC'], [28, '#820080'], [29, '#5100FF'],
                [30, '#020763'], [31, '#0000EA'], [32, '#044BFF'], [33, '#6583CF'], [34, '#36BAFF'], [35, '#0083C7'],
                [36, '#00D3DD'], [37, '#45FFC8'], [38, '#B5E8EE']
            ];
            for (const [id, hex] of fallbackHex) {
                const num = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16);
                const r = (num >> 16) & 255, g = (num >> 8) & 255, b = num & 255;
                const lab = srgbToLab(r, g, b);
                palette.push({ r, g, b, id, L: lab[0], a: lab[1], labB: lab[2] });
            }
        }

        _praytCachedPaletteMap = new Map();
        _praytCachedPaletteObjMap = new Map();
        for (let i = 0; i < palette.length; i++) {
            const c = palette[i];
            _praytCachedPaletteMap.set(c.id, (c.r << 16) | (c.g << 8) | c.b);
            _praytCachedPaletteObjMap.set(c.id, c);
        }

        _praytCachedPalette = palette;
        _praytCachedPaletteTs = now;
        return palette;
    }

    function getPaletteRgbById(id) {
        if (id === null || id === undefined || id < 0) return null;
        getPaletteColors();
        return _praytCachedPaletteMap ? _praytCachedPaletteMap.get(id) : null;
    }

    function getPaletteColorById(id) {
        if (id === null || id === undefined || id < 0) return null;
        getPaletteColors();
        return _praytCachedPaletteObjMap ? _praytCachedPaletteObjMap.get(id) : null;
    }

    // sRGB (0-255) -> CIE Lab
    // İnsan gözünün renk algısına en yakın dönüşüm.
    // D65 beyaz nokta referans.

    function srgbToLab(r, g, b) {

        const rn = r / 255;
        const gn = g / 255;
        const bn = b / 255;

        // 1) sRGB -> linear RGB (gamma düzeltme)

        const lin = (c) =>
            c <= 0.04045
                ? c / 12.92
                : Math.pow(
                    (c + 0.055) / 1.055,
                    2.4
                );

        const R = lin(rn);
        const G = lin(gn);
        const B = lin(bn);

        // 2) linear RGB -> XYZ (D65)

        const X =
            R * 0.4124 + G * 0.3576 + B * 0.1805;

        const Y =
            R * 0.2126 + G * 0.7152 + B * 0.0722;

        const Z =
            R * 0.0193 + G * 0.1192 + B * 0.9505;

        // 3) XYZ -> Lab (D65 beyaz nokta)

        const Xn = 0.95047;
        const Yn = 1.0;
        const Zn = 1.08883;

        const f = (t) =>
            t > 0.008856
                ? Math.cbrt(t)
                : 7.787 * t + 16 / 116;

        const fx = f(X / Xn);
        const fy = f(Y / Yn);
        const fz = f(Z / Zn);

        const L = 116 * fy - 16;
        const a = 500 * (fx - fy);
        const bb = 200 * (fy - fz);

        return [L, a, bb];
    }

    const _praytNearestRgbCache = new Map();

    function nearestPaletteId(r, g, b, palette) {
        const rgbKey = ((r & 255) << 16) | ((g & 255) << 8) | (b & 255);
        const cached = _praytNearestRgbCache.get(rgbKey);
        if (cached !== undefined) return cached;

        // Lab uzayında Euclidean mesafeyle en yakın rengi bul.
        // Lab, insan algısındaki renk farkını en iyi
        // modelleyen uzay olduğu için daha doğru eşler.

        const lab =
            srgbToLab(r, g, b);

        const L = lab[0];
        const a = lab[1];
        const bb = lab[2];

        let bestId = null;
        let bestDist = Infinity;

        for (const c of palette) {

            const dL = L - c.L;
            const da = a - c.a;
            const db = bb - (c.labB !== undefined ? c.labB : c.b);

            const dist =
                dL * dL + da * da + db * db;

            if (dist < bestDist) {

                bestDist = dist;
                bestId = c.id;
            }
        }

        _praytNearestRgbCache.set(rgbKey, bestId);
        if (_praytNearestRgbCache.size > 8192) _praytNearestRgbCache.clear();
        return bestId;
    }

    function loadImageFile(file) {

        if (!file) return;

        const reader =
            new FileReader();

        reader.onload = () => {

            const image =
                new Image();

            image.onload = () => {
                state.image.sourceImage = image;
                applyImageDimensions();
            };

            image.onerror = () => {
                setStatus("IMG ERR");
                updateImageInfo("Could not load image.");
            };
            image.src =
                reader.result;
        };

        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    }

    function beginImageOriginPick() {

        state.image.picking =
            !state.image.picking;

        if (
            state.areaSelecting &&
            state.image.picking
        ) {
            cancelAreaSelection();
        }

        const btn =
            $('#w3x-image-origin');

        if (btn) {
            btn.classList.toggle(
                'active',
                state.image.picking
            );
        }

        const shield =
            $('#w3x-select-shield');

        const help =
            $('#w3x-select-help');

        if (shield) {
            shield.classList.toggle(
                'active',
                state.image.picking
            );
        }

        if (help) {

            if (state.image.picking) {
                help.textContent =
                    'Click on the canvas to set image origin';
                help.classList.add('active');
            } else {
                help.classList.remove('active');
            }
        }

        document.body.style.cursor =
            state.image.picking
                ? 'crosshair'
                : '';
    }

    function handleImageOriginClick(event) {

        if (!state.image.picking) return;

        if (event.button !== 0) return;

        event.preventDefault();

        event.stopPropagation();

        const point =
            getCanvasPoint(event);

        if (!point) return;

        state.image.origin =
            point;

        state.image.picking = false;

        const btn =
            $('#w3x-image-origin');

        if (btn)
            btn.classList.remove('active');

        const shield =
            $('#w3x-select-shield');

        const help =
            $('#w3x-select-help');

        if (shield)
            shield.classList.remove('active');

        if (help)
            help.classList.remove('active');

        document.body.style.cursor = '';

        updateImagePreview();

        updateImageInfo();
    }

    function updateImagePreview() {

        const box =
            $('#w3x-image-preview-box');

        if (!box) return;

        if (state.bot.running) {
            box.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }

        if (!state.image.previewOn) {
            box.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }

        const origin =
            state.image.origin;

        const previewUrl =
            state.image.previewUrl;

        if (!origin || !previewUrl) {
            box.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }

        const scale =
            state.image.scale || 1;

        const bw =
            state.image.w * scale;

        const bh =
            state.image.h * scale;

        const tl =
            canvasToScreen(
                origin.x,
                origin.y
            );

        const br =
            canvasToScreen(
                origin.x + bw,
                origin.y + bh
            );

        if (!tl || !br) {
            box.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }

        box.style.left =
            `${tl.x}px`;

        box.style.top =
            `${tl.y}px`;

        box.style.width =
            `${Math.max(2, br.x - tl.x)}px`;

        box.style.height =
            `${Math.max(2, br.y - tl.y)}px`;

        box.innerHTML =
            `<img src="${previewUrl}" alt="">`;

        box.style.display =
            'block';
    }

    function hideImagePreview() {

        const box =
            $('#w3x-image-preview-box');

        if (box)
            box.style.display = 'none';
    }

    function updateImageInfo(reason) {

        const info =
            $('#w3x-image-info');

        if (!info) return;

        if (reason) {
            info.innerHTML =
                reason;
            return;
        }

        const origin =
            state.image.origin;

        const scale =
            state.image.scale || 1;

        let html = '';

        if (!state.image.hasImage) {

            html =
                'No image loaded. Choose a file.';
        } else {

            html =
                `Image: <b>${state.image.w}×${state.image.h}</b>` +
                ` • Scale <b>${scale}x</b>` +
                ` • Size <b>${state.image.w * scale}×${state.image.h * scale}</b><br>`;
        }

        if (!origin) {

            html +=
                'Origin: not set. Click canvas.';
        } else {

            html +=
                `Origin: <b>${origin.x}, ${origin.y}</b>`;
        }

        info.innerHTML =
            html;
    }

    function buildImageQueue(palette) {

        // Resim pixel'lerini en yakın palette rengine eşler
        // ve { x, y, colorId } kuyruğu üretir.
        // Saydam (düşük alpha) pixel'ler atlanır.

        if (!state.image.hasImage)
            return [];

        const queue = [];

        const origin =
            state.image.origin;

        const scale =
            state.image.scale || 1;

        const data =
            state.image.data;

        const w =
            state.image.w;

        const h =
            state.image.h;

        // Take snapshot of image bounds so same-colored canvas pixels are skipped
        takeSnapshot({
            x1: origin.x,
            y1: origin.y,
            x2: origin.x + w * scale - 1,
            y2: origin.y + h * scale - 1
        });

        // === PraytClient Dithering ===
        let imgData = { data: data, width: w, height: h };
        if (state.prayt.dither && w > 0 && h > 0) {
            imgData = praytFloydSteinberg(imgData, w, h, palette);
        }
        const pixelData = imgData.data || data;

        for (let sy = 0; sy < h; sy++) {

            const base =
                sy * w * 4;

            for (let sx = 0; sx < w; sx++) {

                const i =
                    base + sx * 4;

                const a =
                    pixelData[i + 3];

                // Saydamlık eşiği: yeterince opak olmayanı atla.

                if (a < 128) continue;

                const r = pixelData[i];
                const g = pixelData[i + 1];
                const b = pixelData[i + 2];

                const colorId =
                    nearestPaletteId(
                        r,
                        g,
                        b,
                        palette
                    );

                if (colorId === null)
                    continue;

                // === PraytClient Sea Skip (by color ID or RGB) ===
                if (state.prayt.seaSkip && (state.prayt.seaColors.has(colorId) || isSeaRGB(r, g, b))) continue;

                // === PraytClient Color Ignore ===
                if (state.prayt.ignoreColors.has(colorId)) continue;

                for (
                    let dy = 0;
                    dy < scale;
                    dy++
                ) {
                    for (
                        let dx = 0;
                        dx < scale;
                        dx++
                    ) {
                        const cx = origin.x + sx * scale + dx;
                        const cy = origin.y + sy * scale + dy;

                        if (state.prayt.seaSkip && isCanvasSeaPixel(cx, cy)) continue;
                        if (isPixelAlreadyColor(cx, cy, colorId)) continue;

                        queue.push({
                            x: cx,
                            y: cy,
                            colorId,
                            forcePlace: true
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return queue;
    }

    function drawImage() {

        if (!state.image.hasImage) {

            setStatus('NO IMG');

            return;
        }

        if (!state.image.origin) {

            setStatus('ORIGIN?');

            return;
        }

        if (!state.wsReady) {

            setStatus('NO WS');

            return;
        }

        const palette =
            getPaletteColors();

        if (palette.length === 0) {

            setStatus('NO PALETTE');

            return;
        }

        const handColor = getSelectedColorId();
        if (handColor !== null) state.prayt.currentHandColor = handColor;

        let queue =
            buildImageQueue(palette);
        queue = praytSortQueue(queue);

        if (queue.length === 0) {

            setStatus('BOT DONE (ALL SAME COLOR)');

            return;
        }

        // Auto-Setup Image Target Map & Protection for Active and Post-Draw Defense
        const imgW = state.image.w, imgH = state.image.h, imgData = state.image.data;
        const scale = state.image.scale || 1;
        const ox = state.image.origin.x, oy = state.image.origin.y;
        const mapW = imgW * scale;
        const mapH = imgH * scale;
        const targetData = new Int16Array(mapW * mapH);
        for (let sy = 0; sy < imgH; sy++) {
            for (let sx = 0; sx < imgW; sx++) {
                const idx = (sy * imgW + sx) * 4;
                const a = imgData[idx + 3];
                const cid = (a >= 128) ? nearestPaletteId(imgData[idx], imgData[idx+1], imgData[idx+2], palette) : -1;
                for (let dy = 0; dy < scale; dy++) {
                    for (let dx = 0; dx < scale; dx++) {
                        const mx = sx * scale + dx;
                        const my = sy * scale + dy;
                        const ax = ox + mx;
                        const ay = oy + my;
                        if (state.prayt.seaSkip && (isCanvasSeaPixel(ax, ay) || (cid !== null && state.prayt.seaColors.has(cid)) || isSeaRGB(imgData[idx], imgData[idx+1], imgData[idx+2]))) {
                            targetData[my * mapW + mx] = -1;
                        } else {
                            targetData[my * mapW + mx] = (cid !== null) ? cid : -1;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        state.prayt.areaTargetMap = {
            x1: ox,
            y1: oy,
            x2: ox + mapW - 1,
            y2: oy + mapH - 1,
            w: mapW,
            h: mapH,
            data: targetData,
            type: 'image'
        };
        state.savedArea = {
            x1: ox,
            y1: oy,
            x2: ox + mapW - 1,
            y2: oy + mapH - 1
        };
        state.prayt.protectImage = { w: imgW, h: imgH, data: imgData };
        state.prayt.protectOrigin = { x: ox, y: oy };
        state.prayt.realtimeProtect = true;
        state.prayt.godDefense = false;
        if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') praytGodDefense.active = false;
        state.bot.protect = false;
        updatePraytProtectInfo();

        state.bot.running = true;
        state.bot.paused = false;
        state.bot.placed = 0;
        state.bot.skipped = 0;
        state.bot.tur = 1;
        state.bot.queue = queue;
        state.bot.total = queue.length;

        setStatus('IMAGE BOTTING');

        praytMxoEngine.stop();
        for (let my = 0; my < mapH; my++) {
            for (let mx = 0; mx < mapW; mx++) {
                const expCid = targetData[my * mapW + mx];
                if (expCid >= 0) {
                    const ax = ox + mx;
                    const ay = oy + my;
                    if (isPixelAlreadyColor(ax, ay, expCid)) {
                        praytMxoEngine.drawnPixels.set(`${ax},${ay}`, expCid);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < queue.length; i++) {
            const p = queue[i];
            praytMxoEngine.putPixel(p.x, p.y, p.colorId, {
                forcePlace: true,
                isSlowCleanup: !!p.isSlowCleanup,
                turnPause: !!p.turnPause,
                strokePause: !!p.strokePause,
                colorSwitchPause: !!p.colorSwitchPause,
                strokeStep: p.strokeStep !== undefined ? p.strokeStep : i
            });
        }
        praytMxoEngine.start();
    }

    function imageBotTick(callback) {

        if (!state.bot.running) return;

        if (state.bot.queue.length === 0) {

            state.bot.running = false;

            if (callback) callback();

            return;
        }

        // === Mxo Priority Defense for Image Bot ===
        let pixel = null;
        if (state.prayt.realtimeProtect && state.prayt.protectQueue && state.prayt.protectQueue.length > 0) {
            pixel = state.prayt.protectQueue.shift();
            if (pixel && pixel.key) state.prayt.protectQueueSet.delete(pixel.key);
        } else if (state.bot.queue && state.bot.queue.length > 0) {
            pixel = state.bot.queue.shift();
        }
        if (!pixel) {
            if (state.prayt.realtimeProtect) {
                state.bot.timer = setTimeout(() => imageBotTick(callback), state.bot.speed || 13);
                return;
            }
            state.bot.running = false;
            if (callback) callback();
            return;
        }

        if (!state.ws) {

            state.bot.timer =
                setTimeout(
                    () => imageBotTick(callback),
                    state.bot.speed
                );

            return;
        }

        wsSend(
            'p',
            `[${pixel.x},${pixel.y},${pixel.colorId},1]`
        );

        state.bot.placed++;

        updateBotUI();

        state.bot.timer =
            setTimeout(
                () => imageBotTick(callback),
                state.bot.speed
            );
    }

    function clearImage() {

        state.image.origin = null;

        if (
            $('#w3x-bot-stop') &&
            state.bot.running
        ) {
            stopBot();
        }

        hideImagePreview();

        updateImageInfo();
    }

    function startBot() {
        if (!state.savedArea) {
            setStatus('NO AREA');
            return;
        }
        if (!state.wsReady) {
            setStatus('NO WS');
            return;
        }
        const colorId = getSelectedColorId() !== null ? getSelectedColorId() : state.bot.colorId;
        if (colorId === null || colorId === undefined) {
            setStatus('NO COLOR');
            return;
        }

        const modeSel = $('#w3x-bot-mode');
        if (modeSel && modeSel.value) {
            state.bot.mode = modeSel.value;
            if (state.prayt) state.prayt.drawStrategy = modeSel.value;
        } else if (state.bot.mode === 'radius_attack') {
            state.bot.mode = 'sequential';
        }

        if (state.prayt) {
            state.prayt.protectImage = null;
            state.prayt.protectOrigin = null;
            state.prayt.realtimeProtect = false;
            state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect = false;
            state.prayt.godDefense = false;
        }
        if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') {
            praytGodDefense.active = false;
        }
        state.bot.protect = false;
        state.bot.currentTaskType = null;
        state.bot.running = true;
        state.bot.paused = false;
        state.prayt.statsStartTime = Date.now();
        if (state.prayt.userManualPixels) state.prayt.userManualPixels.clear();
        state.bot.placed = 0;
        state.bot.skipped = 0;
        state.bot.tur = 1;
        state.bot.colorId = colorId;
        state.prayt.currentHandColor = colorId;
        state.bot.accent = findSelectedColor();
        state.bot.rgb = hexToInt(state.bot.accent);
        setSolidAreaTargetMap(state.savedArea, colorId);

        praytMxoEngine.stop();
        takeSnapshot(null, true);

        let queue = buildBotQueue().map(p => ({ ...p, colorId }));
        if (state.bot.mode !== 'circle') {
            queue = praytSortQueue(queue);
        }
        state.bot.queue = queue;
        state.bot.total = queue.length;

        if (state.savedArea && praytMxoEngine.drawnPixels) {
            for (let y = state.savedArea.y1; y <= state.savedArea.y2; y++) {
                for (let x = state.savedArea.x1; x <= state.savedArea.x2; x++) {
                    if (isPixelAlreadyColor(x, y, colorId)) {
                        praytMxoEngine.drawnPixels.set(`${x},${y}`, colorId);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (queue.length === 0) {
            state.bot.running = false;
            state.bot.protect = true;
            state.bot.humanProtect = false;
            if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') {
                praytGodDefense.active = true;
                state.prayt.godDefense = true;
                praytGodDefense.syncButtons();
                praytGodDefense.updateUI();
            }
            praytMxoEngine.markAreaAsFullyDrawn();
            if (typeof _globalSyncAreaProtectUI === 'function') _globalSyncAreaProtectUI();
            updatePraytProtectInfo();
            praytMxoEngine.start();
            updateBotUI(true);
            setStatus('✅ ALAN TAM • 🛡️ MXO 0MS KORUMA AKTİF');
            return;
        }

        for (let i = 0; i < queue.length; i++) {
            const p = queue[i];
            praytMxoEngine.putPixel(p.x, p.y, p.colorId ?? colorId, {
                forcePlace: true,
                isSlowCleanup: !!p.isSlowCleanup,
                turnPause: !!p.turnPause,
                strokePause: !!p.strokePause,
                colorSwitchPause: !!p.colorSwitchPause,
                strokeStep: p.strokeStep !== undefined ? p.strokeStep : i
            });
        }
        praytMxoEngine.start();

        updateBotUI(true);
        setStatus('BOTTING');
    }

    function stopBot() {
        if (state.prayt && state.prayt.liveClockActive) {
            praytStopLiveClock(false);
        }
        state.bot.running = false;
        state.bot.protect = false;
        state.bot.humanProtect = false;
        state.bot.currentTaskType = null;
        if (state.prayt) {
            state.prayt.realtimeProtect = false;
            state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect = false;
        }
        if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined' && praytGodDefense.active) {
            praytGodDefense.toggle(false);
        }
        if (typeof praytHumanDuo !== 'undefined' && praytHumanDuo.active) {
            praytHumanDuo.toggle(false);
        }
        praytMxoEngine.stop();
        state.bot.queue = [];
        state.bot.protectQueue = [];
        state.bot.protectQueueSet.clear();
        if (state.bot.protectTimer) {
            clearTimeout(state.bot.protectTimer);
            state.bot.protectTimer = null;
        }
        state.bot.snapshot = null;
        if (typeof _globalSyncAreaProtectUI === 'function') _globalSyncAreaProtectUI();
        updateBotUI(true);
        updatePraytProtectInfo();
        setStatus('CONNECTED');
    }

    function botTick() {

        if (!state.bot.running) return;

        if (state.bot.queue.length === 0) {

            // Yerleşim turu bitti.

            if (state.bot.protect) {

                // KORUMA MODU:
                // Alanı tara; yanlış renkli pixel varsa düzelt,
                // yoksa kısa süre bekleyip tekrar tara.
                // Bu döngü STOP'a basılana kadar sürer,
                // yani biri bota dokunursa bot hemen düzeltir.

                state.bot.queue =
                    buildPatrolQueue();

                if (state.bot.queue.length > 0) {

                    state.bot.tur++;
                    updateBotUI();

                    state.bot.timer =
                        setTimeout(
                            botTick,
                            state.bot.speed
                        );

                    return;
                }

                // Alan şu an temiz; bekle ve tekrar tara.
                updateBotUI();

                state.bot.timer =
                    setTimeout(
                        botTick,
                        state.bot.protectDelay
                    );

                return;
            }

            if (state.savedArea) {
                state.bot.running = false;
                state.bot.protect = true;
                state.bot.humanProtect = false;
                if (state.prayt) state.prayt.noProtectWhileDrawing = false;
                if (typeof _globalSyncAreaProtectUI === 'function') _globalSyncAreaProtectUI();
                if (typeof syncImageProtectToggles === 'function') syncImageProtectToggles();
                updatePraytProtectInfo();
                setStatus('⚡ ÇİZİM BİTTİ → 🛡️ MXO ULTRA GÜÇLÜ KORUMA AKTİF');
                updateBotUI();
                if (typeof praytMxoEngine !== 'undefined') {
                    praytMxoEngine.running = true;
                    praytMxoEngine.ensureRunning();
                }
                return;
            }

            state.bot.running = false;

            updateBotUI();
            setStatus('BOT DONE');

            return;
        }

        // === Mxo Priority Defense: protectQueue takes 100% priority ===
        let pixel = null;
        if (state.bot.protect && state.bot.protectQueue && state.bot.protectQueue.length > 0) {
            pixel = state.bot.protectQueue.shift();
            if (pixel && pixel.key) state.bot.protectQueueSet.delete(pixel.key);
        } else if (state.bot.queue && state.bot.queue.length > 0) {
            pixel = state.bot.queue.shift();
        }
        if (!pixel) {
            if (state.bot.protect) {
                state.bot.timer = setTimeout(botTick, state.bot.speed || 13);
            } else {
                state.bot.running = false;
            }
            return;
        }

        if (!state.ws) {

            state.bot.timer =
                setTimeout(
                    botTick,
                    state.bot.speed
                );

            return;
        }

        wsSend(
            'p',
            `[${pixel.x},${pixel.y},${state.bot.colorId},1]`
        );

        markPlaced(pixel.x, pixel.y);

        state.bot.placed++;

        updateBotUI();

        state.bot.timer =
            setTimeout(
                botTick,
                state.bot.speed
            );
    }

    let _lastBotUIUpdate = 0;
    function updateBotUI(force) {
        const now = Date.now();
        if (!force && now - _lastBotUIUpdate < 120) return;
        _lastBotUIUpdate = now;

        const runText =
            $('#w3x-bot-run-text');

        const info =
            $('#w3x-bot-info');

        const progress =
            $('#w3x-bot-progress');

        const startBtn =
            $('#w3x-bot-start');

        const stopBtn =
            $('#w3x-bot-stop');

        if (runText) {

            runText.textContent =
                !state.bot.running
                    ? ((state.bot.protect || (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined' && praytGodDefense.active))
                        ? 'PROTECTING'
                        : (state.bot.placed > 0
                            ? 'DONE'
                            : 'STOPPED'))
                    : (state.bot.protect &&
                       state.bot.queue.length === 0)
                        ? 'PROTECTING'
                        : 'RUNNING';
        }

        const isBotActive = !!(state.bot.running || state.bot.protect || state.bot.humanProtect ||
            (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined' && praytGodDefense.active) ||
            (state.prayt && (state.prayt.liveClockActive || state.prayt.realtimeProtect || state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect)));

        // START butonu koruma açıkken ASLA kilitlenmez; basıldığında yeni boyamayı anında başlatır!
        if (startBtn) {
            startBtn.disabled = false;
        }

        if (stopBtn) {
            stopBtn.disabled = !isBotActive;
        }

        if (info) {

            if (state.bot.running) {

                const pct =
                    state.bot.total > 0
                        ? (
                            (state.bot.placed /
                            state.bot.total) *
                            100
                        ).toFixed(1)
                        : 0;

                const modeLabel =
                    state.bot.mode === 'nuke'
                        ? '💥 MAHVET (NUKE)'
                        : state.bot.mode === 'human'
                        ? 'HUMAN (KARALAMA)'
                        : state.bot.mode === 'random'
                        ? 'RANDOM'
                        : state.bot.mode === 'circle'
                            ? 'CIRCLE'
                            : 'ORDERED';

                // Koruma modunda ana iş bitince alanı izler.

                const remaining = Math.max(0, state.bot.total - state.bot.placed);
                const eta = ((remaining * (state.bot.speed || 16)) / 1000).toFixed(1);

                if (progress) {
                    progress.style.width = `${pct}%`;
                }

                const prioLen = (typeof praytMxoEngine !== 'undefined' && praytMxoEngine.priorityTasks) ? praytMxoEngine.priorityTasks.length : 0;
                const balTag = !(state.prayt && state.prayt.noProtectWhileDrawing)
                    ? (prioLen > 0 ? ` • <b style="color:#ffd54f;">⚖️KORUMA(${prioLen})</b>` : ` • <b style="color:#00e676;">⚖️DENGELİ</b>`)
                    : '';

                info.innerHTML =
                    `<b>${modeLabel}</b>${balTag} | ` +
                    `<b>${state.bot.placed}</b>/${state.bot.total} (<b>%${pct}</b>) | ` +
                    `${state.bot.speed}ms • <b>${eta}s</b>`;

            } else if (state.bot.protect || (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined' && praytGodDefense.active)) {
                if (progress) progress.style.width = '100%';
                info.innerHTML = `🛡️ <b>MXO 0MS KORUMA AKTİF</b> • Basılan: <b>${state.bot.placed.toLocaleString()}</b> px`;
            } else if (
                state.bot.placed > 0
            ) {
                info.innerHTML =
                    `✅ Bitti: <b>${state.bot.placed.toLocaleString()}</b> px (Atlanan: <b>${state.bot.skipped}</b>)`;
            }
        }
    }

    // =========================================================
    // UPDATE
    // =========================================================

    function update() {

        const canvas =
            getCanvas();

        if (!canvas)
            return;

        detectColor();
        updatePraytStats();

        const size =
            $('#w3x-size');

        if (size) {

            size.textContent =
                `${canvas.width} × ${canvas.height}`;
        }

        const cw =
            $('#w3x-canvas-width');

        const ch =
            $('#w3x-canvas-height');

        if (cw)
            cw.textContent =
                canvas.width;

        if (ch)
            ch.textContent =
                canvas.height;

        const params =
            new URLSearchParams(
                location.hash.slice(1)
            );

        const cx =
            params.get('x');

        const cy =
            params.get('y');

        const zoom =
            params.get('s');

        const camera =
            $('#w3x-camera');

        if (camera) {

            camera.textContent =
                `${cx ?? '-'}, ${cy ?? '-'} @ ${zoom ?? '-'}`;
        }

        if (state.savedArea) {

            const rect =
                getAreaScreenRect(
                    state.savedArea
                );

            const marker =
                $('#w3x-area-marker');

            if (
                rect &&
                marker
            ) {
                positionMarker(
                    marker,
                    rect
                );
            }

            if (
                !marker &&
                rect
            ) {
                renderSavedArea();
            }
        }

        if (
            state.areaSelecting &&
            state.areaStart &&
            state.areaEnd
        ) {
            drawPreview();
        }

        if (state.targetPosition) {
            renderTargetPosition();
        }

        // Yazı / resim origin preview'ını kamera değişince tazele.

        updateTextPreview();

        updateImagePreview();

        // Aktif wake flash'lerini kamera değişince yeniden yerleştir.

        updateWakeFlashes();
    }

    let _lastStatusText = '';
    let _lastStatusTime = 0;
    function setStatus(text) {
        if (text === _lastStatusText) return;
        const now = performance.now();
        if (now - _lastStatusTime < 80 && !/BİTTİ|AKTİF|DURDUR|KAPALI|SEÇ/i.test(text)) return;
        _lastStatusText = text;
        _lastStatusTime = now;
        const status = $('#w3x-status');
        if (status) status.textContent = text;
    }

    // =========================================================
    // RESIZE / CAMERA WATCHER
    // =========================================================

    function setupResizeWatcher() {
        let _rafPending = false;
        const syncTransform = () => {
            if (_rafPending) return;
            _rafPending = true;
            requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                _rafPending = false;
                getAreaLayer();
                if (state.savedArea) {
                    renderSavedArea();
                    if (state.prayt && state.prayt.flagPreviewActive && state.prayt.flagPreviewVisible && typeof praytRenderFlagPreview === 'function') {
                        praytRenderFlagPreview(state.savedArea);
                    }
                }
                if (state.areaSelecting && state.areaStart && state.areaEnd) {
                    drawPreview();
                }
                if (state.targetPosition) {
                    renderTargetPosition();
                }
            });
        };

        window.addEventListener('resize', syncTransform, { passive: true });
        window.addEventListener('wheel', syncTransform, { passive: true });
        window.addEventListener('scroll', syncTransform, { passive: true });

        let _wakeRafActive = false;
        function frame() {
            if (state.wake && state.wake.on && document.querySelector('.w3x-wake-flash')) {
                updateWakeFlashes();
                requestAnimationFrame(frame);
            } else {
                _wakeRafActive = false;
            }
        }
        window._triggerWakeFlashAnim = () => {
            if (!_wakeRafActive && state.wake && state.wake.on) {
                _wakeRafActive = true;
                requestAnimationFrame(frame);
            }
        };
    }

    // =========================================================
    // CLEANUP
    // =========================================================

    // =========================================================
    // PRAYTCLIENT CORE FUNCTIONS & ENGINE
    // =========================================================

    function rgbToHex(r, g, b) {
        return "#" + ((1 << 24) + (r << 16) + (g << 8) + b).toString(16).slice(1).toUpperCase();
    }

    function isSeaRGB(r, g, b) {
        if (!state.prayt || !state.prayt.seaRGBs || state.prayt.seaRGBs.length === 0) return false;
        for (let i = 0; i < state.prayt.seaRGBs.length; i++) {
            const c = state.prayt.seaRGBs[i];
            const dr = r - c.r, dg = g - c.g, db = b - c.b;
            if (dr * dr + dg * dg + db * db < 625) return true; // threshold ~25
        }
        return false;
    }

    function isCanvasSeaPixel(x, y) {
        if (!state.prayt || !state.prayt.seaSkip) return false;
        const hasColors = state.prayt.seaColors && state.prayt.seaColors.size > 0;
        const hasRGBs = state.prayt.seaRGBs && state.prayt.seaRGBs.length > 0;

        // Kullanıcı deniz rengi seçmediyse kesinlikle hiçbir yeri deniz sayma!
        if (!hasColors && !hasRGBs) return false;

        let r, g, b;
        let colorId = null;
        const snap = state.bot.snapshot;
        if (snap && x >= snap.x1 && y >= snap.y1 && x < snap.x1 + snap.w && y < snap.y1 + snap.h) {
            const px = x - snap.x1;
            const py = y - snap.y1;
            const col = snap.colors[py * snap.w + px];
            if (col >= 0) {
                r = (col >> 16) & 255;
                g = (col >> 8) & 255;
                b = col & 255;
            }
        } else if (state.prayt.mapCache && state.prayt.mapLoaded) {
            const i = state.prayt.mapWidth * y + x;
            colorId = state.prayt.mapCache[i];
            const col = getPaletteColorById(colorId);
            if (col) {
                r = col.r; g = col.g; b = col.b;
            }
        }

        if (r === undefined && colorId === null) return false;

        // 1. Kullanıcının haritadan eyedropper veya hex ile eklediği RGB kontrolü
        if (hasRGBs && r !== undefined && isSeaRGB(r, g, b)) {
            return true;
        }

        // 2. Kullanıcının seçtiği Palette ID kontrolü
        if (hasColors) {
            if (colorId !== null && state.prayt.seaColors.has(colorId)) {
                return true;
            }
            if (r !== undefined) {
                const palette = getPaletteColors();
                if (colorId === null) {
                    colorId = nearestPaletteId(r, g, b, palette);
                }
                if (colorId !== null && state.prayt.seaColors.has(colorId)) {
                    return true;
                }
                // 3. Kullanıcının seçtiği HEX kontrolü
                const hex = rgbToHex(r, g, b);
                if (state.prayt.seaColors.has(hex)) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    // =========================================================
    // COLOR-FIRST HAND PERSISTENCE ("İLK ELİNE ALDIĞI COLOR'U BİTİR")
    // =========================================================
    // Çoklu renkli çizimlerde (Türk Bayrağı, LGBT Bayrağı, Resim, Koruma)
    // her pikselde renk değiştirip bot gibi görünmesini engeller:
    // Önce eldeki (veya ilk seçilen) rengin TÜM piksellerini tamamen bitirir,
    // ardından paletten diğer renge geçip onu bitirir!
    function selectPaletteColorInDOM(colorId) {
        if (colorId === null || colorId === undefined) return;
        state.prayt.currentHandColor = colorId;
        state.bot.colorId = colorId;
        // Kullanıcı son 1 saniyede kendisi piksel koyduysa, kullanıcının elindeki rengi zorla değiştirme!
        if (state.prayt && state.prayt.lastUserManualPlaceTime && (Date.now() - state.prayt.lastUserManualPlaceTime < 1000)) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            const btn = document.querySelector(`#palette-buttons > a[data-id="${colorId}"]`);
            if (btn && !btn.classList.contains('selected')) {
                document.querySelectorAll('#palette-buttons > a.selected').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('selected'));
                btn.classList.add('selected');
                btn.click();
            }
        } catch {}
    }

    function groupByFirstHandColor(pixels, perColorSorter = null) {
        if (!pixels || pixels.length <= 1) return pixels;

        const groups = new Map();
        for (let i = 0; i < pixels.length; i++) {
            const p = pixels[i];
            const obj = Array.isArray(p) ? { x: p[0], y: p[1], colorId: p[2] } : { ...p };
            const cId = obj.colorId !== undefined && obj.colorId !== null ? obj.colorId : state.bot.colorId;
            if (!groups.has(cId)) groups.set(cId, []);
            groups.get(cId).push(obj);
        }

        if (groups.size <= 1) {
            const singleList = Array.from(groups.values())[0] || pixels;
            return perColorSorter ? perColorSorter(singleList) : singleList;
        }

        // 1. Öncelik: Kullanıcının o an elinde tuttuğu renk (ilk eline aldığı color!)
        const handColor = getSelectedColorId() !== null
            ? getSelectedColorId()
            : (state.prayt.currentHandColor !== null ? state.prayt.currentHandColor : state.bot.colorId);

        const colorOrder = Array.from(groups.keys());
        colorOrder.sort((a, b) => {
            if (a === handColor && b !== handColor) return -1;
            if (b === handColor && a !== handColor) return 1;
            // Eldeki renk yoksa en çok pikseli olan ana rengi ilk eline alır
            return groups.get(b).length - groups.get(a).length;
        });

        const finalQueue = [];
        for (let cIdx = 0; cIdx < colorOrder.length; cIdx++) {
            const cId = colorOrder[cIdx];
            let subQueue = groups.get(cId);
            if (perColorSorter && subQueue.length > 1) {
                subQueue = perColorSorter(subQueue);
            }
            if (cIdx > 0 && subQueue.length > 0) {
                // Yeni renge geçerken paletten renk seçme duraksaması işareti koy
                subQueue[0] = { ...subQueue[0], colorSwitchPause: true };
            }
            for (let i = 0; i < subQueue.length; i++) {
                finalQueue.push(subQueue[i]);
            }
        }

        return finalQueue;
    }

    // =========================================================
    // TRUE HUMAN HAND SIMULATOR V5 (PRO PIXELPLACE CONNECTED BRUSH + ZERO ORPHAN HOLES)
    // =========================================================
    function buildSingleColorOrganicPath(pixels, isDefense = false) {
        if (!pixels || pixels.length <= 1) return pixels;

        const unvisited = new Map();
        const getKey = (x, y) => `${x},${y}`;
        let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
        for (let i = 0; i < pixels.length; i++) {
            const p = pixels[i];
            const obj = Array.isArray(p) ? { x: p[0], y: p[1], colorId: p[2] } : { ...p };
            unvisited.set(getKey(obj.x, obj.y), obj);
            if (obj.x < minX) minX = obj.x;
            if (obj.x > maxX) maxX = obj.x;
            if (obj.y < minY) minY = obj.y;
            if (obj.y > maxY) maxY = obj.y;
        }

        // Yardımcı: Bir pikselin etrafındaki (8-komşuluk) henüz boyanmamış piksel sayısı (0..8)
        // deg <= 1 olan pikseller "yetim kalmak üzere olan" piksellerdir; fırça geçerken anında süpürür,
        // böylece arkada ASLA tek tek basılacak delik (Swiss-cheese hole) kalmaz!
        const countUnvisitedNeighbors8 = (cx, cy) => {
            let cnt = 0;
            for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
                for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
                    if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) continue;
                    if (unvisited.has(getKey(cx + dx, cy + dy))) cnt++;
                }
            }
            return cnt;
        };

        const result = [];
        // Başlangıç noktası: Gerçek bir PixelPlace oyuncusu gibi alanın dış kenarından / köşesine yakın bir sınır pikselinden başla
        let startCand = null;
        let bestEdgeScore = Infinity;
        let sampleCount = 0;
        for (const cand of unvisited.values()) {
            const distToCorner = Math.min(
                Math.hypot(cand.x - minX, cand.y - minY),
                Math.hypot(cand.x - maxX, cand.y - minY),
                Math.hypot(cand.x - minX, cand.y - maxY),
                Math.hypot(cand.x - maxX, cand.y - maxY)
            );
            const deg = countUnvisitedNeighbors8(cand.x, cand.y);
            const edgeScore = distToCorner * 0.7 + deg * 1.8 + Math.random() * 3.5;
            if (edgeScore < bestEdgeScore) {
                bestEdgeScore = edgeScore;
                startCand = cand;
            }
            if (++sampleCount > 180) break;
        }
        let current = startCand || unvisited.values().next().value;
        unvisited.delete(getKey(current.x, current.y));
        result.push({
            ...current,
            isSlowCleanup: isDefense,
            isHumanDefense: isDefense,
            strokeStart: true,
            strokeStep: 0,
            jumpDist: 0
        });

        let lastDx = 1;
        let lastDy = 0;
        let axisLockRun = 0;
        // Hafif eğimli insan bilek açısı (PixelPlace'te oyuncular sağ-sol / çapraz kavisli tarama yapar)
        const baseTilt = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.42; // ~±12 derece doğal bilek eğimi
        let sweepDir = 1;
        let sweepProgress = 0;
        let sweepSpan = Math.max(8, Math.min(28, Math.round(Math.hypot(maxX - minX, maxY - minY) * 0.55)));
        let strokeStep = 0;

        while (unvisited.size > 0) {
            strokeStep++;
            sweepProgress++;
            if (sweepProgress >= sweepSpan) {
                sweepProgress = 0;
                sweepDir = -sweepDir;
                sweepSpan = Math.max(7, Math.min(30, sweepSpan + Math.floor((Math.random() - 0.5) * 5)));
            }

            // Sinüzoidal kavisli bilek açısı: Düz cetvel çizgisi yerine akıcı S-kavisli fırça sürüklemesi
            const waveOffset = Math.sin(strokeStep * 0.34) * 0.38 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.18;
            const targetAngle = (sweepDir > 0 ? baseTilt : (Math.PI + baseTilt)) + waveOffset;

            let best = null;
            let bestScore = Infinity;

            // 1. AŞAMA: KESİNLİKLE BİTİŞİK 8-KOMŞULUK (r = 1: |dx| <= 1, |dy| <= 1)
            // Fareyi basılı tutup sürükleyen gerçek bir insan gibi pikseller %100 birbirine değerek ilerler!
            for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
                for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
                    if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) continue;
                    const nx = current.x + dx;
                    const ny = current.y + dy;
                    const cand = unvisited.get(getKey(nx, ny));
                    if (!cand) continue;

                    const deg = countUnvisitedNeighbors8(nx, ny);

                    // 🛡️ ANTI-ORPHAN KURALI:
                    // Eğer bu komşunun etrafında 0 veya 1 boyanmamış piksel kaldıysa,
                    // onu geride bırakırsak tek başına yetim kalır ("tek tek boyama" görüntüsü oluşur).
                    // Bu yüzden deg === 0 veya deg === 1 olan bitişik komşuyu ANINDA süpür!
                    let orphanBonus = 0;
                    if (deg === 0) {
                        orphanBonus = -2500;
                    } else if (deg === 1) {
                        orphanBonus = -1200;
                    } else if (deg <= 4) {
                        // Sınır/cephe (frontier) piksellerini tercih et ki fırça kalın bir şerit halinde boşluksuz doldursun
                        orphanBonus = -(5 - deg) * 42;
                    } else {
                        // İçerideki yoğun pikseller
                        orphanBonus = deg * 8;
                    }

                    // Bilek yönü ve akıcılık hesabı
                    const moveAngle = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
                    const angleDiff = Math.abs(Math.atan2(Math.sin(moveAngle - targetAngle), Math.cos(moveAngle - targetAngle)));

                    // Bir önceki hareket yönüyle (lastDx, lastDy) yumuşak devamlılık (ani 180 derece zikzak yapmasın)
                    const dot = (dx * lastDx + dy * lastDy) / (Math.hypot(dx, dy) * Math.hypot(lastDx, lastDy) || 1);
                    const momentumBonus = -dot * 18;

                    // Robotik dümdüz yatay/dikey cetvel çizgisini (5+ piksel üst üste aynı eksen) hafifçe kır
                    const isSameAxisDir = (dx === lastDx && dy === lastDy) && (dx === 0 || dy === 0);
                    const straightPenalty = (isSameAxisDir && axisLockRun >= 4) ? (axisLockRun - 3) * 22 : 0;

                    // Çapraz adım (|dx|===1 && |dy|===1) mesafesi 1.41, tam komşu 1.0
                    const stepDistCost = (dx !== 0 && dy !== 0) ? 4.5 : 0;
                    const jitter = Math.random() * 3.2;

                    const score = orphanBonus + angleDiff * 14 + momentumBonus + straightPenalty + stepDistCost + jitter;
                    if (score < bestScore) {
                        bestScore = score;
                        best = cand;
                    }
                }
            }

            // 2. AŞAMA: Eğer r=1 (8-komşu) içinde hiç piksel kalmadıysa, en yakın boyanmamış kümeye elini kaydır
            let jumpDist = 1;
            if (!best) {
                let minDistSq = Infinity;
                let checked = 0;
                for (const cand of unvisited.values()) {
                    const dSq = (cand.x - current.x) ** 2 + (cand.y - current.y) ** 2;
                    const deg = countUnvisitedNeighbors8(cand.x, cand.y);
                    // Kümeye geçerken de kenar/yetim piksellerden başla ki yeni kümede de delik kalmasın
                    const weightedDist = dSq + deg * 2.5 + Math.random() * 2.0;
                    if (weightedDist < minDistSq) {
                        minDistSq = weightedDist;
                        best = cand;
                        if (dSq <= 4) break;
                    }
                    if (++checked > 450) break;
                }
                if (!best) best = unvisited.values().next().value;
                jumpDist = Math.hypot(best.x - current.x, best.y - current.y);
                strokeStep = 0;
            }

            const ndx = Math.sign(best.x - current.x);
            const ndy = Math.sign(best.y - current.y);
            const dotTurn = ndx * lastDx + ndy * lastDy;
            const isSharpTurn = (jumpDist <= 1.5 && dotTurn < 0); // Bileğin yön değiştirdiği kıvrım noktası

            if (ndx === lastDx && ndy === lastDy && (ndx === 0 || ndy === 0)) {
                axisLockRun++;
            } else {
                axisLockRun = 0;
            }
            if (ndx !== 0 || ndy !== 0) {
                lastDx = ndx;
                lastDy = ndy;
            }

            unvisited.delete(getKey(best.x, best.y));
            result.push({
                ...best,
                isSlowCleanup: isDefense || best.isSlowCleanup,
                isHumanDefense: isDefense,
                turnPause: isSharpTurn,
                strokePause: jumpDist > 2.4,
                jumpDist,
                strokeStep
            });
            current = best;
        }

        return result;
    }

    function buildOrganicHumanPath(pixels, isDefense = false) {
        if (!pixels || pixels.length <= 1) return pixels;
        return groupByFirstHandColor(pixels, (singleColorPixels) =>
            buildSingleColorOrganicPath(singleColorPixels, isDefense)
        );
    }

    function buildHumanScribbleQueue(queue, area) {
        if (!queue || queue.length <= 1) return queue;

        // Gerçek PixelPlace İnsan Boyaması:
        // 1. Önce eldeki rengi (%100 bitirmeden asla başka renge geçmez!)
        // 2. Rastgele delik bırakıp sonradan tek tek nokta atışı yapmaz!
        // 3. Bitişik 8-komşuluklu, kavisli ve cephe-süpürmeli (Anti-Orphan) akıcı fırça darbeleriyle
        //    sanki sol tıka / Space tuşuna basılı tutup fareyi gezdiriyormuş gibi tek seferde kesintisiz boyar.
        return groupByFirstHandColor(queue, (singleColorQueue) => {
            return buildSingleColorOrganicPath(singleColorQueue, false);
        });
    }

    function buildHumanStrokeQueue(queue, area) {
        return buildHumanScribbleQueue(queue, area);
    }

    // --- Dynamic Image Scaling ---
    function applyImageDimensions() {
        const image = state.image.sourceImage;
        if (!image) return;
        const MAX_SIZE = state.prayt.maxImageSize || 80;
        let srcW = image.width;
        let srcH = image.height;
        if (srcW === 0 || srcH === 0) return;
        if (srcW > MAX_SIZE || srcH > MAX_SIZE) {
            const ratio = Math.min(MAX_SIZE / srcW, MAX_SIZE / srcH);
            srcW = Math.max(1, Math.round(srcW * ratio));
            srcH = Math.max(1, Math.round(srcH * ratio));
        }
        const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        canvas.width = srcW;
        canvas.height = srcH;
        const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true });
        ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
        ctx.drawImage(image, 0, 0, srcW, srcH);
        const imgData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, srcW, srcH);
        state.image.img = image;
        state.image.previewUrl = canvas.toDataURL();
        state.image.w = srcW;
        state.image.h = srcH;
        state.image.data = imgData.data;
        state.image.hasImage = true;
        updateImagePreview();
        updateImageInfo();
        const dims = $("#w3x-image-dims");
        if (dims) dims.textContent = `${srcW} × ${srcH} px (Hazır)`;
    }

    // --- Sea Color Eyedropper (Canvas Color Pick) ---
    function beginSeaColorPick() {
        state.prayt.seaPicking = !state.prayt.seaPicking;
        if (state.areaSelecting) cancelAreaSelection();
        if (state.text.picking) state.text.picking = false;
        if (state.image.picking) state.image.picking = false;
        const shield = $("#w3x-select-shield");
        const help = $("#w3x-select-help");
        const btn = $("#w3x-prayt-sea-pick");
        if (btn) btn.classList.toggle("active", state.prayt.seaPicking);
        if (shield) shield.classList.toggle("active", state.prayt.seaPicking);
        if (help) {
            if (state.prayt.seaPicking) {
                help.textContent = "Haritada deniz/arkaplan rengine tıkla...";
                help.classList.add("active");
            } else {
                help.classList.remove("active");
            }
        }
        document.body.style.cursor = state.prayt.seaPicking ? "crosshair" : "";
    }

    function handleSeaPickClick(event) {
        if (!state.prayt.seaPicking) return;
        if (event.button !== 0) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
        const point = getCanvasPoint(event);
        if (!point) return;
        const canvas = getCanvas();
        if (!canvas) return;
        try {
            let r, g, b, colorId = null;
            const snap = state.bot.snapshot;
            if (snap && point.x >= snap.x1 && point.y >= snap.y1 && point.x < snap.x1 + snap.w && point.y < snap.y1 + snap.h) {
                const px = point.x - snap.x1;
                const py = point.y - snap.y1;
                const col = snap.colors[py * snap.w + px];
                r = (col >> 16) & 255;
                g = (col >> 8) & 255;
                b = col & 255;
            } else if (state.prayt.mapCache && state.prayt.mapLoaded) {
                const i = state.prayt.mapWidth * point.y + point.x;
                colorId = state.prayt.mapCache[i];
                const col = getPaletteColorById(colorId);
                if (col) {
                    r = col.r; g = col.g; b = col.b;
                }
            }

            if (r === undefined) {
                const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true }) || canvas.getContext("2d");
                const d = ctx.getImageData(point.x, point.y, 1, 1).data;
                r = d[0]; g = d[1]; b = d[2];
            }

            const hex = rgbToHex(r, g, b);
            const palette = state.prayt.autoPalette || getPaletteColors();
            if (colorId === null) {
                colorId = nearestPaletteId(r, g, b, palette);
            }
            if (colorId !== null) state.prayt.seaColors.add(colorId);
            state.prayt.seaColors.add(hex);
            if (!state.prayt.seaRGBs.some(c => c.hex === hex)) {
                state.prayt.seaRGBs.push({ r, g, b, hex });
            }
            state.prayt.seaSkip = true;
            const t1 = $("#w3x-prayt-sea-toggle");
            const t2 = $("#w3x-bot-sea-skip-toggle");
            if (t1) { t1.textContent = "SEA SKIP: ON"; t1.classList.add("protecting"); }
            if (t2) { t2.textContent = "SEA SKIP: ON"; t2.classList.add("protecting"); }
            console.log(`[PraytClient] Deniz rengi seçildi: ${hex} (ID: ${colorId})`);
            if (typeof setStatus === "function") {
                setStatus(`🌊 ATLANACAK RENK SEÇİLDİ: ${hex} (ID: #${colorId !== null ? colorId : '?'})`);
            }
            if (typeof praytShowToast === "function") {
                praytShowToast(`🌊 Deniz Rengi Seçildi: ${hex}`, "info");
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.warn("[PraytClient] Eyedropper hatasi:", e);
        }
        state.prayt.seaPicking = false;
        const shield = $("#w3x-select-shield");
        const help = $("#w3x-select-help");
        const btn = $("#w3x-prayt-sea-pick");
        if (shield) shield.classList.remove("active");
        if (help) help.classList.remove("active");
        if (btn) btn.classList.remove("active");
        document.body.style.cursor = "";
        updateSeaInfo();
    }

    function updateSeaInfo() {
        const info = $("#w3x-prayt-sea-info");
        if (!info) return;
        const ids = [...state.prayt.seaColors];
        const rgbs = state.prayt.seaRGBs || [];
        if (ids.length === 0 && rgbs.length === 0) {
            info.innerHTML = "Atlanacak renk seçilmedi. Haritadan veya paletten renk ekleyin.";
            return;
        }
        let html = "<b>Atlanacak Renkler:</b><br>";
        if (ids.length > 0) html += `ID: <b>${ids.join(", ")}</b><br>`;
        if (rgbs.length > 0) {
            html += '<div style="display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:4px;">';
            for (const c of rgbs) {
                html += `<span style="display:inline-block;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:3px;background:${c.hex};color:#fff;text-shadow:0 0 2px #000;font-size:10px;">${c.hex}</span>`;
            }
            html += "</div>";
        }
        info.innerHTML = html;
    }

    // =========================================================
    // RADIUS ATTACK (DAİRESEL SALDIRI - ELDEKİ RENK, AYNI RENKLERİ ATLA, DENİZ ATLA, İNSANSI AKICI KAVİSLİ YOL)
    // =========================================================

    function praytToggleClickAttack(forceState = null) {
        if (!state.prayt) return;
        if (typeof forceState === "boolean") {
            state.prayt.radiusAttackClicking = forceState;
        } else {
            state.prayt.radiusAttackClicking = !state.prayt.radiusAttackClicking;
        }

        if (state.prayt.radiusAttackClicking) {
            if (state.areaSelecting) cancelAreaSelection();
            if (state.text.picking) state.text.picking = false;
            if (state.image.picking) state.image.picking = false;
            if (state.prayt.seaPicking) state.prayt.seaPicking = false;
        }

        const shield = $("#w3x-select-shield");
        const help = $("#w3x-select-help");
        const btnToggle = $("#w3x-click-attack-toggle");
        const btnTop = $("#w3x-top-radius-attack");

        if (state.prayt.radiusAttackClicking) {
            if (shield) shield.classList.add("active");
            if (help) {
                help.textContent = `🎯 Dairesel Saldırı (R: ${state.prayt.radiusAttackR || 25}px): Haritada merkeze tıkla!`;
                help.classList.add("active");
            }
            if (btnToggle) {
                btnToggle.textContent = "🎯 TIKLA & SALDIR: ON";
                btnToggle.style.background = "rgba(255, 60, 0, 0.4)";
                btnToggle.style.borderColor = "#ff3300";
                btnToggle.style.color = "#fff";
            }
            if (btnTop) btnTop.classList.add("active");
            document.body.style.cursor = "crosshair";
            setStatus("🎯 HEDEF SEÇ: Haritada merkeze tıkla");
        } else {
            if (shield && !state.text.picking && !state.image.picking && !state.prayt.seaPicking && !state.areaSelecting) {
                shield.classList.remove("active");
            }
            if (help) help.classList.remove("active");
            if (btnToggle) {
                btnToggle.textContent = "🎯 TIKLA & SALDIR: OFF";
                btnToggle.style.background = "rgba(255, 140, 0, 0.18)";
                btnToggle.style.borderColor = "#ff8c00";
                btnToggle.style.color = "#ff9800";
            }
            if (btnTop) btnTop.classList.remove("active");
            if (!state.text.picking && !state.image.picking && !state.prayt.seaPicking) {
                document.body.style.cursor = "";
            }
        }
    }

    function praytExecuteRadiusAttack(cx, cy) {
        if (!state.wsReady) {
            setStatus("NO WS");
            return;
        }

        let colorId = getSelectedColorId();
        if (colorId === null || colorId === undefined) {
            colorId = state.bot.colorId;
        }
        if (colorId === null || colorId === undefined) {
            setStatus("❌ RENK SEÇİLMEDİ!");
            console.warn("[PraytClient] Radius Attack: Renk seçilmedi.");
            return;
        }

        const r = Math.max(1, state.prayt.radiusAttackR || 25);
        const r2 = r * r;
        state.target = { x: cx, y: cy };

        // 1-2 ms hızlı snapshot: binlerce pikseli tek Int32Array'de okur
        takeSnapshot({ x1: cx - r, y1: cy - r, x2: cx + r, y2: cy + r });

        const candidates = [];
        let skippedSameColor = 0;
        let skippedSea = 0;

        for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
            for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
                if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= r2) {
                    const x = cx + dx;
                    const y = cy + dy;

                    // 1. Deniz Renk Atla kontrolü
                    if (state.prayt.seaSkip && isCanvasSeaPixel(x, y)) {
                        skippedSea++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // 2. Elindeki renkle aynı olan pikselleri atla (aynı renklere şey yapmadan)
                    if (isPixelAlreadyColor(x, y, colorId)) {
                        skippedSameColor++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    candidates.push({ x, y, colorId, isRadiusAttack: true });
                }
            }
        }

        if (candidates.length === 0) {
            setStatus(`🎯 SALDIRI: TÜM ALAN ZATEN BU RENKTE (${skippedSameColor} atlandı)`);
            return;
        }

        // 3. Moderatörlere yakalatmayan akıcı insansı bilek ve kavis yolu (anti-scanline, anti-bot-grid)
        const queue = buildSingleColorOrganicPath(candidates, false);

        state.bot.running = true;
        state.bot.paused = false;
        state.bot.placed = 0;
        state.bot.skipped = skippedSameColor + skippedSea;
        state.bot.total = queue.length;
        state.bot.colorId = colorId;
        state.prayt.currentHandColor = colorId;
        state.prayt.statsStartTime = Date.now();
        state.bot.currentTaskType = "radius_attack";

        praytMxoEngine.stop();
        for (const p of queue) {
            praytMxoEngine.putPixel(p.x, p.y, colorId, {
                isRadiusAttack: true,
                strokePause: !!p.strokePause,
                isSlowCleanup: false
            });
        }
        praytMxoEngine.start();

        updateBotUI(true);
        setStatus(`🎯 SALDIRI: ${queue.length} px (R:${r})`);
    }

    // =========================================================
    // 🌑 VOİD MODU (DENİZ HARİÇ SİYAH ZEMİN + YAMUK BOYNUZLU KIRMIZI GÜLEN FACE)
    // =========================================================
    function praytToggleVoidMode(forceState = null) {
        if (!state.prayt) return;
        if (typeof forceState === "boolean") {
            state.prayt.voidModeClicking = forceState;
        } else {
            state.prayt.voidModeClicking = !state.prayt.voidModeClicking;
        }

        if (state.prayt.voidModeClicking) {
            if (state.areaSelecting) cancelAreaSelection();
            if (state.text.picking) state.text.picking = false;
            if (state.image.picking) state.image.picking = false;
            if (state.prayt.seaPicking) state.prayt.seaPicking = false;
            if (state.prayt.radiusAttackClicking) praytToggleClickAttack(false);
        }

        const shield = $("#w3x-select-shield");
        const help = $("#w3x-select-help");
        const btnTop = $("#w3x-top-void-mode");
        const btnPanel = $("#w3x-btn-void-mode");

        if (state.prayt.voidModeClicking) {
            if (shield) shield.classList.add("active");
            if (help) {
                help.textContent = "🌑 VOİD MODU: Haritada merkeze tıkla! (Deniz hariç etrafı simsiyah kaplayıp boynuzlu kırmızı gülen face çizer)";
                help.classList.add("active");
            }
            if (btnTop) btnTop.style.boxShadow = "0 0 16px #ff1744";
            if (btnPanel) btnPanel.style.boxShadow = "0 0 16px #ff1744";
            document.body.style.cursor = "crosshair";
            setStatus("🌑 VOİD: Haritada merkeze tıkla");
            if (typeof praytShowToast === "function") {
                praytShowToast("🌑 Void Modu: Haritada patlatmak istediğin merkeze tıkla!", "neon");
            }
        } else {
            if (shield && !state.text.picking && !state.image.picking && !state.prayt.seaPicking && !state.areaSelecting && !state.prayt.radiusAttackClicking) {
                shield.classList.remove("active");
            }
            if (help) help.classList.remove("active");
            if (btnTop) btnTop.style.boxShadow = "0 0 6px rgba(255,23,68,0.5)";
            if (btnPanel) btnPanel.style.boxShadow = "none";
            if (!state.text.picking && !state.image.picking && !state.prayt.seaPicking && !state.prayt.radiusAttackClicking) {
                document.body.style.cursor = "";
            }
        }
    }

    function praytExecuteVoidMode(cx, cy) {
        if (!state.wsReady) {
            setStatus("NO WS");
            return;
        }

        const palette = getPaletteColors();
        if (!palette || palette.length === 0) {
            setStatus("NO PALETTE");
            return;
        }

        const blackColorId = nearestPaletteId(0, 0, 0, palette);
        const redColorId = nearestPaletteId(227, 10, 23, palette);

        if (blackColorId === null || redColorId === null) {
            setStatus("❌ RENK BULUNAMADI!");
            return;
        }

        // Void Yarıçapı: r = 22px (~45x45 geniş karanlık bölge - hızlı ve akıcı insan fırçası)
        const r = Math.max(12, state.prayt.voidRadius || 22);
        const r2 = r * r;
        const x1 = cx - r, y1 = cy - r;
        const x2 = cx + r, y2 = cy + r;
        const w = x2 - x1 + 1;
        const h = y2 - y1 + 1;

        // Yeni Void çizimi başlarken eski koruma kilitlerini sıfırla ki kendi çizimine saldırmasın!
        state.bot.protect = false;
        state.bot.humanProtect = false;
        if (state.prayt) {
            state.prayt.protectImage = null;
            state.prayt.protectOrigin = null;
            state.prayt.realtimeProtect = false;
            state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect = false;
            state.prayt.godDefense = false;
        }
        if (typeof praytGodDefense !== "undefined") {
            praytGodDefense.active = false;
            praytGodDefense.syncButtons();
            praytGodDefense.updateUI();
        }

        state.savedArea = { x1, y1, x2, y2 };
        takeSnapshot({ x1, y1, x2, y2 }, true);

        const blackCandidates = [];
        let skippedSea = 0;
        let skippedSameBlack = 0;

        // --- KATMAN 1: YAMUK BOYNUZLU KIRMIZI GÜLEN FACE (İNSAN YAPMIŞ GİBİ ASİMETRİK) ---
        const faceCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        faceCanvas.width = w;
        faceCanvas.height = h;
        const fctx = faceCanvas.getContext("2d");

        fctx.strokeStyle = "#E30A17";
        fctx.fillStyle = "#E30A17";
        fctx.lineWidth = Math.max(2, Math.round(r * 0.085));
        fctx.lineCap = "round";
        fctx.lineJoin = "round";

        const fcx = r;
        const fcy = r;

        // 1. Sol Boynuz (Yamuk, yukarı-dışa kavisli ve sivri uçlu)
        fctx.beginPath();
        fctx.moveTo(fcx - r * 0.32, fcy - r * 0.34);
        fctx.bezierCurveTo(fcx - r * 0.48, fcy - r * 0.58, fcx - r * 0.58, fcy - r * 0.78, fcx - r * 0.46, fcy - r * 0.94);
        fctx.bezierCurveTo(fcx - r * 0.36, fcy - r * 0.80, fcx - r * 0.24, fcy - r * 0.56, fcx - r * 0.16, fcy - r * 0.36);
        fctx.closePath();
        fctx.fill();

        // 2. Sağ Boynuz (Daha dik ve hafif asimetrik - tam insan eliyle çizilmiş gibi yamuk!)
        fctx.beginPath();
        fctx.moveTo(fcx + r * 0.18, fcy - r * 0.35);
        fctx.bezierCurveTo(fcx + r * 0.36, fcy - r * 0.54, fcx + r * 0.62, fcy - r * 0.74, fcx + r * 0.56, fcy - r * 0.90);
        fctx.bezierCurveTo(fcx + r * 0.44, fcy - r * 0.76, fcx + r * 0.30, fcy - r * 0.56, fcx + r * 0.30, fcy - r * 0.33);
        fctx.closePath();
        fctx.fill();

        // 3. Sol Göz (Şeytani/troll kısık üçgen göz, hafif yamuk eğimli)
        fctx.beginPath();
        fctx.moveTo(fcx - r * 0.44, fcy - r * 0.10);
        fctx.lineTo(fcx - r * 0.18, fcy - r * 0.06);
        fctx.lineTo(fcx - r * 0.30, fcy - r * 0.24);
        fctx.closePath();
        fctx.fill();

        // 4. Sağ Göz (Hafif daha yukarıda ve farklı açılı yamuk göz)
        fctx.beginPath();
        fctx.moveTo(fcx + r * 0.16, fcy - r * 0.08);
        fctx.lineTo(fcx + r * 0.44, fcy - r * 0.14);
        fctx.lineTo(fcx + r * 0.28, fcy - r * 0.26);
        fctx.closePath();
        fctx.fill();

        // 5. Yamuk Çarpık Gülüş (Geniş kavisli, yukarı sırıtan devasa ağız)
        fctx.beginPath();
        fctx.lineWidth = Math.max(2, Math.round(r * 0.09));
        fctx.moveTo(fcx - r * 0.54, fcy + r * 0.18);
        fctx.bezierCurveTo(fcx - r * 0.32, fcy + r * 0.58, fcx + r * 0.14, fcy + r * 0.62, fcx + r * 0.58, fcy + r * 0.14);
        fctx.stroke();

        // 6. Gülümseme Kenar Çizgileri (Gamzeler / Sırıtış Köşeleri)
        fctx.beginPath();
        fctx.moveTo(fcx - r * 0.56, fcy + r * 0.10);
        fctx.lineTo(fcx - r * 0.46, fcy + r * 0.25);
        fctx.moveTo(fcx + r * 0.52, fcy + r * 0.06);
        fctx.lineTo(fcx + r * 0.62, fcy + r * 0.22);
        fctx.stroke();

        // 7. Yamuk Sivri Dişler (Ağızdan aşağı sarkan sivri dişler)
        fctx.beginPath();
        fctx.moveTo(fcx - r * 0.24, fcy + r * 0.38);
        fctx.lineTo(fcx - r * 0.18, fcy + r * 0.52);
        fctx.lineTo(fcx - r * 0.12, fcy + r * 0.42);
        fctx.moveTo(fcx + r * 0.14, fcy + r * 0.44);
        fctx.lineTo(fcx + r * 0.20, fcy + r * 0.56);
        fctx.lineTo(fcx + r * 0.26, fcy + r * 0.45);
        fctx.fill();

        const faceData = fctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h).data;
        const redCandidates = [];
        const targetData = new Int16Array(w * h);

        // Target map hazırlığı (Katman 0 siyah zemin + Katman 1 kırmızı yüz birbirine çakışmadan ayrılır!)
        for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
            for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
                const rx = x1 + dx;
                const ry = y1 + dy;
                const fidx = (dy * w + dx) * 4;

                if (state.prayt.seaSkip && isCanvasSeaPixel(rx, ry)) {
                    targetData[dy * w + dx] = -1;
                    skippedSea++;
                    continue;
                }

                const distSq = (dx - r) * (dx - r) + (dy - r) * (dy - r);
                if (distSq > r2) {
                    targetData[dy * w + dx] = -1;
                    continue;
                }

                const isRedPixel = faceData[fidx + 3] > 80;
                const expColor = isRedPixel ? redColorId : blackColorId;
                targetData[dy * w + dx] = expColor;

                if (isRedPixel) {
                    if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(rx, ry, redColorId)) {
                        redCandidates.push({ x: rx, y: ry, colorId: redColorId, layer: 1, isLayered: true, forcePlace: true });
                    }
                } else {
                    if (isPixelAlreadyColor(rx, ry, blackColorId)) {
                        skippedSameBlack++;
                    } else {
                        blackCandidates.push({ x: rx, y: ry, colorId: blackColorId, layer: 0, isLayered: true });
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (blackCandidates.length === 0 && redCandidates.length === 0) {
            setStatus("🌑 VOİD: ALAN ZATEN TAM");
            return;
        }

        // İNSANSI ORGANİK YOL VE SIRALAMA:
        // Katman 0: Önce tüm siyah zemin pikselleri insan bilek darbeleriyle boyanır
        const orderedBlack = buildSingleColorOrganicPath(blackCandidates, false);

        // Katman 1: Siyah zemin bittikten sonra insan gibi palete gidip kırmızı seçer
        const orderedRed = buildSingleColorOrganicPath(redCandidates, false);
        if (orderedRed.length > 0) {
            orderedRed[0] = { ...orderedRed[0], colorSwitchPause: true };
        }

        const finalQueue = [...orderedBlack, ...orderedRed];

        state.prayt.areaTargetMap = {
            x1, y1, x2, y2, w, h,
            data: targetData,
            type: 'void_mode'
        };
        updatePraytProtectInfo();

        state.bot.running = true;
        state.bot.paused = false;
        state.bot.placed = 0;
        state.bot.skipped = skippedSameBlack;
        state.bot.total = finalQueue.length;
        state.bot.currentTaskType = "void_mode";
        state.prayt.statsStartTime = Date.now();

        praytMxoEngine.stop();
        for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
            for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
                const expCid = targetData[dy * w + dx];
                if (expCid >= 0) {
                    const rx = x1 + dx;
                    const ry = y1 + dy;
                    if (isPixelAlreadyColor(rx, ry, expCid)) {
                        praytMxoEngine.drawnPixels.set(`${rx},${ry}`, expCid);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        for (const p of finalQueue) {
            praytMxoEngine.putPixel(p.x, p.y, p.colorId, {
                isSlowCleanup: false,
                isHumanDefense: false,
                isVoidStroke: true,
                strokePause: !!p.strokePause,
                colorSwitchPause: !!p.colorSwitchPause,
                forcePlace: !!p.forcePlace
            });
        }
        praytMxoEngine.start();

        updateBotUI(true);
        setStatus(`🌑 VOİD MODU: ${finalQueue.length} px (Siyah Void + Kırmızı Yüz)`);
        if (typeof praytShowToast === "function") {
            praytShowToast(`🌑 Void Modu Başlatıldı! (${finalQueue.length} px - Deniz korundu)`, "neon");
        }
        if (typeof praytAudio !== "undefined") praytAudio.playStart();
    }

    // =========================================================
    // TRUE MXO BOT REALTIME PRIORITY DEFENSE & ANTI-RATELIMIT TURBO ENGINE
    // =========================================================

    
    // =========================================================
    // ⚡ İMKÂNSIZ SAVUNMA (IMPOSSIBLE GOD DEFENSE ENGINE)
    // Sub-millisecond Reflection + Adaptive Token Bucket Anti-RateLimit
    // =========================================================
    // ⚡ UNTHROTTLED WEB WORKER TIMER (SEKME ARKA PLANDA OLSA BİLE HIZ DÜŞMEZ)
    const praytFastTimer = (() => {
        try {
            const blob = new Blob([`
                const timers = new Map();
                self.onmessage = function(e) {
                    const d = e.data;
                    if (d.cmd === 'set') {
                        const tid = setTimeout(() => {
                            timers.delete(d.id);
                            self.postMessage(d.id);
                        }, d.ms);
                        timers.set(d.id, tid);
                    } else if (d.cmd === 'clear') {
                        const tid = timers.get(d.id);
                        if (tid !== undefined) {
                            clearTimeout(tid);
                            timers.delete(d.id);
                        }
                    }
                };
            `], { type: 'application/javascript' });
            const worker = new Worker(URL.createObjectURL(blob));
            let nextId = 1;
            const cbs = new Map();
            worker.onmessage = (e) => {
                const cb = cbs.get(e.data);
                if (cb) {
                    cbs.delete(e.data);
                    cb();
                }
            };
            return {
                setTimeout(fn, ms) {
                    const id = nextId++;
                    cbs.set(id, fn);
                    worker.postMessage({ cmd: 'set', id, ms: Math.max(1, Math.round(ms || 1)) });
                    return id;
                },
                clearTimeout(id) {
                    if (id) {
                        cbs.delete(id);
                        worker.postMessage({ cmd: 'clear', id });
                    }
                }
            };
        } catch (_) {
            return {
                setTimeout: (fn, ms) => window.setTimeout(fn, ms),
                clearTimeout: (id) => window.clearTimeout(id)
            };
        }
    })();

        // 👥 PRAYT SWARM (TEK HESAP UYUMLU STUB)
    const praytSwarm = {
        swarmCount: 1,
