        init: () => {},
        shouldHandlePixel: () => true,
        broadcastHumanDefense: () => {},
        broadcastDefenseState: () => {},
        updateBadge: () => {}
    };

        // ⚡ PRAYT ALT SOCKET (REMOVED - SINGLE ACCOUNT ONLY)
    const praytAltSocket = {
        connected: false,
        canSend: () => false,
        sendPixel: () => false,
        disconnect: () => {}
    };

    // =========================================================================
    // 🛡️ PRAYT HUMAN DUO ENGINE (MODLARA BELLİ ETMEYEN HIZLI İNSANSI FIRÇA SİSTEMİ)
    // =========================================================================
    const praytHumanDuo = {
        active: false,
        pendingGrief: new Map(), // key -> { x, y, colorId, ts }
        processTimer: null,
        strokeTimer: null,
        isStroking: false,
        lastStrokeColor: null,
        lastRepairActivityTs: 0,
        pixelsInCurrentStroke: 0,
        counteredCount: 0,

        toggle(forceState = null) {
            const next = (forceState !== null) ? forceState : !this.active;
            this.active = next;
            state.bot.humanProtect = next;

            if (next) {
                if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined' && praytGodDefense.active) {
                    praytGodDefense.toggle(false);
                }
                if (!state.savedArea) {
                    praytShowToast('⚠️ İnsansı Savunma için haritada alan seçin!', 'warn');
                    setStatus('ALAN SEÇİN (İNSANSI)');
                    beginAreaSelection();
                } else {
                    const area = state.savedArea;
                    const cur = state.prayt.areaTargetMap;
                    const matchesArea = cur && cur.x1 === area.x1 && cur.y1 === area.y1 && cur.x2 === area.x2 && cur.y2 === area.y2 && cur.data;
                    if (!matchesArea) {
                        captureCanvasAreaToTargetMap(area);
                    }
                    ensureAreaTargetMap();
                    setStatus('🛡️ İNSANSI NÖBETTE (AKICI FIRÇA KORUMASI)');
                }
                praytAudio.playStart();
                praytShowToast('🛡️ İNSANSI KORUMA AKTİF! (Hızlı Akıcı Fırça • Mod Gözü Dolanmaz)', 'neon');
            } else {
                this.pendingGrief.clear();
                if (this.processTimer) clearTimeout(this.processTimer);
                if (this.strokeTimer) clearTimeout(this.strokeTimer);
                this.processTimer = null;
                this.strokeTimer = null;
                this.isStroking = false;
                praytAudio.playStop();
                praytShowToast('🛡️ İnsansı Mod Kapatıldı', 'warn');
                setStatus('CONNECTED');
            }

            this.syncButtons();
            this.updateUI();

            if (typeof praytSwarm !== 'undefined') {
                praytSwarm.broadcastHumanDefense(this.active, state.savedArea, (state.prayt ? state.prayt.areaTargetMap : null));
            }
        },

        onPixelAttacked(x, y, attackerColor, expectedColorId) {
            if (!this.active) return false;
            if (expectedColorId === null || expectedColorId === undefined || expectedColorId < 0) return false;
            if (attackerColor === expectedColorId) return false;

            if (typeof praytTriggerRaidDetection === 'function') praytTriggerRaidDetection(x, y);
            praytRecordAttack(x, y, attackerColor, expectedColorId);

            const key = `${x},${y}`;
            const now = performance.now();

            if (typeof praytSwarm !== 'undefined' && !praytSwarm.shouldHandlePixel(x, y)) {
                return true;
            }

            this.pendingGrief.set(key, { x, y, colorId: expectedColorId, expectedColorId, ts: now });

            // Gerçek PixelPlace Oyuncusu Refleksi:
            // - Eğer oyuncunun eli zaten son 2.5 saniye içinde tuvalin üzerindeyse (Space/Sol Tık basılı tutup fırça gezdiriyorsa):
            //   42ms - 78ms içinde yeni bozulan pikselleri de aynı fırça darbesinin devamına katar!
            // - Eğer ilk saldırıysa (bekleme halindeyse): 145ms - 225ms doğal insan görsel algı penceresi bırakır,
            //   bu sürede gelen pikselleri toplar ve tek tek tıklamak yerine BİRBİRİNE BAĞLI HIZLI FIRÇA SÜRÜKLEMESİYLE süpürür!
            if (!this.processTimer) {
                const isHandAlreadyWarm = (now - this.lastRepairActivityTs) < 2500;
                const reactionDelay = isHandAlreadyWarm
                    ? Math.floor(Math.random() * 36 + 42)
                    : Math.floor(Math.random() * 80 + 145);
                this.processTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                    this.processTimer = null;
                    this.executeHumanStroke();
                }, reactionDelay);
            }

            this.updateUI();
            return true;
        },

        executeHumanStroke() {
            if (!this.active || this.pendingGrief.size === 0) {
                this.isStroking = false;
                return;
            }

            this.isStroking = true;
            this.lastRepairActivityTs = performance.now();

            const rawList = Array.from(this.pendingGrief.values());
            this.pendingGrief.clear();

            // 1. Önce eldeki rengi %100 bitir, 2. Her rengi 8-komşuluklu Anti-Orphan akıcı fırça darbesiyle sırala
            const organicQueue = buildOrganicHumanPath(rawList, true);
            for (let i = 0; i < organicQueue.length; i++) {
                const p = organicQueue[i];
                const cid = p.colorId !== undefined ? p.colorId : p.expectedColorId;
                praytMxoEngine.putPixel(p.x, p.y, cid, {
                    forcePlace: true,
                    isSlowCleanup: true,
                    isHumanDefense: true,
                    turnPause: !!p.turnPause,
                    strokePause: !!p.strokePause,
                    colorSwitchPause: !!p.colorSwitchPause,
                    strokeStep: p.strokeStep || i
                });
            }

            this.counteredCount += organicQueue.length;
            praytMxoEngine.start();
            this.isStroking = false;
            this.updateUI();
            setStatus(`🛡️ İNSANSI FIRÇA ONARIM (${organicQueue.length} px)`);
        },

        syncButtons() {
            const btns = [
                document.getElementById('w3x-top-human-protect'),
                document.getElementById('w3x-prayt-human-protect-toggle'),
                document.getElementById('w3x-bot-human-protect-toggle')
            ];
            btns.forEach(btn => {
                if (!btn) return;
                if (btn.id === 'w3x-top-human-protect') {
                    btn.textContent = this.active ? '🛡️ İNSANSI: ON' : '🛡️ İNSANSI KORUMA';
                } else {
                    btn.textContent = this.active ? '🛡️ İNSANSI KORUMA: ON' : '🛡️ İNSANSI KORUMA: OFF';
                }
                btn.style.background = this.active ? 'linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(255,126,187,0.35), rgba(124,77,255,0.35))' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)';
                btn.style.borderColor = this.active ? '#ff7ebb' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)';
                btn.style.color = this.active ? '#ff7ebb' : '#ccc';
                btn.style.boxShadow = this.active ? '0 0 14px rgba(255,126,187,0.45)' : 'none';
            });
        },

        updateUI() {
            const info = document.getElementById('w3x-prayt-protect-info');
            if (info && this.active) {
                if (this.pendingGrief.size > 0) {
                    info.textContent = `🛡️ İnsansı Fırça: ${this.pendingGrief.size} piksel akıcı darbeyle süpürülüyor...`;
                    info.style.color = '#ff7ebb';
                } else {
                    info.textContent = `🛡️ İnsansı Koruma Aktif (${this.counteredCount} piksel modlara çaktırmadan onarıldı)`;
                    info.style.color = '#69f0ae';
                }
            }
        }
    };

    // =========================================================
    // 🚨 RAID DETECTION & ALARM SYSTEM
    // =========================================================
    function praytTriggerRaidDetection(x, y) {
        if (!state.prayt) return;
        // Yalnızca kullanıcı savunmayı AÇIK tuttuğunda raid koruması devreye girer. Saldırırken veya normal çizim yaparken asla tetiklenmez!
        const isDefenseOn = (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined' && praytGodDefense.active) || state.bot.protect || state.bot.humanProtect;
        if (!isDefenseOn || state.bot.mode === 'radius_attack' || state.bot.mode === 'nuke') return;
        if (state.bot.running && !state.bot.protect && !state.bot.humanProtect) return;

        // Yalnızca bizim koruma alanımızın İÇİNDEKİ piksellere gelen saldırılarda raid tetiklenir!
        if (state.savedArea) {
            if (x < state.savedArea.x1 || x > state.savedArea.x2 || y < state.savedArea.y1 || y > state.savedArea.y2) {
                return;
            }
        } else if (state.prayt.protectImage && state.prayt.protectOrigin) {
            const ox = state.prayt.protectOrigin.x;
            const oy = state.prayt.protectOrigin.y;
            const pw = state.prayt.protectImage.width;
            const ph = state.prayt.protectImage.height;
            if (x < ox || x >= ox + pw || y < oy || y >= oy + ph) {
                return;
            }
        } else {
            return;
        }

        const now = performance.now();
        if (!state.prayt.raidWindowStart || (now - state.prayt.raidWindowStart > 700)) {
            state.prayt.raidWindowStart = now;
            state.prayt.raidCounter = 1;
        } else {
            state.prayt.raidCounter = (state.prayt.raidCounter || 0) + 1;
        }

        if (state.prayt.raidCounter >= 2 && !state.prayt.raidModeActive) {
            state.prayt.raidModeActive = true;
            // Sessiz turbo hız takviyesi - ekranda rahatsız edici toast basmaz!
            const godInfo = document.getElementById('w3x-god-protect-info');
            if (godInfo) {
                godInfo.textContent = '🚨 YOĞUN SALDIRI PÜSKÜRTÜLÜYOR (Turbo Refleks)';
                godInfo.style.color = '#ff5252';
            }
        }

        if (state.prayt.raidModeActive) {
            if (state.prayt.raidModeTimer) clearTimeout(state.prayt.raidModeTimer);
            state.prayt.raidModeTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                if (state.prayt) state.prayt.raidModeActive = false;
                if (state.prayt) state.prayt.raidModeTimer = null;
            }, 5000);
        }
    }

    // =========================================================
    // 👻 SİTE TAKİP VE CİHAZ İZLERİNİ TEMİZLEME MOTORU (STEALTH WIPE)
    // =========================================================
    function praytWipeTrackingData() {
        try {
            let cleared = 0;
            const keysToRemove = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {
                const key = localStorage.key(i);
                if (!key) continue;
                // PraytClient kendi ayarlarını ve favorilerini koru, geri kalan her şeyi sil
                if (key.startsWith('prayt_') || key.startsWith('w3x_') || key.startsWith('__PRAYT')) continue;
                keysToRemove.push(key);
            }
            for (const k of keysToRemove) {
                localStorage.removeItem(k);
                cleared++;
            }
            if (typeof sessionStorage !== 'undefined') {
                sessionStorage.clear();
            }
            // Erişilebilir çerezleri temizle
            try {
                const cookies = document.cookie.split(";");
                for (let i = 0; i < cookies.length; i++) {
                    const cookie = cookies[i];
                    const eqPos = cookie.indexOf("=");
                    const name = eqPos > -1 ? cookie.substr(0, eqPos).trim() : cookie.trim();
                    document.cookie = name + "=;expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 GMT;path=/";
                }
            } catch (_) {}

            praytAudio.playSuccess();
            praytShowToast(`👻 ${cleared} adet takip anahtarı ve cihaz izi silindi! Sayfayı yenileyebilirsiniz.`, 'info');
            setStatus('👻 İZLER SİLİNDİ');
        } catch (e) {
            praytShowToast('İzler silinirken hata: ' + e.message, 'danger');
        }
    }

    const praytGodDefense = {
        active: false,
        counteredCount: 0,
        inFlightMap: new Map(),
        _lastUiUpdateAt: 0,

        intercept(x, y, attackerColor, expectedColorId) {
            const isDrawingNow = !!(typeof praytMxoEngine !== 'undefined' && praytMxoEngine.isDrawing());
            const isBalancedOn = isDrawingNow && !(state.prayt && state.prayt.noProtectWhileDrawing);
            const isProtected = this.active || state.bot.protect || (state.prayt && state.prayt.realtimeProtect) || isBalancedOn;
            if (!isProtected || state.bot.mode === 'radius_attack' || state.bot.mode === 'nuke') return false;
            if (state.prayt && state.prayt.noProtectWhileDrawing && isDrawingNow) return false;
            if (expectedColorId === null || expectedColorId === undefined || expectedColorId < 0) return false;
            if (attackerColor === expectedColorId) return false;

            const key = `${x},${y}`;
            const now = performance.now();

            // 1. ⚡ V8 FORTRESS 0.00ms WS-ÖNCELİKLİ ANLIK REFLEKS ATEŞİ:
            // Hiçbir DOM/Tablo/Ses/Token beklemesi yapmadan ÖNCE, paketi geldiği milisaniyenin içinde doğrudan WebSocket'e çak!
            let firedDirect = false;
            if (typeof praytMxoEngine !== 'undefined') {
                const canDirectFire = state.ws && state.wsReady && state.ws.readyState === 1 &&
                    now >= (praytMxoEngine.rateLimitBackoffUntil || 0) &&
                    _wsPixelTimestamps.length < 59;

                if (canDirectFire) {
                    const sentNow = wsSend('p', `[${x},${y},${expectedColorId},1]`, true);
                    if (sentNow) {
                        firedDirect = true;
                        markPlaced(x, y, expectedColorId);
                        praytMxoEngine.inFlightMap.set(key, { colorId: expectedColorId, ts: now });
                        praytMxoEngine.drawnPixels.set(key, expectedColorId);
                        if (praytMxoEngine.botSentColors) praytMxoEngine.botSentColors.set(key, expectedColorId);
                        // Çizim yapmıyorsak 130ms sonra tuvali tekrar kontrol et (2-3 kişi aynı anda basarsa anında tekrar ezer!)
                        if (!isDrawingNow) {
                            praytMxoEngine.verifyMap.set(key, { x, y, colorId: expectedColorId, checkAt: now + 130 });
                        }
                        praytMxoEngine.recentSentRing.push({ x, y, colorId: expectedColorId, ts: now });
                        if (praytMxoEngine.recentSentRing.length > 16) praytMxoEngine.recentSentRing.shift();
                        if (praytMxoEngine.priorityTaskKeys.has(key)) {
                            praytMxoEngine.priorityTaskKeys.delete(key);
                            praytMxoEngine.priorityTasks = praytMxoEngine.priorityTasks.filter(t => t[3] !== key);
                        }
                        state.bot.placed++;
                        state.prayt.totalPlacedSession = (state.prayt.totalPlacedSession || 0) + 1;
                        if (!state.prayt.placedTimestamps) state.prayt.placedTimestamps = [];
                        state.prayt.placedTimestamps.push(now);
                        this.inFlightMap.set(key, now);
                        this.counteredCount++;
                    }
                }

                if (!firedDirect) {
                    // Kuyruk başına koy ve 0.00ms beklemesiz ateşle!
                    if (!isDrawingNow) {
                        praytMxoEngine.verifyMap.set(key, { x, y, colorId: expectedColorId, checkAt: now + 140 });
                    }
                    praytMxoEngine.putPixelWithPriority(x, y, expectedColorId, { forcePlace: true, isPriorityDefense: true });
                    this.inFlightMap.set(key, now);
                    this.counteredCount++;
                }
            }

            // 2. Paket ateşlendikten SONRA Raid & Grief Radar kaydını yap (DOM Throttled - Sıfır Gecikme)
            if (!state.bot.running && (this.active || state.bot.protect || (state.prayt && state.prayt.realtimeProtect) || state.bot.humanProtect)) {
                praytTriggerRaidDetection(x, y);
            }
            praytRecordAttack(x, y, attackerColor, expectedColorId);

            if (now - this._lastUiUpdateAt > 90) {
                this._lastUiUpdateAt = now;
                this.updateUI();
                if (firedDirect) updateBotUI();
            }
            return true;
        },

        onRateLimitSignal(cooldownMs = 0) {
            if (typeof praytMxoEngine !== 'undefined') {
                praytMxoEngine.onRateLimitSignal(cooldownMs);
            }
        },

        toggle(forceState = null) {
            const isCurrentlyOn = !!(this.active || state.bot.protect || (state.prayt && state.prayt.realtimeProtect));
            const next = (forceState !== null) ? forceState : !isCurrentlyOn;
            this.active = next;
            state.prayt.godDefense = next;

            if (next) {
                state.bot.protect = true;
                state.bot.humanProtect = false;
                if (state.prayt) {
                    state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect = false;
                    if (state.prayt.protectImage && state.prayt.protectOrigin) {
                        state.prayt.realtimeProtect = true;
                    }
                }
                if (typeof praytHumanDuo !== 'undefined' && praytHumanDuo.active) {
                    praytHumanDuo.active = false;
                    if (typeof praytHumanDuo.syncButtons === 'function') praytHumanDuo.syncButtons();
                }

                if (!state.savedArea && !(state.prayt && state.prayt.protectImage && state.prayt.protectOrigin)) {
                    praytShowToast('⚠️ Savunulacak alanı haritada seçin!', 'warn');
                    setStatus('ALAN SEÇİN (SAVUNMA)');
                    beginAreaSelection();
                } else {
                    if (state.savedArea) {
                        const area = state.savedArea;
                        const cur = state.prayt.areaTargetMap;
                        const matchesArea = cur && cur.x1 === area.x1 && cur.y1 === area.y1 && cur.x2 === area.x2 && cur.y2 === area.y2 && cur.data;
                        if (!matchesArea) {
                            captureCanvasAreaToTargetMap(area);
                        }
                        ensureAreaTargetMap();
                        praytMxoEngine.markAreaAsFullyDrawn();
                        scanAndQueueDamagedColorPixels();
                    }
                    if (state.prayt && state.prayt.protectImage && state.prayt.protectOrigin) {
                        scanAndQueueDamagedImagePixels();
                    }
                    praytMxoEngine.start();
                    setStatus('⚡ MXO / İMKÂNSIZ SAVUNMA AKTİF');
                }
                praytAudio.playStart();
                praytShowToast('⚡ MXO İMKÂNSIZ KALE SAVUNMASI AKTİF! (0.00ms 25x Multi-Shot)', 'neon');
            } else {
                this.active = false;
                state.prayt.godDefense = false;
                state.bot.protect = false;
                if (state.prayt) {
                    state.prayt.realtimeProtect = false;
                }
                if (typeof praytMxoEngine !== 'undefined') {
                    praytMxoEngine.priorityTasks = [];
                    praytMxoEngine.priorityTaskKeys.clear();
                    praytMxoEngine.inFlightMap.clear();
                    praytMxoEngine.verifyMap.clear();
                    if (!state.bot.running) {
                        praytMxoEngine.stop();
                    }
                }
                praytAudio.playStop();
                praytShowToast('🛡️ MXO / İmkânsız Savunma Kapatıldı', 'warn');
                setStatus(state.bot.running ? 'BOTTING' : 'CONNECTED');
            }

            this.syncButtons();
            if (typeof _globalSyncAreaProtectUI === 'function') _globalSyncAreaProtectUI();
            if (typeof syncImageProtectToggles === 'function') syncImageProtectToggles();
            updatePraytProtectInfo();
            this.updateUI();
        },

        syncButtons() {
            if (typeof _globalSyncAreaProtectUI === 'function') _globalSyncAreaProtectUI();
            if (typeof syncImageProtectToggles === 'function') syncImageProtectToggles();
        },

        updateUI() {
            const isAnyMxoOn = !!(this.active || state.bot.protect || (state.prayt && state.prayt.realtimeProtect));
            const statusEl = document.getElementById('w3x-god-protect-info');
            if (statusEl) {
                if (!isAnyMxoOn) {
                    statusEl.textContent = 'İmkânsız Savunma kapalı. Açmak için yukarıdaki butona tıklayın.';
                    statusEl.style.color = '#888';
                } else {
                    statusEl.textContent = `🛡️ İMKÂNSIZ KALE AKTİF: ${this.counteredCount} saldırı 0.00ms hızla ezildi! (Kapatmak için butona bas)`;
                    statusEl.style.color = '#00e5ff';
                    if (this.counteredCount > 0 && !state.bot.running) {
                        setStatus(`⚡ MXO SAVUNMA (${this.counteredCount} ezildi)`);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    };

    const praytMxoEngine = {
        tasks: [],             // [ [x, y, colorId, key, meta], ... ]
        priorityTasks: [],     // Instant priority defense tasks
        taskKeys: new Set(),
        priorityTaskKeys: new Set(), // Prevents duplicate defense packets in priorityTasks
        drawnPixels: new Map(),      // key -> colorId (Tracks pixels ALREADY drawn so balanced mode only protects drawn area!)
        botSentColors: new Map(),    // key -> colorId (Tracks every pixel sent by the bot during current session)
        verifyMap: new Map(),        // key -> { x, y, colorId, checkAt } (Double-Tap Verification Shield)
        attackTimestamps: [],        // Sliding 2000ms window of enemy attacks on our drawn area
        _balanceCounter: 0,          // Interleaving counter for Balanced Draw-Protect Mode
        _consecutiveDefenses: 0,     // Counts consecutive priority defense repairs
        _lastDrawPatrolAt: 0,        // Timestamp of last live drawnPixels patrol while drawing
        inFlightMap: new Map(),      // key -> { colorId, ts }
        recentSentRing: [],          // Last sent pixels for recovery
        head: 0,
        timer: null,
        running: false,
        lastPlacedColorId: null,
        nextAllowedAt: 0,
        rateLimitBackoffUntil: 0,
        consecutiveRateLimits: 0,
        lastRateLimitTime: 0,
        serverRateLimit: 60,
        safeMinInterval: 16.6,
        tokens: 2.0,
        lastTokenRefill: 0,
        refillIntervalMs: 16.6,
        maxBurstTokens: 2.0,

        isDrawing() {
            return !!(state.bot && state.bot.running && this.tasks && this.head < this.tasks.length);
        },

        recordEnemyHit(now = performance.now()) {
            this.attackTimestamps.push(now);
            while (this.attackTimestamps.length > 0 && (now - this.attackTimestamps[0]) > 2000) {
                this.attackTimestamps.shift();
            }
        },

        getRecentAttackCount(now = performance.now()) {
            while (this.attackTimestamps.length > 0 && (now - this.attackTimestamps[0]) > 2000) {
                this.attackTimestamps.shift();
            }
            return this.attackTimestamps.length;
        },

        markAreaAsFullyDrawn() {
            const atm = state.prayt && state.prayt.areaTargetMap;
            if (atm && atm.data) {
                for (let dy = 0; dy < atm.h; dy++) {
                    for (let dx = 0; dx < atm.w; dx++) {
                        const cid = atm.data[dy * atm.w + dx];
                        if (cid >= 0) {
                            this.drawnPixels.set(`${atm.x1 + dx},${atm.y1 + dy}`, cid);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        },

        scanDamagedDrawnPixels(now = performance.now()) {
            if (!this.drawnPixels || this.drawnPixels.size === 0) return;
            // Çizim devam ederken <canvas> okuması yapma (WebSocket 0ms'de gerçek saldırıyı zaten yakalar; canvas gecikmesi kendi piksellerimizi bozuk sanıp kendi kendini korumaya yol açar!)
            if (this.isDrawing()) return;
            const isAttackTask = (state.bot.currentTaskType === 'radius_attack' || state.bot.mode === 'radius_attack' || state.bot.mode === 'nuke');
            if (isAttackTask) return;
            if (typeof takeSnapshot === 'function') {
                takeSnapshot();
            }
            let directFired = 0;
            for (const [key, expColorId] of this.drawnPixels.entries()) {
                if (expColorId === null || expColorId === undefined || expColorId < 0) continue;
                if (this.priorityTaskKeys.has(key)) continue;
                const inflight = this.inFlightMap.get(key);
                if (inflight && (now - inflight.ts) < 1500) continue;

                const comma = key.indexOf(',');
                if (comma < 0) continue;
                const x = parseInt(key.slice(0, comma), 10);
                const y = parseInt(key.slice(comma + 1), 10);
                if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(x, y, expColorId)) {
                    if (directFired < 12 && state.ws && state.wsReady && state.ws.readyState === 1 &&
                        now >= (this.rateLimitBackoffUntil || 0) && _wsPixelTimestamps.length < 58) {
                        const sentImmediate = wsSend('p', `[${x},${y},${expColorId},1]`, true);
                        if (sentImmediate) {
                            directFired++;
                            markPlaced(x, y, expColorId);
                            this.inFlightMap.set(key, { colorId: expColorId, ts: now });
                            this.verifyMap.set(key, { x, y, colorId: expColorId, checkAt: now + 150 });
                            this.recentSentRing.push({ x, y, colorId: expColorId, ts: now });
                            if (this.recentSentRing.length > 16) this.recentSentRing.shift();
                            state.bot.placed++;
                            state.prayt.totalPlacedSession = (state.prayt.totalPlacedSession || 0) + 1;
                            if (!state.prayt.placedTimestamps) state.prayt.placedTimestamps = [];
                            state.prayt.placedTimestamps.push(now);
                            continue;
                        }
                    }
                    this.priorityTaskKeys.add(key);
                    this.taskKeys.add(key);
                    this.priorityTasks.push([x, y, expColorId, key, { forcePlace: true, isPriorityDefense: true }]);
                }
            }
        },

        onRateChange(newRate) {
            const num = Number(newRate);
            if (!isNaN(num) && num > 0) {
                this.serverRateLimit = num;
                this.safeMinInterval = Math.max(4.0, 1000 / num);
                console.log(`[PraytClient] Sunucu hız limiti güncellendi: ${num} px/s (Aralık: ${this.safeMinInterval}ms)`);
            }
        },

        putPixel(x, y, colorId, meta = null) {
            const key = `${x},${y}`;
            if (meta && meta.forcePlace) {
                this.taskKeys.add(key);
                this.tasks.push([x, y, colorId, key, meta]);
            } else {
                if (this.taskKeys.has(key)) return;
                this.taskKeys.add(key);
                this.tasks.push([x, y, colorId, key, meta]);
            }
            if (this.running) {
                this.ensureRunning();
            }
        },

        clearClockTasks() {
            this.tasks = [];
            this.taskKeys.clear();
            this.priorityTaskKeys.clear();
            this.inFlightMap.clear();
            this.verifyMap.clear();
            this.head = 0;
        },

        putPixelWithPriority(x, y, colorId, meta = null) {
            if (state.prayt && state.prayt.noProtectWhileDrawing && this.isDrawing()) return;
            const key = `${x},${y}`;
            this.inFlightMap.delete(key);
            // Zaten öncelikli savunma kuyruğundaysa mükerrer ekleme yapma, ama en başa (1. sıraya) çek!
            if (this.priorityTaskKeys.has(key)) {
                for (let i = 0; i < this.priorityTasks.length; i++) {
                    if (this.priorityTasks[i][3] === key) {
                        this.priorityTasks[i][2] = colorId;
                        if (i > 0) {
                            const item = this.priorityTasks.splice(i, 1)[0];
                            this.priorityTasks.unshift(item);
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                }
                this.nextAllowedAt = 0;
                if (this.timer) {
                    praytFastTimer.clearTimeout(this.timer);
                    this.timer = null;
                }
                if (!this._inTick) {
                    this.tick();
                }
                return;
            }
            this.priorityTaskKeys.add(key);
            this.taskKeys.add(key);
            const prioMeta = Object.assign({}, meta || {}, { forcePlace: true, isPriorityDefense: true });
            this.priorityTasks.push([x, y, colorId, key, prioMeta]);
            this.running = true;
            // ⚡ 0.00ms ANLIK MXO REFLEKS: Normal çizim bekleme süresini (nextAllowedAt) ANINDA sıfırla ve aynı milisaniyede ateşle!
            this.nextAllowedAt = 0;

            if (this.timer) {
                praytFastTimer.clearTimeout(this.timer);
                this.timer = null;
            }
            if (!this._inTick) {
                this.tick();
            } else {
                this.timer = praytFastTimer.setTimeout(() => this.tick(), 1);
            }
        },

        onRateLimitSignal(explicitCooldownMs = 0) {
            const now = performance.now();
            if (now - this.lastRateLimitTime > 30000) {
                this.consecutiveRateLimits = 0;
            }
            this.consecutiveRateLimits++;
            this.lastRateLimitTime = now;
            this.tokens = 0;

            let backoffMs = explicitCooldownMs > 0 ? Math.min(explicitCooldownMs, 1200) : (
                this.consecutiveRateLimits === 1 ? 350 :
                this.consecutiveRateLimits === 2 ? 650 : 950
            );

            this.rateLimitBackoffUntil = Math.max(this.rateLimitBackoffUntil, now + backoffMs);
            this.nextAllowedAt = this.rateLimitBackoffUntil;

            if (this.recentSentRing && this.recentSentRing.length > 0) {
                while (this.recentSentRing.length > 0) {
                    const dropped = this.recentSentRing.pop();
                    const k = `${dropped.x},${dropped.y}`;
                    this.inFlightMap.delete(k);
                    this.drawnPixels.set(k, dropped.colorId);
                    if (!this.priorityTaskKeys.has(k)) {
                        this.priorityTaskKeys.add(k);
                        this.taskKeys.add(k);
                        this.priorityTasks.unshift([dropped.x, dropped.y, dropped.colorId, k, { forcePlace: true, isPriorityDefense: true }]);
                    }
                }
            }

            if (this.timer) {
                praytFastTimer.clearTimeout(this.timer);
                this.timer = null;
            }
            if (this.running || (state.bot && state.bot.running)) {
                this.timer = praytFastTimer.setTimeout(() => this.tick(), backoffMs + 10);
            }
        },

        ensureRunning() {
            if (this.timer || this._inTick) return;
            this.tick();
        },

        start() {
            this.running = true;
            if (this.lastPlacedColorId === null) {
                const hand = getSelectedColorId();
                if (hand !== null) this.lastPlacedColorId = hand;
            }
            this.ensureRunning();
        },

        stop() {
            this.running = false;
            if (this.timer) {
                praytFastTimer.clearTimeout(this.timer);
                this.timer = null;
            }
            this.tasks = [];
            this.priorityTasks = [];
            this.taskKeys.clear();
            this.priorityTaskKeys.clear();
            this.drawnPixels.clear();
            this.botSentColors.clear();
            this.verifyMap.clear();
            this.attackTimestamps = [];
            this._balanceCounter = 0;
            this._consecutiveDefenses = 0;
            this._lastDrawPatrolAt = 0;
            this.inFlightMap.clear();
            this.recentSentRing = [];
            this.head = 0;
            this.tokens = this.maxBurstTokens;
            this._drawFinishWaitUntil = 0;
            this._multiPassCount = 0;
        },

        finishDrawingAndHandoverToMxo(isAttackTask = false) {
            if (!state.bot.running || this.head < this.tasks.length) return false;

            if (state.prayt && state.prayt.liveClockActive) {
                this.tasks = [];
                this.head = 0;
                this.taskKeys.clear();
                this.running = false;
                setStatus(`⏰ SAAT: ${state.prayt.clockLastTimeStr || 'ON'}`);
                updateBotUI(true);
                return true;
            }

            // 🛑 ÇİZİM VE ÇİZİM-KORUMA MODUNU ANINDA DURDUR!
            const finishedTaskType = state.bot.currentTaskType;
            this.tasks = [];
            this.head = 0;
            this.taskKeys.clear();
            this._consecutiveDefenses = 0;
            this._balanceCounter = 0;
            this._drawFinishWaitUntil = 0;
            this._multiPassCount = 0;
            state.bot.running = false;
            state.bot.currentTaskType = null;

            // Saldırı görevi bittiyse koruma açmadan dur
            if (isAttackTask) {
                this.running = false;
                setStatus(`🎯 SALDIRI BİTTİ (${state.bot.placed} px)`);
                updateBotUI(true);
                if (typeof praytShowToast === 'function') {
                    praytShowToast('🎯 Saldırı başarıyla tamamlandı!', 'neon');
                }
                return true;
            }

            // Metin çizimi bittiyse metin alanını koruma haritasına kaydet
            if (finishedTaskType === 'text' && state.bot.textTargetPixels && state.bot.textTargetPixels.length > 0) {
                let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
                for (const p of state.bot.textTargetPixels) {
                    if (p.x < minX) minX = p.x;
                    if (p.x > maxX) maxX = p.x;
                    if (p.y < minY) minY = p.y;
                    if (p.y > maxY) maxY = p.y;
                }
                state.savedArea = { x1: minX, y1: minY, x2: maxX, y2: maxY };
                const tw = maxX - minX + 1;
                const th = maxY - minY + 1;
                const tData = new Int16Array(tw * th);
                tData.fill(-1);
                for (const p of state.bot.textTargetPixels) {
                    const tx = p.x - minX;
                    const ty = p.y - minY;
                    tData[ty * tw + tx] = p.colorId;
                }
                state.prayt.areaTargetMap = {
                    x1: minX, y1: minY, x2: maxX, y2: maxY,
                    w: tw, h: th, data: tData, type: 'text'
                };
            }

            // ⚡ KORUMAYI %100 MXO / İMKÂNSIZ SAVUNMAYA DEVRET!
            if (state.savedArea || (state.prayt && state.prayt.protectImage && state.prayt.protectOrigin)) {
                this.markAreaAsFullyDrawn();
                state.bot.protect = true;
                state.bot.humanProtect = false;
                if (state.prayt) {
                    state.prayt.noProtectWhileDrawing = false;
                    state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect = false;
                    if (state.prayt.protectImage && state.prayt.protectOrigin) {
                        state.prayt.realtimeProtect = true;
                    }
                }
                if (typeof praytHumanDuo !== 'undefined' && praytHumanDuo.active) {
                    praytHumanDuo.active = false;
                    if (typeof praytHumanDuo.syncButtons === 'function') praytHumanDuo.syncButtons();
                }
                if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') {
                    praytGodDefense.active = true;
                    state.prayt.godDefense = true;
                    praytGodDefense.syncButtons();
                    praytGodDefense.updateUI();
                }
                if (typeof _globalSyncAreaProtectUI === 'function') _globalSyncAreaProtectUI();
                if (typeof syncImageProtectToggles === 'function') syncImageProtectToggles();
                updatePraytProtectInfo();
                updateBotUI(true);

                setStatus('⚡ ÇİZİM BİTTİ → MXO 0MS SAVUNMA DEVREDE');
                if (typeof praytShowToast === 'function') {
                    praytShowToast('⚡ Çizim Bitti! Çizim-Koruma durdu, koruma %100 MXO 0ms Savunmaya devredildi!', 'neon');
                }
                this.running = true;
                return false;
            }

            this.running = false;
            setStatus('BOT DONE');
            updateBotUI(true);
            return true;
        },

        tick() {
            this.timer = null;
            this._inTick = true;
            try {
                const isNuke = (state.bot.mode === 'nuke' || (state.prayt && state.prayt.drawStrategy === 'nuke'));
                const isRadiusAttack = (state.bot.currentTaskType === 'radius_attack' || state.bot.mode === 'radius_attack' || (state.prayt && state.prayt.drawStrategy === 'radius_attack'));
                const isAttackTask = isNuke || isRadiusAttack;
                const now = performance.now();

                // 🛡️ DOUBLE-TAP DOĞRULAMA KALKANI (Yalnızca çizim bittikten sonra aktif olur ki çizim esnasında kendi piksellerimizi tekrar tekrar sorgulamasın!)
                if (!this.isDrawing() && this.verifyMap.size > 0) {
                    for (const [vKey, v] of this.verifyMap.entries()) {
                        if (now >= v.checkAt) {
                            this.verifyMap.delete(vKey);
                            if (!this.priorityTaskKeys.has(vKey) && !isPixelAlreadyColor(v.x, v.y, v.colorId)) {
                                this.inFlightMap.delete(vKey);
                                this.priorityTaskKeys.add(vKey);
                                this.taskKeys.add(vKey);
                                this.priorityTasks.unshift([v.x, v.y, v.colorId, vKey, { forcePlace: true, isPriorityDefense: true }]);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Zaten boyanmış (veya savunma tarafından onarılmış) çizim piksellerini anında atla ki Çizim-Koruma takılı kalmasın!
                while (this.head < this.tasks.length) {
                    const [tx, ty, tCid, tKey, tMeta] = this.tasks[this.head];
                    const isClock = !!(tMeta && (tMeta.isClockDraw || tMeta.isClockBg || tMeta.isClockErase));
                    const isSlowHuman = !!(tMeta && tMeta.isHumanDefense);
                    if (!isNuke && !isClock && !isSlowHuman && isPixelAlreadyColor(tx, ty, tCid)) {
                        const atm = state.prayt && state.prayt.areaTargetMap;
                        let isFinalColor = true;
                        if (atm && atm.data && tx >= atm.x1 && tx <= atm.x2 && ty >= atm.y1 && ty <= atm.y2) {
                            const expId = atm.data[(ty - atm.y1) * atm.w + (tx - atm.x1)];
                            if (expId >= 0 && expId !== tCid) isFinalColor = false;
                        }
                        if (isFinalColor) {
                            this.drawnPixels.set(tKey, tCid);
                        }
                        this.taskKeys.delete(tKey);
                        state.bot.skipped++;
                        this.head++;
                    } else {
                        break;
                    }
                }

                // Çizim kuyruğu bittiği milisaniyede (saldırı altında olsak bile!) Çizim-Korumayı durdurup %100 MXO'ya devret!
                if (state.bot.running && this.head >= this.tasks.length) {
                    if (this.finishDrawingAndHandoverToMxo(isAttackTask)) {
                        return;
                    }
                }

                const isGodActive = !!(typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined' && praytGodDefense.active);
                const hasPrio = (this.priorityTasks && this.priorityTasks.length > 0);
                const hasTasks = (this.tasks && (this.tasks.length - this.head) > 0);
                const isProtectActive = state.bot.protect || state.bot.humanProtect || state.prayt.realtimeProtect || state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect || isGodActive;

                // Motor yalnızca hiçbir görev ve koruma modu aktif değilse uyur
                if (!this.running && !state.bot.running && !isProtectActive && !hasPrio && !hasTasks && this.verifyMap.size === 0 && !(state.prayt && state.prayt.liveClockActive)) {
                    this.running = false;
                    return;
                }

                // Sıradaki görevi kontrol et ve değişkenleri en başta başlat (TDZ hatasını %100 önler)
                const nextTask = (this.priorityTasks.length > 0) ? this.priorityTasks[0] : (this.head < this.tasks.length ? this.tasks[this.head] : null);
                const nextMeta = (nextTask && nextTask[4]) ? nextTask[4] : null;

                let placedInBurst = 0;
                let lastMeta = nextMeta;
                let didSwitchColor = false;

                // Token yenileme
                if (!this.lastTokenRefill) this.lastTokenRefill = now;
                this.lastTokenRefill = now;

                const isRaid = !!(state.prayt && state.prayt.raidModeActive);

                // ⚡ Kesintisiz Akıcı Hız + MXO Savunma Senkronizasyonu
                const userSpeed = Math.max(5, state.bot.speed || 5);
                const recentHits = this.getRecentAttackCount(now);
                const attackPressure = Math.max(recentHits, this.priorityTasks.length);
                const recentSent1s = _wsPixelTimestamps.length;

                let targetSpeed = Math.max(16, userSpeed);
                if (this.priorityTasks.length > 0 || isRaid) {
                    targetSpeed = (recentSent1s < 56) ? 1 : 4;
                }

                if (now < this.rateLimitBackoffUntil) {
                    this.timer = praytFastTimer.setTimeout(() => this.tick(), Math.ceil(this.rateLimitBackoffUntil - now) + 10);
                    return;
                }

                // ⚡ ULTRA-FAST MXO BURST: Acil savunma piksellerinde aynı milisaniyede 20 pakete kadar tek seferde boşalt!
                const burstLimit = (this.priorityTasks.length > 0 && recentSent1s < 58)
                    ? Math.min(20, this.priorityTasks.length, Math.max(1, 59 - recentSent1s))
                    : 1;

                if (this.inFlightMap.size > 0) {
                    for (const [k, v] of this.inFlightMap.entries()) {
                        if (now - v.ts > 1600) this.inFlightMap.delete(k);
                    }
                }

                let pickedMode = 'draw';

                while (placedInBurst < burstLimit) {
                    // Çizim kuyruğunun başındaki zaten boyalı pikselleri temizle
                    while (this.head < this.tasks.length) {
                        const [tx, ty, tCid, tKey, tMeta] = this.tasks[this.head];
                        const isClock = !!(tMeta && (tMeta.isClockDraw || tMeta.isClockBg || tMeta.isClockErase));
                        const isSlowHuman = !!(tMeta && tMeta.isHumanDefense);
                        if (!isNuke && !isClock && !isSlowHuman && isPixelAlreadyColor(tx, ty, tCid)) {
                            const atm = state.prayt && state.prayt.areaTargetMap;
                            let isFinalColor = true;
                            if (atm && atm.data && tx >= atm.x1 && tx <= atm.x2 && ty >= atm.y1 && ty <= atm.y2) {
                                const expId = atm.data[(ty - atm.y1) * atm.w + (tx - atm.x1)];
                                if (expId >= 0 && expId !== tCid) isFinalColor = false;
                            }
                            if (isFinalColor) {
                                this.drawnPixels.set(tKey, tCid);
                            }
                            this.taskKeys.delete(tKey);
                            state.bot.skipped++;
                            this.head++;
                        } else {
                            break;
                        }
                    }

                    let task = null;
                    const hasPrioNow = this.priorityTasks.length > 0;
                    const hasDrawNow = this.head < this.tasks.length;

                    if (hasPrioNow && hasDrawNow) {
                        this._consecutiveDefenses = (this._consecutiveDefenses || 0) + 1;
                        const sneakDraw = (this.priorityTasks.length <= 2 && this._consecutiveDefenses >= 14 && (this._consecutiveDefenses % 14 === 0)) ||
                            (this._consecutiveDefenses >= 28 && (this._consecutiveDefenses % 28 === 0));
                        if (!sneakDraw) {
                            task = this.priorityTasks.shift();
                            if (task) this.priorityTaskKeys.delete(task[3]);
                            pickedMode = (attackPressure > 6) ? 'defense_heavy' : 'defense_balanced';
                        } else {
                            task = this.tasks[this.head++];
                            if (this.head > 1500) {
                                this.tasks = this.tasks.slice(this.head);
                                this.head = 0;
                            }
                            pickedMode = 'defense_light';
                        }
                    } else if (hasPrioNow) {
                        this._consecutiveDefenses = (this._consecutiveDefenses || 0) + 1;
                        task = this.priorityTasks.shift();
                        if (task) this.priorityTaskKeys.delete(task[3]);
                        pickedMode = 'defense_only';
                    } else if (hasDrawNow) {
                        this._consecutiveDefenses = 0;
                        task = this.tasks[this.head++];
                        if (this.head > 1500) {
                            this.tasks = this.tasks.slice(this.head);
                            this.head = 0;
                        }
                        pickedMode = 'draw';
                    }

                    if (!task) {
                        if (state.bot.running && this.head >= this.tasks.length) {
                            if (this.finishDrawingAndHandoverToMxo(isAttackTask)) {
                                return;
                            }
                        }
                        if (placedInBurst === 0 && this.priorityTasks.length === 0 && this.head >= this.tasks.length) {
                            const nowGod = !!(typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined' && praytGodDefense.active);
                            if (state.bot.protect || state.bot.humanProtect || state.prayt.realtimeProtect || state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect || nowGod) {
                                const isHumanPatrol = (state.bot.humanProtect || state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect || (typeof praytHumanDuo !== 'undefined' && praytHumanDuo.active)) && !state.bot.protect && !nowGod;
                                const minQuiet = isHumanPatrol ? 550 : 50;
                                if (!this._quietMs || this._quietMs < minQuiet) this._quietMs = minQuiet;
                                const pDelay = isHumanPatrol ? Math.min(3000, this._quietMs) : (this.verifyMap.size > 0 ? 45 : Math.min(110, this._quietMs));
                                this.timer = praytFastTimer.setTimeout(() => {
                                    const stillProtecting = state.bot.protect || state.bot.humanProtect || state.prayt.realtimeProtect || state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect || (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined' && praytGodDefense.active);
                                    if (!stillProtecting) {
                                        this.timer = null;
                                        this._quietMs = 50;
                                        return;
                                    }
                                    const before = (praytMxoEngine.priorityTasks.length + (praytMxoEngine.tasks.length - praytMxoEngine.head));
                                    this.patrol();
                                    const after = (praytMxoEngine.priorityTasks.length + (praytMxoEngine.tasks.length - praytMxoEngine.head));
                                    if (after > before) {
                                        this._quietMs = isHumanPatrol ? 550 : 50;
                                    } else {
                                        this._quietMs = isHumanPatrol ? Math.min(3000, (this._quietMs || minQuiet) + 120) : Math.min(110, (this._quietMs || minQuiet) + 15);
                                    }
                                    this.tick();
                                }, pDelay);
                                return;
                            }

                            this.running = false;
                            state.bot.running = false;
                            setStatus('BOT DONE');
                            updateBotUI(true);
                            return;
                        }
                        break;
                    }

                    const [x, y, colorId, key, meta] = task;
                    this.taskKeys.delete(key);
                    lastMeta = meta;

                    const forcePlace = !!(meta && meta.forcePlace);
                    const isPrioTask = !!(meta && meta.isPriorityDefense);

                    if (!forcePlace && !isPrioTask) {
                        const inflight = this.inFlightMap.get(key);
                        if (inflight && inflight.colorId === colorId && (now - inflight.ts) < 1500) {
                            continue;
                        }
                    }

                    if (!isPrioTask && !isNuke && !(meta && (meta.isClockDraw || meta.isClockBg || meta.isClockErase || meta.isHumanDefense)) && isPixelAlreadyColor(x, y, colorId)) {
                        const atm = state.prayt && state.prayt.areaTargetMap;
                        let isFinalColor = true;
                        if (atm && atm.data && x >= atm.x1 && x <= atm.x2 && y >= atm.y1 && y <= atm.y2) {
                            const expId = atm.data[(y - atm.y1) * atm.w + (x - atm.x1)];
                            if (expId >= 0 && expId !== colorId) isFinalColor = false;
                        }
                        if (isFinalColor) {
                            this.drawnPixels.set(key, colorId);
                        }
                        state.bot.skipped++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (this.lastPlacedColorId !== null && this.lastPlacedColorId !== colorId && !isNuke) {
                        didSwitchColor = true;
                    }
                    this.lastPlacedColorId = colorId;
                    state.prayt.currentHandColor = colorId;

                    if (state.ws && state.wsReady && state.ws.readyState === 1) {
                        const sentOk = wsSend('p', `[${x},${y},${colorId},1]`, isPrioTask);
                        if (sentOk) {
                            markPlaced(x, y, colorId);
                            this.inFlightMap.set(key, { colorId, ts: now });
                            this.botSentColors.set(key, colorId);
                            if (isPrioTask && !this.isDrawing()) {
                                this.verifyMap.set(key, { x, y, colorId, checkAt: now + 150 });
                            }
                            this.recentSentRing.push({ x, y, colorId, ts: now });
                            if (this.recentSentRing.length > 16) this.recentSentRing.shift();
                            state.bot.placed++;
                            placedInBurst++;
                            state.prayt.totalPlacedSession = (state.prayt.totalPlacedSession || 0) + 1;
                            if (!state.prayt.placedTimestamps) state.prayt.placedTimestamps = [];
                            state.prayt.placedTimestamps.push(now);
                        } else {
                            if (isPrioTask) {
                                this.priorityTaskKeys.add(key);
                                this.priorityTasks.unshift(task);
                            } else if (this.head > 0) {
                                this.head--;
                                this.taskKeys.add(key);
                            } else {
                                this.priorityTasks.unshift(task);
                            }
                            if (placedInBurst > 0) {
                                break;
                            }
                            const reqGap = isPrioTask ? 1.0 : 17.5;
                            const gapWait = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(reqGap - (now - _lastWsPixelTime)));
                            const winWait = (_wsPixelTimestamps.length >= (isPrioTask ? 59 : 54) && _wsPixelTimestamps.length > 0) ? Math.ceil(1001 - (now - _wsPixelTimestamps[0])) : 0;
                            const waitRem = Math.max(1, gapWait, winWait);
                            this.timer = praytFastTimer.setTimeout(() => this.tick(), waitRem);
                            return;
                        }
                    } else {
                        if (isPrioTask) {
                            this.priorityTaskKeys.add(key);
                            this.priorityTasks.unshift(task);
                        } else if (this.head > 0) {
                            this.head--;
                            this.taskKeys.add(key);
                        }
                        this.timer = praytFastTimer.setTimeout(() => this.tick(), 50);
                        return;
                    }
                }

                // Bu tick içinde son çizim pikseli koyulduysa bile beklemeden ANINDA Çizim-Korumayı kapatıp MXO'ya devret!
                while (this.head < this.tasks.length) {
                    const [tx, ty, tCid, tKey, tMeta] = this.tasks[this.head];
                    const isClock = !!(tMeta && (tMeta.isClockDraw || tMeta.isClockBg || tMeta.isClockErase));
                    const isSlowHuman = !!(tMeta && tMeta.isHumanDefense);
                    if (!isNuke && !isClock && !isSlowHuman && isPixelAlreadyColor(tx, ty, tCid)) {
                        const atm = state.prayt && state.prayt.areaTargetMap;
                        let isFinalColor = true;
                        if (atm && atm.data && tx >= atm.x1 && tx <= atm.x2 && ty >= atm.y1 && ty <= atm.y2) {
                            const expId = atm.data[(ty - atm.y1) * atm.w + (tx - atm.x1)];
                            if (expId >= 0 && expId !== tCid) isFinalColor = false;
                        }
                        if (isFinalColor) {
                            this.drawnPixels.set(tKey, tCid);
                        }
                        this.taskKeys.delete(tKey);
                        state.bot.skipped++;
                        this.head++;
                    } else {
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (state.bot.running && this.head >= this.tasks.length) {
                    if (this.finishDrawingAndHandoverToMxo(isAttackTask)) {
                        return;
                    }
                    if (pickedMode === 'defense_balanced' || pickedMode === 'defense_heavy' || pickedMode === 'defense_light') {
                        pickedMode = 'defense_only';
                    }
                }

                if (placedInBurst > 0 || state.bot.skipped > 0) {
                    updateBotUI();
                }

                const isPriorityNow = !!(lastMeta && lastMeta.isPriorityDefense) || pickedMode !== 'draw' || this.priorityTasks.length > 0;
                const isHuman = !isPriorityNow && (
                    state.bot.mode === 'human' ||
                    (state.prayt && state.prayt.drawStrategy === 'human') ||
                    state.bot.currentTaskType === 'void_mode' ||
                    (!!state.bot.humanProtect && !state.bot.protect) ||
                    (typeof praytHumanDuo !== 'undefined' && praytHumanDuo.active && !state.bot.protect) ||
                    !!(lastMeta && (lastMeta.isHumanDefense || lastMeta.isSlowCleanup || lastMeta.isVoidStroke))
                );

                // ⚡ Gecikme hesabı (1ms/4ms MXO Reflex & Kesintisiz Akıcı Çizim Hızı)
                let delay;
                if (isPriorityNow) {
                    if (_wsPixelTimestamps.length < 56) {
                        delay = 1;
                    } else {
                        delay = 4;
                    }
                    if (pickedMode === 'defense_light') {
                        setStatus(`⚖️ KALE KORUMA+ÇİZİM: ${this.priorityTasks.length} onarım`);
                    } else if (pickedMode === 'defense_balanced' || pickedMode === 'defense_heavy') {
                        setStatus(`🛡️ KALE ÇİZİM-KORUMA (${this.priorityTasks.length} onarım)`);
                    } else {
                        setStatus(`⚡ MXO KALE SAVUNMA (${this.priorityTasks.length} onarım)`);
                    }
                } else if (isHuman) {
                    const isVoid = !!(lastMeta && lastMeta.isVoidStroke) || state.bot.currentTaskType === 'void_mode';
                    const stepNum = (lastMeta && typeof lastMeta.strokeStep === 'number') ? lastMeta.strokeStep : state.bot.placed;
                    const wristWave = Math.sin(stepNum * 0.42) * 2.2 + (Math.random() - 0.45) * 2.4;
                    const baseHumanDrag = isVoid ? 17 : Math.max(17, userSpeed);
                    delay = Math.max(16, Math.round(baseHumanDrag + wristWave));

                    if (lastMeta && lastMeta.turnPause) {
                        delay += Math.floor(Math.random() * 6 + 4);
                    }
                    if (lastMeta && lastMeta.strokePause) {
                        delay += isVoid ? Math.floor(Math.random() * 10 + 8) : Math.floor(Math.random() * 34 + 28);
                    }
                    if (didSwitchColor || (lastMeta && lastMeta.colorSwitchPause)) {
                        if (this.lastPlacedColorId !== null) {
                            selectPaletteColorInDOM(this.lastPlacedColorId);
                        }
                        delay += isVoid ? Math.floor(Math.random() * 75 + 95) : Math.floor(Math.random() * 85 + 135);
                    }
                    const fatigueInterval = isVoid ? 175 : 145;
                    if (state.bot.placed > 0 && state.bot.placed % (Math.floor(Math.random() * 35) + fatigueInterval) === 0) {
                        delay += Math.floor(Math.random() * 30 + 35);
                    }
                } else if (isRadiusAttack || (lastMeta && lastMeta.isRadiusAttack)) {
                    delay = Math.max(16, Math.round(targetSpeed));
                    setStatus(`🎯 SALDIRI: ${state.bot.placed}/${state.bot.total || '?'} px`);
                } else {
                    const jitter = userSpeed <= 18 ? 0 : Math.floor((Math.random() - 0.5) * 2);
                    delay = Math.max(16, Math.round(targetSpeed + jitter));

                    if (isNuke) {
                        setStatus(`💥 MAHVET: ${state.bot.placed} px`);
                    }
                }

                this.nextAllowedAt = now + delay;
                this.timer = praytFastTimer.setTimeout(() => this.tick(), delay);
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('[PraytClient] Engine tick error:', err);
                if (this.running || (state.bot && state.bot.running)) {
                    this.timer = setTimeout(() => this.tick(), 60);
                }
            } finally {
                this._inTick = false;
            }
        },

        patrol() {
            const isGodActive = !!(typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined' && praytGodDefense.active);
            if ((state.bot.protect || state.bot.humanProtect || isGodActive) && state.savedArea) {
                scanAndQueueDamagedColorPixels();
            }
            if ((state.prayt.realtimeProtect || state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect) && state.prayt.protectImage && state.prayt.protectOrigin) {
                scanAndQueueDamagedImagePixels();
            }
        }
    };

    // =========================================================
    // PER-PIXEL AREA TARGET MAP (TÜRK BAYRAĞI, LGBT & ÇOK RENKLİ ALAN KORUMA)
    // =========================================================

    function setSolidAreaTargetMap(area, colorId) {
        if (!area || colorId === null || colorId === undefined) return;
        const w = area.x2 - area.x1 + 1;
        const h = area.y2 - area.y1 + 1;
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
        const data = new Int16Array(w * h);
        for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
            for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
                const ax = area.x1 + dx;
                const ay = area.y1 + dy;
                if (state.prayt.seaSkip && isCanvasSeaPixel(ax, ay)) {
                    data[dy * w + dx] = -1;
                } else {
                    data[dy * w + dx] = colorId;
                }
            }
        }
        state.prayt.areaTargetMap = {
            x1: area.x1,
            y1: area.y1,
            x2: area.x2,
            y2: area.y2,
            w,
            h,
            data,
            type: 'solid'
        };
    }

    function captureCanvasAreaToTargetMap(area) {
        if (!area) return null;
        const w = area.x2 - area.x1 + 1;
        const h = area.y2 - area.y1 + 1;
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return null;

        takeSnapshot(area);
        const snap = state.bot.snapshot;
        const palette = getPaletteColors();
        const fallbackColor = state.bot.colorId !== null ? state.bot.colorId : getSelectedColorId();

        if (snap && palette && palette.length > 0 && snap.x1 === area.x1 && snap.y1 === area.y1) {
            const data = new Int16Array(w * h);
            for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
                for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
                    const ax = area.x1 + dx;
                    const ay = area.y1 + dy;
                    if (state.prayt.seaSkip && isCanvasSeaPixel(ax, ay)) {
                        data[dy * w + dx] = -1;
                        continue;
                    }
                    const col = snap.colors[dy * snap.w + dx];
                    const r = (col >> 16) & 255;
                    const g = (col >> 8) & 255;
                    const b = col & 255;
                    const cid = nearestPaletteId(r, g, b, palette);
                    data[dy * w + dx] = (cid !== null && cid !== undefined) ? cid : (fallbackColor ?? -1);
                }
            }
            state.prayt.areaTargetMap = {
                x1: area.x1, y1: area.y1, x2: area.x2, y2: area.y2,
                w, h, data, type: 'canvas_capture'
            };
            if (typeof praytMxoEngine !== 'undefined' && praytMxoEngine.drawnPixels) {
                for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
                    for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
                        const cid = data[dy * w + dx];
                        if (cid >= 0) {
                            praytMxoEngine.drawnPixels.set(`${area.x1 + dx},${area.y1 + dy}`, cid);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            return state.prayt.areaTargetMap;
        }
        return null;
    }

    function praytStartSelectAndHumanProtect() {
        state.prayt.pendingHumanProtect = true;
        state.prayt.pendingMxoProtect = false;
        state.prayt.pendingFlag = null;
        setStatus('🛡️ KORUNACAK ALANI SEÇİN...');
        beginAreaSelection();
        const help = $('#w3x-select-help');
        if (help) {
            help.textContent = '🛡️ İnsansı korunacak çizimi/bölgeyi haritada sürükleyerek seçin — otomatik hafızaya alınıp korunacak!';
            help.classList.add('active');
        }
    }

    function praytCaptureAndHumanProtect() {
        if (!state.savedArea) {
            praytStartSelectAndHumanProtect();
            return;
        }
        captureCanvasAreaToTargetMap(state.savedArea);
        state.bot.humanProtect = true;
        state.bot.protect = false;
        if (_globalSyncAreaProtectUI) _globalSyncAreaProtectUI();
        updatePraytProtectInfo();
        praytMxoEngine.start();
        setStatus('🛡️ ÇİZİM HAFIZADA (İNSAN KORUMA)');
    }

    function praytCaptureAndMxoProtect() {
        if (!state.savedArea) return;
        captureCanvasAreaToTargetMap(state.savedArea);
        state.bot.protect = true;
        state.bot.humanProtect = false;
        if (_globalSyncAreaProtectUI) _globalSyncAreaProtectUI();
        updatePraytProtectInfo();
        praytMxoEngine.start();
        setStatus('⚡ ÇİZİM HAFIZADA (MXO 0MS)');
    }

    // =========================================================
    // CANLI SALDIRI RADARI & SESLİ/GÖRSEL UYARI SİSTEMİ
    // =========================================================

    let _radarAudioCtx = null;
    function playRadarAlertSound(forceTest = false) {
        if (!forceTest && !state.prayt.radarSound) return;
        const now = Date.now();
        if (!forceTest && now - (state.prayt.lastBeepAt || 0) < 1100) return;
        state.prayt.lastBeepAt = now;
        try {
            const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
            if (!AudioCtx) return;
            if (!_radarAudioCtx) _radarAudioCtx = new AudioCtx();
            if (_radarAudioCtx.state === 'suspended') _radarAudioCtx.resume();

            const osc = _radarAudioCtx.createOscillator();
            const gain = _radarAudioCtx.createGain();
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, _radarAudioCtx.currentTime);
            osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1320, _radarAudioCtx.currentTime + 0.085);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.08, _radarAudioCtx.currentTime);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, _radarAudioCtx.currentTime + 0.095);
            osc.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(_radarAudioCtx.destination);
            osc.start();
            osc.stop(_radarAudioCtx.currentTime + 0.1);
        } catch (e) {}
    }

    let _radarFlashTimer = null;
    let _lastGriefDomUpdate = 0;
    function praytRecordAttack(x, y, attackerColorId, expectedColorId) {
        const nowPerf = performance.now();
        state.prayt.attackCount = (state.prayt.attackCount || 0) + 1;
        state.prayt.lastAttack = {
            x,
            y,
            attackerColorId,
            expectedColorId,
            time: Date.now()
        };

        if (!state.prayt.griefLog) state.prayt.griefLog = [];
        const d = new Date();
        const pad2 = n => (n < 10 ? '0' + n : '' + n);
        state.prayt.griefLog.unshift({
            x,
            y,
            attackerColorId,
            expectedColorId,
            time: `${pad2(d.getHours())}:${pad2(d.getMinutes())}:${pad2(d.getSeconds())}`
        });
        if (state.prayt.griefLog.length > 8) state.prayt.griefLog.pop();

        // ⚡ SIFIR GECİKMELİ DOM THROTTLING: Ağır saldırı altında DOM tablo yenilemesini 120ms'de bire sınırla ki WS refleksi yavaşlamasın!
        if (nowPerf - _lastGriefDomUpdate < 120) return;
        _lastGriefDomUpdate = nowPerf;

        const griefTbody = document.getElementById('prayt-grief-tbody');
        if (griefTbody) {
            griefTbody.innerHTML = state.prayt.griefLog.map(g => `
                <tr>
                    <td style="color:#00e5ff;font-weight:bold;">[${g.x}, ${g.y}]</td>
                    <td style="color:#ff5252;font-weight:bold;">#${g.attackerColorId}</td>
                    <td style="color:#69f0ae;font-weight:bold;">#${g.expectedColorId}</td>
                    <td style="color:#aaa;">${g.time}</td>
                </tr>
            `).join('');
        }

        playRadarAlertSound(false);

        const radarBar = $('#w3x-radar-bar');
        const radarStatus = $('#w3x-radar-status');
        if (radarStatus) {
            radarStatus.textContent = `🚨 SALDIRI: ${x},${y} (${state.prayt.attackCount} px)`;
            radarStatus.style.color = '#ff4d5a';
        }
        if (radarBar) {
            radarBar.style.borderColor = '#ff4d5a';
            radarBar.style.background = 'rgba(227,10,23,0.22)';
            if (_radarFlashTimer) clearTimeout(_radarFlashTimer);
            _radarFlashTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                if (radarBar) {
                    radarBar.style.borderColor = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)';
                    radarBar.style.background = 'rgba(20,24,33,0.9)';
                }
                if (radarStatus) {
                    radarStatus.style.color = '#ffa657';
                }
            }, 1800);
        }
    }

    function praytFocusAttack(x, y) {
        state.target = { x, y };
        const xInput = $('#w3x-pos-x');
        const yInput = $('#w3x-pos-y');
        if (xInput) xInput.value = x;
        if (yInput) yInput.value = y;
        renderTargetMarker();
        setStatus(`🎯 RADAR HEDEFİ: ${x}, ${y}`);
    }

    function ensureAreaTargetMap() {
        const area = state.savedArea;
        if (!area) return null;
        const cur = state.prayt.areaTargetMap;

        if (cur && cur.x1 === area.x1 && cur.y1 === area.y1 && cur.x2 === area.x2 && cur.y2 === area.y2 && cur.data) {
            return cur;
        }

        const captured = captureCanvasAreaToTargetMap(area);
        if (captured) return captured;

        const botColor = (state.bot.colorId !== null && state.bot.colorId !== undefined) ? state.bot.colorId : getSelectedColorId();
        if (botColor !== null && botColor !== undefined) {
            state.bot.colorId = botColor;
            setSolidAreaTargetMap(area, botColor);
            return state.prayt.areaTargetMap;
        }
        return null;
    }

    function getExpectedAreaColorId(x, y) {
        const area = state.savedArea;
        if (!area) return null;
        if (x < area.x1 || x > area.x2 || y < area.y1 || y > area.y2) return null;

        const map = ensureAreaTargetMap();
        if (map && map.data) {
            const dx = x - map.x1;
            const dy = y - map.y1;
            if (dx >= 0 && dx < map.w && dy >= 0 && dy < map.h) {
                const cid = map.data[dy * map.w + dx];
                return cid >= 0 ? cid : -1;
            }
        }

        if (state.prayt.seaSkip && isCanvasSeaPixel(x, y)) return -1;

        let colorId = state.bot.colorId;
        if (colorId === null) colorId = getSelectedColorId();
        return colorId;
    }

    function isPixelAlreadyColor(x, y, colorId) {
        if (colorId === null || colorId === undefined || colorId < 0) return false;

        // 1. ÖNCE Gerçek DOM <canvas>'tan alınan canlı Snapshot kontrolü (Int32Array - 0xRRGGBB)
        const snap = state.bot.snapshot;
        if (snap && snap.colors && x >= snap.x1 && y >= snap.y1 && x < snap.x1 + snap.w && y < snap.y1 + snap.h) {
            const px = x - snap.x1;
            const py = y - snap.y1;
            const col = snap.colors[py * snap.w + px];
            if (col >= 0) {
                const expRgb = getPaletteRgbById(colorId);
                if (expRgb !== null && expRgb !== undefined) {
                    if (col === expRgb) return true;
                    // Tarayıcı renk profili / gamma kaynaklı +-3 RGB sapmasını tolere et (Kendi koyduğu rengi farklı sanıp döngüye girmesini %100 önler!)
                    const r = (col >> 16) & 255, g = (col >> 8) & 255, b = col & 255;
                    const er = (expRgb >> 16) & 255, eg = (expRgb >> 8) & 255, eb = expRgb & 255;
                    return Math.abs(r - er) <= 3 && Math.abs(g - eg) <= 3 && Math.abs(b - eb) <= 3;
                }
            }
        }

        // 2. Harita önbelleği (MapCache) kontrolü
        if (state.prayt && state.prayt.mapCache && state.prayt.mapLoaded &&
            x >= 0 && x < state.prayt.mapWidth && y >= 0 && y < state.prayt.mapHeight) {
            const currentMapId = state.prayt.mapCache[y * state.prayt.mapWidth + x];
            if (currentMapId >= 0) {
                return currentMapId === colorId;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    // --- Color & Flag Bot: Mxo Realtime Priority Defense (0ms Instant Multi-Color & Flag Aware) ---
    function praytColorRealtimeProtect(pixels) {
        if (!state.bot.protect || !state.savedArea) return;
        const area = state.savedArea;
        const list = Array.isArray(pixels) && typeof pixels[0] === "number" ? [pixels] : pixels;

        let added = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
            const px = list[i];
            if (!Array.isArray(px)) continue;
            const [x, y, placedColor] = px;
            if (typeof x !== "number" || typeof y !== "number") continue;
            if (x < area.x1 || x > area.x2 || y < area.y1 || y > area.y2) continue;

            const expectedColorId = getExpectedAreaColorId(x, y);
            if (expectedColorId === null || expectedColorId === undefined || expectedColorId < 0) continue;
            if (placedColor === expectedColorId) continue;

            praytRecordAttack(x, y, placedColor, expectedColorId);
            praytMxoEngine.putPixelWithPriority(x, y, expectedColorId, { forcePlace: true, isPriorityDefense: true });
            added++;
        }

        if (added > 0) {
            setStatus(`⚡ MXO SAVUNMA (${praytMxoEngine.priorityTasks.length + (praytMxoEngine.tasks.length - praytMxoEngine.head)})`);
        }
    }

    // --- Color & Flag Bot: Human-like Defense (Organic Curved Wrist Repair + Multi-Color Flag Support) ---
    function praytColorHumanProtect(pixels) {
        if (!state.bot.humanProtect || !state.savedArea) return;
        const area = state.savedArea;
        const list = Array.isArray(pixels) && typeof pixels[0] === "number" ? [pixels] : pixels;

        for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
            const px = list[i];
            if (!Array.isArray(px)) continue;
            const [x, y, placedColor] = px;
            if (typeof x !== "number" || typeof y !== "number") continue;
            if (x < area.x1 || x > area.x2 || y < area.y1 || y > area.y2) continue;

            const expectedColorId = getExpectedAreaColorId(x, y);
            if (expectedColorId === null || expectedColorId === undefined || expectedColorId < 0) continue;
            if (placedColor === expectedColorId) continue;

            praytRecordAttack(x, y, placedColor, expectedColorId);
            state.bot.humanProtectBuffer.push({ x, y, colorId: expectedColorId });
        }

        // Hızlı ve %100 İnsansı Görsel Tepki Penceresi:
        // Eli zaten tuvaldeyse 45-80ms, ilk saldırıysa 145-225ms içinde gelen pikselleri toplar
        // ve tek tek tıklamak yerine BİRBİRİNE BAĞLI AKICI FIRÇA SÜRÜKLEMESİYLE süpürür!
        if (!state.bot.humanProtectTimer && state.bot.humanProtectBuffer.length > 0) {
            const nowPerf = performance.now();
            const isWarm = state.bot._lastHumanRepairTs && (nowPerf - state.bot._lastHumanRepairTs) < 2500;
            const reactionDelay = isWarm
                ? Math.floor(Math.random() * 35 + 45)
                : Math.floor(Math.random() * 80 + 145);
            state.bot.humanProtectTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                state.bot.humanProtectTimer = null;
                state.bot._lastHumanRepairTs = performance.now();
                const rawBuffer = state.bot.humanProtectBuffer.splice(0);
                if (rawBuffer.length === 0) return;
                // Deduplicate by coordinate keeping latest target color
                const dedupMap = new Map();
                for (const item of rawBuffer) dedupMap.set(`${item.x},${item.y}`, item);
                const uniqueList = [...dedupMap.values()];
                // Sort via Color-First + 8-Connected Anti-Orphan Human Brush Path!
                const organicQueue = buildOrganicHumanPath(uniqueList, true);
                for (let i = 0; i < organicQueue.length; i++) {
                    const p = organicQueue[i];
                    praytMxoEngine.putPixel(p.x, p.y, p.colorId, {
                        forcePlace: true,
                        isSlowCleanup: true,
                        isHumanDefense: true,
                        turnPause: !!p.turnPause,
                        strokePause: !!p.strokePause,
                        colorSwitchPause: !!p.colorSwitchPause,
                        strokeStep: p.strokeStep || i
                    });
                }
                praytMxoEngine.start();
                setStatus(`🛡️ İNSANSI FIRÇA ONARIM (${organicQueue.length} px)`);
            }, reactionDelay);
        }
    }

    function scanAndQueueDamagedColorPixels() {
        if (!state.savedArea) return;
