        const isDrawingNow = !!(typeof praytMxoEngine !== 'undefined' && praytMxoEngine.isDrawing());
        // Çizim devam ederken <canvas> okuması yapma (WebSocket 0ms'de gerçek saldırıyı zaten yakalar!)
        if (isDrawingNow) return;
        const area = state.savedArea;

        // Her devriye turunda gerçek DOM <canvas>'tan taze görüntü al!
        takeSnapshot();
        const hasMapCache = !!(state.prayt && state.prayt.mapCache && state.prayt.mapLoaded);
        if (!state.bot.snapshot && !hasMapCache) return;

        const w = area.x2 - area.x1 + 1;
        const h = area.y2 - area.y1 + 1;
        const total = w * h;

        const isUnderAttack = !!(state.prayt && state.prayt.raidModeActive) ||
            (praytMxoEngine.priorityTasks && praytMxoEngine.priorityTasks.length > 0);
        let startIdx = 0, endIdx = total;
        if (!isUnderAttack && total > 15000) {
            const MAX_SCAN = 15000;
            if (!state.prayt._scanOffset) state.prayt._scanOffset = 0;
            startIdx = state.prayt._scanOffset;
            endIdx = Math.min(total, startIdx + MAX_SCAN);
            state.prayt._scanOffset = (endIdx >= total) ? 0 : endIdx;
        } else {
            if (state.prayt) state.prayt._scanOffset = 0;
        }

        const nowPerf = performance.now();
        const damaged = [];
        for (let i = startIdx; i < endIdx; i++) {
            const dx = i % w;
            const dy = Math.floor(i / w);
            const x = area.x1 + dx;
            const y = area.y1 + dy;
            const key = `${x},${y}`;
            if (praytMxoEngine.priorityTaskKeys && praytMxoEngine.priorityTaskKeys.has(key)) continue;
            if (praytMxoEngine.inFlightMap) {
                const inflight = praytMxoEngine.inFlightMap.get(key);
                if (inflight && (nowPerf - inflight.ts) < 1500) continue;
            }

            const expectedColorId = getExpectedAreaColorId(x, y);
            if (expectedColorId === null || expectedColorId === undefined || expectedColorId < 0) continue;
            if (state.prayt && state.prayt.seaSkip && isCanvasSeaPixel(x, y)) continue;
            if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(x, y, expectedColorId)) {
                damaged.push({ x, y, colorId: expectedColorId, forcePlace: true });
            }
        }
        if (damaged.length === 0) return;

        if ((state.bot.humanProtect || (typeof praytHumanDuo !== 'undefined' && praytHumanDuo.active)) && !state.bot.protect) {
            const organic = buildOrganicHumanPath(damaged, true);
            for (let i = 0; i < organic.length; i++) {
                const p = organic[i];
                praytMxoEngine.putPixel(p.x, p.y, p.colorId, {
                    forcePlace: true,
                    isSlowCleanup: true,
                    isHumanDefense: true,
                    turnPause: !!p.turnPause,
                    strokePause: !!p.strokePause,
                    colorSwitchPause: !!p.colorSwitchPause,
                    strokeStep: p.strokeStep || i
                });
            }
            praytMxoEngine.start();
            setStatus(`🛡️ İNSANSI DEVRİYE (${organic.length} px)`);
        } else {
            const sortedDamaged = groupByFirstHandColor(damaged);
            let directFired = 0;
            for (const p of sortedDamaged) {
                const key = `${p.x},${p.y}`;
                if (directFired < 14 && state.ws && state.wsReady && state.ws.readyState === 1 &&
                    nowPerf >= (praytMxoEngine.rateLimitBackoffUntil || 0) &&
                    _wsPixelTimestamps.length < 58) {
                    const sentNow = wsSend('p', `[${p.x},${p.y},${p.colorId},1]`, true);
                    if (sentNow) {
                        directFired++;
                        markPlaced(p.x, p.y, p.colorId);
                        praytMxoEngine.inFlightMap.set(key, { colorId: p.colorId, ts: nowPerf });
                        praytMxoEngine.drawnPixels.set(key, p.colorId);
                        praytMxoEngine.verifyMap.set(key, { x: p.x, y: p.y, colorId: p.colorId, checkAt: nowPerf + 150 });
                        praytMxoEngine.recentSentRing.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y, colorId: p.colorId, ts: nowPerf });
                        if (praytMxoEngine.recentSentRing.length > 16) praytMxoEngine.recentSentRing.shift();
                        state.bot.placed++;
                        state.prayt.totalPlacedSession = (state.prayt.totalPlacedSession || 0) + 1;
                        if (!state.prayt.placedTimestamps) state.prayt.placedTimestamps = [];
                        state.prayt.placedTimestamps.push(nowPerf);
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                praytMxoEngine.putPixelWithPriority(p.x, p.y, p.colorId, {
                    forcePlace: true,
                    isPriorityDefense: true,
                    colorSwitchPause: !!p.colorSwitchPause
                });
            }
            praytMxoEngine.start();
            setStatus(`⚡ MXO KALE DEVRİYE (${sortedDamaged.length} onarılıyor)`);
        }
    }


    // --- Image Bot: Mxo Realtime Priority Defense (0ms Instant) ---
    function praytRealtimeProtect(pixels) {
        const img = state.prayt.protectImage;
        const origin = state.prayt.protectOrigin;
        if (!img || !origin) return;
        if (!pixels || !Array.isArray(pixels)) {
            scanAndQueueDamagedImagePixels();
            return;
        }
        const imgW = img.w, imgH = img.h, imgData = img.data;
        const scale = state.image.scale || 1;
        const ox = origin.x, oy = origin.y;
        const totalW = imgW * scale, totalH = imgH * scale;
        const list = Array.isArray(pixels) && typeof pixels[0] === "number" ? [pixels] : pixels;

        let added = 0;
        const nowPerf = performance.now();
        for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
            const px = list[i];
            if (!Array.isArray(px)) continue;
            const [x, y, placedColor] = px;
            if (typeof x !== "number" || typeof y !== "number") continue;
            if (x < ox || x >= ox + totalW || y < oy || y >= oy + totalH) continue;
            const imgX = Math.floor((x - ox) / scale);
            const imgY = Math.floor((y - oy) / scale);
            if (imgX < 0 || imgX >= imgW || imgY < 0 || imgY >= imgH) continue;
            const idx = (imgY * imgW + imgX) * 4;
            const a = imgData[idx + 3];
            if (a < 128) continue;
            const palette = state.prayt.autoPalette || getPaletteColors();
            if (!state.prayt.autoPalette) state.prayt.autoPalette = palette;
            const expectedColorId = nearestPaletteId(imgData[idx], imgData[idx+1], imgData[idx+2], palette);
            if (expectedColorId === null || expectedColorId === placedColor) continue;

            const key = `${x},${y}`;
            if (praytMxoEngine.isDrawing() && praytMxoEngine.drawnPixels && !praytMxoEngine.drawnPixels.has(key)) continue;
            if (praytMxoEngine.inFlightMap) {
                praytMxoEngine.inFlightMap.delete(key);
            }
            praytMxoEngine.recordEnemyHit(nowPerf);
            if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') {
                praytGodDefense.intercept(x, y, placedColor, expectedColorId);
            } else {
                praytRecordAttack(x, y, placedColor, expectedColorId);
                praytMxoEngine.putPixelWithPriority(x, y, expectedColorId, { forcePlace: true, isPriorityDefense: true });
            }
            added++;
        }

        if (added > 0) {
            setStatus(`⚡ MXO KALE RESİM KORUMA (${praytMxoEngine.priorityTasks.length} onarım)`);
        }
    }

    // --- Image Bot: Human-like Defense (Organic Curved Wrist Repair - NEVER Straight Line) ---
    function praytRealtimeHumanProtect(pixels) {
        const img = state.prayt.protectImage;
        const origin = state.prayt.protectOrigin;
        if (!img || !origin || !state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect) return;
        const imgW = img.w, imgH = img.h, imgData = img.data;
        const scale = state.image.scale || 1;
        const ox = origin.x, oy = origin.y;
        const totalW = imgW * scale, totalH = imgH * scale;
        const list = Array.isArray(pixels) && typeof pixels[0] === "number" ? [pixels] : pixels;

        if (!state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtectBuffer) state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtectBuffer = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
            const px = list[i];
            if (!Array.isArray(px)) continue;
            const [x, y, placedColor] = px;
            if (typeof x !== "number" || typeof y !== "number") continue;
            if (x < ox || x >= ox + totalW || y < oy || y >= oy + totalH) continue;
            const imgX = Math.floor((x - ox) / scale);
            const imgY = Math.floor((y - oy) / scale);
            if (imgX < 0 || imgX >= imgW || imgY < 0 || imgY >= imgH) continue;
            const idx = (imgY * imgW + imgX) * 4;
            const a = imgData[idx + 3];
            if (a < 128) continue;
            const palette = state.prayt.autoPalette || getPaletteColors();
            if (!state.prayt.autoPalette) state.prayt.autoPalette = palette;
            const expectedColorId = nearestPaletteId(imgData[idx], imgData[idx+1], imgData[idx+2], palette);
            if (expectedColorId === null || expectedColorId === placedColor) continue;

            praytRecordAttack(x, y, placedColor, expectedColorId);
            state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtectBuffer.push({ x, y, colorId: expectedColorId });
        }

        if (!state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtectTimer && state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtectBuffer.length > 0) {
            const nowPerf = performance.now();
            const isWarm = state.prayt._lastHumanImgRepairTs && (nowPerf - state.prayt._lastHumanImgRepairTs) < 2500;
            const reactionDelay = isWarm
                ? Math.floor(Math.random() * 35 + 45)
                : Math.floor(Math.random() * 80 + 145);
            state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtectTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtectTimer = null;
                state.prayt._lastHumanImgRepairTs = performance.now();
                const rawBuffer = state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtectBuffer.splice(0);
                if (rawBuffer.length === 0) return;
                const dedupMap = new Map();
                for (const item of rawBuffer) dedupMap.set(`${item.x},${item.y}`, item);
                const uniqueList = [...dedupMap.values()];
                const organicQueue = buildOrganicHumanPath(uniqueList, true);
                for (let i = 0; i < organicQueue.length; i++) {
                    const p = organicQueue[i];
                    praytMxoEngine.putPixel(p.x, p.y, p.colorId, {
                        forcePlace: true,
                        isSlowCleanup: true,
                        isHumanDefense: true,
                        turnPause: !!p.turnPause,
                        strokePause: !!p.strokePause,
                        colorSwitchPause: !!p.colorSwitchPause,
                        strokeStep: p.strokeStep || i
                    });
                }
                praytMxoEngine.start();
                setStatus(`🛡️ İNSANSI RESİM ONARIM (${organicQueue.length} px)`);
            }, reactionDelay);
        }
    }

    function scanAndQueueDamagedImagePixels() {
        const img = state.prayt.protectImage;
        const origin = state.prayt.protectOrigin;
        if (!img || !origin) return;
        if (typeof praytMxoEngine !== 'undefined' && praytMxoEngine.isDrawing()) return;
        const imgW = img.w, imgH = img.h, imgData = img.data;
        const scale = state.image.scale || 1;
        const ox = origin.x, oy = origin.y;
        const palette = state.prayt.autoPalette || getPaletteColors();
        if (!state.prayt.autoPalette) state.prayt.autoPalette = palette;

        takeSnapshot({
            x1: ox,
            y1: oy,
            x2: ox + imgW * scale - 1,
            y2: oy + imgH * scale - 1
        });

        if (!img._cachedTargetIds || img._cachedTargetPalette !== palette) {
            img._cachedTargetIds = new Int16Array(imgW * imgH);
            img._cachedTargetPalette = palette;
            for (let sy = 0; sy < imgH; sy++) {
                for (let sx = 0; sx < imgW; sx++) {
                    const idx = (sy * imgW + sx) * 4;
                    if (imgData[idx + 3] < 128) {
                        img._cachedTargetIds[sy * imgW + sx] = -1;
                    } else {
                        const pid = nearestPaletteId(imgData[idx], imgData[idx+1], imgData[idx+2], palette);
                        img._cachedTargetIds[sy * imgW + sx] = pid !== null ? pid : -1;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        const nowPerf = performance.now();
        const damaged = [];
        for (let sy = 0; sy < imgH; sy++) {
            for (let sx = 0; sx < imgW; sx++) {
                const expId = img._cachedTargetIds[sy * imgW + sx];
                if (expId < 0) continue;
                const idx = (sy * imgW + sx) * 4;
                for (let dy = 0; dy < scale; dy++) {
                    for (let dx = 0; dx < scale; dx++) {
                        const cx = ox + sx * scale + dx;
                        const cy = oy + sy * scale + dy;
                        const key = `${cx},${cy}`;
                        if (praytMxoEngine.priorityTaskKeys && praytMxoEngine.priorityTaskKeys.has(key)) continue;
                        if (praytMxoEngine.inFlightMap) {
                            const ifl = praytMxoEngine.inFlightMap.get(key);
                            if (ifl && (nowPerf - ifl.ts) < 1500) continue;
                        }
                        if (state.prayt.seaSkip && (isCanvasSeaPixel(cx, cy) || state.prayt.seaColors.has(expId) || isSeaRGB(imgData[idx], imgData[idx+1], imgData[idx+2]))) continue;
                        if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(cx, cy, expId)) {
                            damaged.push({ x: cx, y: cy, colorId: expId, forcePlace: true });
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if (damaged.length === 0) return;

        if (state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect) {
            const organic = buildOrganicHumanPath(damaged, true);
            for (const p of organic) {
                praytMxoEngine.putPixel(p.x, p.y, p.colorId, {
                    forcePlace: true,
                    isSlowCleanup: true,
                    isHumanDefense: true,
                    strokePause: !!p.strokePause,
                    colorSwitchPause: !!p.colorSwitchPause
                });
            }
            praytMxoEngine.start();
            setStatus(`🛡️ İNSANSI RESİM DEVRİYE (${organic.length} px)`);
        } else {
            const sortedDamaged = groupByFirstHandColor(damaged);
            let directFired = 0;
            for (const p of sortedDamaged) {
                const key = `${p.x},${p.y}`;
                if (directFired < 14 && state.ws && state.wsReady && state.ws.readyState === 1 &&
                    nowPerf >= (praytMxoEngine.rateLimitBackoffUntil || 0) &&
                    _wsPixelTimestamps.length < 58) {
                    const sentNow = wsSend('p', `[${p.x},${p.y},${p.colorId},1]`, true);
                    if (sentNow) {
                        directFired++;
                        markPlaced(p.x, p.y, p.colorId);
                        praytMxoEngine.inFlightMap.set(key, { colorId: p.colorId, ts: nowPerf });
                        praytMxoEngine.verifyMap.set(key, { x: p.x, y: p.y, colorId: p.colorId, checkAt: nowPerf + 150 });
                        praytMxoEngine.recentSentRing.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y, colorId: p.colorId, ts: nowPerf });
                        if (praytMxoEngine.recentSentRing.length > 16) praytMxoEngine.recentSentRing.shift();
                        state.bot.placed++;
                        state.prayt.totalPlacedSession = (state.prayt.totalPlacedSession || 0) + 1;
                        if (!state.prayt.placedTimestamps) state.prayt.placedTimestamps = [];
                        state.prayt.placedTimestamps.push(nowPerf);
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                praytMxoEngine.putPixelWithPriority(p.x, p.y, p.colorId, {
                    forcePlace: true,
                    isPriorityDefense: true,
                    colorSwitchPause: !!p.colorSwitchPause
                });
            }
            praytMxoEngine.start();
        }
    }

    // --- Floyd-Steinberg Dithering ---
    function praytFloydSteinberg(imgData, w, h, palette) {
        const src = imgData.data;
        const dithered = new Uint8ClampedArray(src);
        const err = new Float32Array(w * h * 4);
        for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
            for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
                const i = (y * w + x) * 4;
                const r = src[i] + err[i];
                const g = src[i + 1] + err[i + 1];
                const b = src[i + 2] + err[i + 2];
                let bestR = 0, bestG = 0, bestB = 0, bestDist = Infinity;
                for (const c of palette) {
                    const dist = (r - c.r)**2 + (g - c.g)**2 + (b - c.b)**2;
                    if (dist < bestDist) { bestDist = dist; bestR = c.r; bestG = c.g; bestB = c.b; }
                }
                dithered[i] = bestR;
                dithered[i + 1] = bestG;
                dithered[i + 2] = bestB;
                dithered[i + 3] = src[i + 3];
                const eR = r - bestR, eG = g - bestG, eB = b - bestB;
                if (x + 1 < w) {
                    err[i + 4] += eR * 7 / 16;
                    err[i + 5] += eR * 7 / 16;
                    err[i + 6] += eR * 7 / 16;
                }
                if (y + 1 < h) {
                    if (x > 0) {
                        err[i + 4*w - 4] += eR * 3 / 16;
                        err[i + 4*w - 3] += eG * 3 / 16;
                        err[i + 4*w - 2] += eB * 3 / 16;
                    }
                    err[i + 4*w] += eR * 5 / 16;
                    err[i + 4*w + 1] += eG * 5 / 16;
                    err[i + 4*w + 2] += eB * 5 / 16;
                    if (x + 1 < w) {
                        err[i + 4*w + 4] += eR * 1 / 16;
                        err[i + 4*w + 5] += eG * 1 / 16;
                        err[i + 4*w + 6] += eB * 1 / 16;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return { data: dithered, width: w, height: h };
    }

    // --- Atkinson Dithering (Retro Mac Style - Ultra Crisp Pixels) ---
    function praytAtkinson(imgData, w, h, palette) {
        const src = imgData.data;
        const dithered = new Uint8ClampedArray(src);
        const err = new Float32Array(w * h * 4);
        for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
            for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
                const i = (y * w + x) * 4;
                const r = clamp(src[i] + err[i], 0, 255);
                const g = clamp(src[i + 1] + err[i + 1], 0, 255);
                const b = clamp(src[i + 2] + err[i + 2], 0, 255);
                let bestR = 0, bestG = 0, bestB = 0, bestDist = Infinity;
                for (const c of palette) {
                    const dist = (r - c.r)**2 + (g - c.g)**2 + (b - c.b)**2;
                    if (dist < bestDist) { bestDist = dist; bestR = c.r; bestG = c.g; bestB = c.b; }
                }
                dithered[i] = bestR;
                dithered[i + 1] = bestG;
                dithered[i + 2] = bestB;
                dithered[i + 3] = src[i + 3];
                const eR = (r - bestR) / 8;
                const eG = (g - bestG) / 8;
                const eB = (b - bestB) / 8;
                const spread = [
                    [1, 0], [2, 0],
                    [-1, 1], [0, 1], [1, 1],
                    [0, 2]
                ];
                for (const [dx, dy] of spread) {
                    const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;
                    if (nx >= 0 && nx < w && ny >= 0 && ny < h) {
                        const ni = (ny * w + nx) * 4;
                        err[ni] += eR;
                        err[ni + 1] += eG;
                        err[ni + 2] += eB;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return { data: dithered, width: w, height: h };
    }

    // --- Bayer 4x4 Ordered Dithering ---
    function praytBayer4x4(imgData, w, h, palette) {
        const src = imgData.data;
        const dithered = new Uint8ClampedArray(src);
        const bayerMatrix = [
            [ 0,  8,  2, 10],
            [12,  4, 14,  6],
            [ 3, 11,  1,  9],
            [15,  7, 13,  5]
        ];
        for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
            for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
                const i = (y * w + x) * 4;
                const factor = (bayerMatrix[y % 4][x % 4] / 16 - 0.5) * 42;
                const r = clamp(src[i] + factor, 0, 255);
                const g = clamp(src[i + 1] + factor, 0, 255);
                const b = clamp(src[i + 2] + factor, 0, 255);
                let bestR = 0, bestG = 0, bestB = 0, bestDist = Infinity;
                for (const c of palette) {
                    const dist = (r - c.r)**2 + (g - c.g)**2 + (b - c.b)**2;
                    if (dist < bestDist) { bestDist = dist; bestR = c.r; bestG = c.g; bestB = c.b; }
                }
                dithered[i] = bestR;
                dithered[i + 1] = bestG;
                dithered[i + 2] = bestB;
                dithered[i + 3] = src[i + 3];
            }
        }
        return { data: dithered, width: w, height: h };
    }


    // --- Map Cache (download canvas PNG) ---
    function praytInitMapCache() {
        const canvasId = parseInt(location.pathname.replace("/", "").split("-")[0]);
        if (isNaN(canvasId)) return;
        const url = `https://pixelplace.io/canvas/${canvasId}.png?a=${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1e9) + 1e9}`;
        const img = new Image();
        img.crossOrigin = "anonymous";
        const spare = document.createElement("canvas");
        const sctx = spare.getContext("2d");
        img.onload = () => {
            state.prayt.mapWidth = img.naturalWidth;
            state.prayt.mapHeight = img.naturalHeight;
            spare.width = img.naturalWidth;
            spare.height = img.naturalHeight;
            sctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
            try {
                const imgData = sctx.getImageData(0, 0, img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight).data;
                state.prayt.mapCache = new Int16Array(img.naturalWidth * img.naturalHeight);
                const palette = getPaletteColors();
                const colorLookup = new Map();
                for (let i = 0; i < imgData.length; i += 4) {
                    if (imgData[i+3] === 0) {
                        state.prayt.mapCache[i >> 2] = -1;
                        continue;
                    }
                    const rgbKey = (imgData[i] << 16) | (imgData[i+1] << 8) | imgData[i+2];
                    let cid = colorLookup.get(rgbKey);
                    if (cid === undefined) {
                        cid = nearestPaletteId(imgData[i], imgData[i+1], imgData[i+2], palette);
                        colorLookup.set(rgbKey, cid);
                    }
                    state.prayt.mapCache[i >> 2] = cid;
                }
                state.prayt.mapLoaded = true;
                if (typeof takeSnapshot === 'function') takeSnapshot();
                console.log("[PraytClient] Map cache yuklendi (hizli memoize):", state.prayt.mapWidth, "x", state.prayt.mapHeight);
            } catch (err) {
                console.warn("[PraytClient] Map cache CORS uyarisi:", err);
            }
        };
        img.src = url;
    }

    // --- Draw Strategy (Always Finishes First Hand Color 100% Before Switching Colors) ---
    function praytSortQueue(queue) {
        if (!queue || queue.length === 0) return queue;

        // Katmanlı bayrak / çift aşamalı çizim: Önce Katman 0 (Kırmızı Zemin), sonra Katman 1 (Sırayla Altın ve Beyaz Simgeler)
        if (queue.some(p => p.isLayered)) {
            const layer0 = queue.filter(p => !p.layer || p.layer === 0);
            const layer1 = queue.filter(p => p.layer === 1);
            const sorted0 = praytSortQueue(layer0.map(p => ({ ...p, isLayered: false })));

            // Layer 1 birden fazla renk içeriyorsa (örn. Altın sarısı tezhip ve Beyaz ay-yıldız):
            // İnsan gibi her rengi eksiksiz bitirip sonra diğer renge geçsin (yapay durmayan doğal el hareketi)!
            const groups = new Map();
            for (const p of layer1) {
                const cId = p.colorId !== undefined ? p.colorId : 0;
                if (!groups.has(cId)) groups.set(cId, []);
                groups.get(cId).push(p);
            }
            const sorted1 = [];
            for (const [cId, pixels] of groups.entries()) {
                const subSorted = praytSortQueue(pixels.map(p => ({ ...p, isLayered: false })));
                if (subSorted.length > 0) {
                    subSorted[0] = { ...subSorted[0], colorSwitchPause: true };
                    sorted1.push(...subSorted);
                }
            }

            return [...sorted0, ...sorted1];
        }

        let strategy = state.prayt.drawStrategy;
        if (!strategy || strategy === "topdown" || strategy === "sequential") {
            if (state.bot.mode && state.bot.mode !== "horizontal" && state.bot.mode !== "sequential") {
                strategy = state.bot.mode;
            }
        }

        // 1. İNSAN KARALAMASI (PRO PIXELPLACE CONNECTED HUMAN BRUSH)
        if (strategy === "human" || state.bot.mode === "human") {
            return buildHumanScribbleQueue(queue, state.savedArea);
        }

        // 2. ZIG-ZAG (Yılan / Boustrophedon - Maksimum Hız & Sıfır Fare Atlama)
        if (strategy === "zigzag" || state.bot.mode === "zigzag") {
            const rows = new Map();
            for (const p of queue) {
                if (!rows.has(p.y)) rows.set(p.y, []);
                rows.get(p.y).push(p);
            }
            const sortedYs = Array.from(rows.keys()).sort((a, b) => a - b);
            const res = [];
            sortedYs.forEach((y, idx) => {
                const row = rows.get(y);
                if (idx % 2 === 0) row.sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x);
                else row.sort((a, b) => b.x - a.x);
                res.push(...row);
            });
            return res;
        }

        // 3. BORDER FIRST (Önce Alan Sınırları / Kontur)
        if (strategy === "border_first" || state.bot.mode === "border_first") {
            let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
            for (const p of queue) {
                if (p.x < minX) minX = p.x; if (p.x > maxX) maxX = p.x;
                if (p.y < minY) minY = p.y; if (p.y > maxY) maxY = p.y;
            }
            const isBorder = (p) => (p.x === minX || p.x === maxX || p.y === minY || p.y === maxY);
            const borders = queue.filter(isBorder);
            const inners = queue.filter(p => !isBorder(p));
            return [...borders, ...inners];
        }

        // 4. COLOR CLUSTER (Elindeki / En Çok Olan Rengi Önce Bitir)
        if (strategy === "color_cluster" || state.bot.mode === "color_cluster") {
            return groupByFirstHandColor(queue, (group) => group.sort((a, b) => a.y - b.y || a.x - b.x));
        }

        // 5. RADIUS ATTACK
        if (strategy === "radius_attack" || state.bot.mode === "radius_attack") {
            return buildSingleColorOrganicPath(queue, false);
        }

        // 6. NUKE
        if (strategy === "nuke" || state.bot.mode === "nuke") {
            for (let i = queue.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
                const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
                [queue[i], queue[j]] = [queue[j], queue[i]];
            }
            return queue;
        }

        // 7. ORDERED / SIRALI (Varsayılan: Satır satır doğrudan tek seferde basılır)
        if (!strategy || strategy === "sequential" || strategy === "topdown" || strategy === "horizontal") {
            return queue.slice().sort((a, b) => a.y - b.y || a.x - b.x);
        }

        // 7. DİĞER GRUPLAMALAR
        return groupByFirstHandColor(queue, (group) => {
            if (!strategy || strategy === "topdown" || strategy === "horizontal" || strategy === "sequential") {
                return group.sort((a, b) => a.y - b.y || a.x - b.x);
            }
            if (strategy === "random") {
                for (let i = group.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
                    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
                    [group[i], group[j]] = [group[j], group[i]];
                }
                return group;
            }
            if (strategy === "bottomup") return group.sort((a, b) => b.y - a.y || a.x - b.x);
            if (strategy === "leftright" || strategy === "vertical") return group.sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x || a.y - b.y);
            if (strategy === "rightleft") return group.sort((a, b) => b.x - a.x || a.y - b.y);
            if (strategy === "spiral" || state.bot.mode === "spiral") {
                let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
                for (const p of group) {
                    if (p.x < minX) minX = p.x; if (p.x > maxX) maxX = p.x;
                    if (p.y < minY) minY = p.y; if (p.y > maxY) maxY = p.y;
                }
                const cx = (minX + maxX) / 2, cy = (minY + maxY) / 2;
                return group.sort((a, b) => {
                    const ra = (a.x - cx)**2 + (a.y - cy)**2;
                    const rb = (b.x - cx)**2 + (b.y - cy)**2;
                    return ra - rb || Math.atan2(a.y - cy, a.x - cx) - Math.atan2(b.y - cy, b.x - cx);
                });
            }
            if (strategy === "checkerboard" || state.bot.mode === "checkerboard") {
                const evens = group.filter(p => (p.x + p.y) % 2 === 0);
                const odds = group.filter(p => (p.x + p.y) % 2 === 1);
                return [...evens, ...odds];
            }
            return group;
        });
    }

    // --- Nudge ---
    function praytNudge(dx, dy) {
        const step = state.prayt.nudgeStep || 1;
        if (state.image.origin) {
            state.image.origin.x += dx * step;
            state.image.origin.y += dy * step;
            updateImagePreview();
            updateImageInfo();
        }
        if (state.text.origin) {
            state.text.origin.x += dx * step;
            state.text.origin.y += dy * step;
            updateTextPreview();
            updateTextInfo();
        }
    }

    // --- Clipboard Paste ---
    let _clipboardListenerAdded = false;
    function setupClipboardPaste() {
        if (_clipboardListenerAdded) return;
        _clipboardListenerAdded = true;
        document.addEventListener("paste", (e) => {
            if (!state.prayt.clipboard) return;
            const items = (e.clipboardData || e.originalEvent.clipboardData).items;
            let blob = null;
            for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
                if (items[i].type.indexOf("image") === 0) blob = items[i].getAsFile();
            }
            if (blob) {
                loadImageFile(blob);
                console.log("[PraytClient] Resim panodan yapistirildi");
            }
        });
    }

    function updatePraytProtectInfo() {
        const info = $("#w3x-prayt-protect-info");
        if (!info) return;
        const qCount = praytMxoEngine.priorityTasks.length + Math.max(0, praytMxoEngine.tasks.length - praytMxoEngine.head);
        let html = "<b>MXO DEFENSE:</b> ";
        if (state.prayt.realtimeProtect) {
            html += `<span style="color:#00e676">IMG ACTIVE (${qCount} in queue)</span>`;
        } else if (state.bot.protect) {
            const mapType = state.prayt.areaTargetMap ? state.prayt.areaTargetMap.type.toUpperCase() : "AREA";
            html += `<span style="color:#00e676">${mapType} 0MS ACTIVE (${qCount} in queue)</span>`;
        } else if (state.bot.humanProtect) {
            const mapType = state.prayt.areaTargetMap ? state.prayt.areaTargetMap.type.toUpperCase() : "AREA";
            html += `<span style="color:#58a6ff">${mapType} HUMAN ACTIVE (${qCount} in queue)</span>`;
        } else {
            html += `<span style="color:#888">IDLE</span>`;
        }
        info.innerHTML = html;
    }

    function updateIgnoreInfo() {
        const info = $("#w3x-prayt-ignore-info");
        if (!info) return;
        info.innerHTML = `<b>IGNORED:</b> ${state.prayt.ignoreColors.size} color(s)`;
    }

    function updatePraytStats() {
        const now = performance.now();
        if (state.prayt.placedTimestamps) {
            state.prayt.placedTimestamps = state.prayt.placedTimestamps.filter(t => now - t <= 1000);
            state.prayt.cps = state.prayt.placedTimestamps.length;
        }
        const cpsEl = document.getElementById('prayt-live-cps');
        if (cpsEl) {
            cpsEl.textContent = state.prayt.cps || 0;
            cpsEl.style.color = (state.prayt.cps >= 25) ? '#ff0055' : (state.prayt.cps >= 10 ? '#00e5ff' : '#00e676');
        }
        const placedEl = document.getElementById('prayt-live-placed');
        if (placedEl) {
            placedEl.textContent = (state.prayt.totalPlacedSession || state.bot.placed || 0).toLocaleString();
        }

        const pingEl = document.getElementById('prayt-live-ping');
        if (pingEl) {
            const p = state.prayt.ping || 0;
            pingEl.textContent = p > 0 ? `${p}ms` : '--ms';
            pingEl.style.color = (p <= 35) ? '#00e676' : (p <= 75 ? '#ffd54f' : '#ff5252');
        }

        const defendedEl = document.getElementById('prayt-live-defended');
        if (defendedEl) {
            const godCount = (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined' && praytGodDefense.counteredCount) || 0;
            const humanCount = (typeof praytHumanDuo !== 'undefined' && praytHumanDuo.counteredCount) || 0;
            defendedEl.textContent = (godCount + humanCount).toLocaleString();
        }

        const pulseEl = document.getElementById('prayt-ws-pulse');
        if (pulseEl) {
            if (state.wsReady) pulseEl.className = 'prayt-pulse-dot';
            else if (state.ws) pulseEl.className = 'prayt-pulse-dot connecting';
            else pulseEl.className = 'prayt-pulse-dot offline';
        }

        const statsEl = $("#w3x-prayt-stats");
        if (!statsEl) return;
        if (!state.prayt.statsStartTime || !state.bot.placed) {
            statsEl.innerHTML = "<b>STATS:</b> 0 px/min | 0 placed";
            return;
        }
        const elapsedMins = (Date.now() - state.prayt.statsStartTime) / 60000;
        const ppm = elapsedMins > 0 ? Math.round(state.bot.placed / elapsedMins) : 0;
        statsEl.innerHTML = `<b>STATS:</b> ${ppm.toLocaleString()} px/min | ${state.bot.placed.toLocaleString()} placed | ${state.bot.skipped.toLocaleString()} skipped`;
    }

    // =========================================================
    // AUTO FLAG GENERATOR (TURKISH & LGBT FLAGS - SEA & PROTECT AWARE)
    // =========================================================

    // =========================================================
    // TURKISH ART & INTERNET TEMPLATE SUITE (HIGH DEFINITION)
    // Made by Prayt
    // =========================================================

    // 1. Resmi / Klasik Türk Bayrağı (Türk Bayrağı Kanunu Standartlarında)
    
    // =========================================================================
    // 🇹🇷 EFSANE TARİHÎ TÜRK BAYRAĞI (HEPSİ TEK BİR BAYRAĞIN İÇİNDE!)
    // Atatürk Silüeti & İmzası + Anıtkabir + Cami + Safranbolu Evleri + Çay + Hilal-Yıldız
    // Made by Prayt
        // =========================================================================
    // =========================================================================
    // =========================================================================
    // 🇹🇷 GRAND ARTWORK: İHTİŞAMLI TARİHÎ TÜRK SANCAĞI & BAYRAĞI
    // - Asil Al & Koyu Bordo İpek Kumaş Zemini (Crimson Imperial Silk)
    // - Altın Selçuklu & Osmanlı Tezhip Bordürü
    // - 4 Köşede 8 Köşeli Altın Selçuklu Yıldızları & Yakut Taşlar
    // - Orhun Abideleri Göktürkçe "𐱅𐰇𐰼𐰰  TÜRK" Altın Kitabesi
    // - Saf Asil Türk Kırmızısı Zemin (#E30A17) - Kesinlikle gradyan/gürültü içermez!
    // - Altın Selçuklu Tezhip Çerçevesi & 8 Köşeli Selçuklu Köşe Yıldızları (#FFD700)
    // - Sol Kanat: Asil Altın Kayı Boyu Tamgası ("I Y I") - Keskin oklar & yay
    // - Merkez: Görkemli Beyaz & Altın Haresiyle Ay-Yıldız (#FFFFFF & #FFD700)
    // - Sağ Kanat: Asil Altın Bozkurt (Göktürk Kurt Başı Silüeti)
    // - Üst Merkez: El Çizimi Geometrik Göktürkçe "𐱅𐰇𐰼𐰰" (TÜRK) Yazıtı
    // =========================================================================
    function praytDrawGrandTurkishHistoryFlag(off, w, h) {
        const ctx = (off && off.getContext) ? off.getContext('2d') : off;
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

        const minDim = Math.min(w, h);

        // 1. ZEMİN: SAF & ASİL TÜRK KIRMIZISI (#E30A17)
        // Kesinlikle gradyan/şeffaflık içermez; paletteki kırmızıyla %100 birebir eşleşir!
        ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

        // 2. DIŞ BORDÜR: ALTIN SELÇUKLU TEZHİP ÇERÇEVESİ (#FFD700)
        const borderInset = Math.max(1, Math.round(minDim * 0.035));
        const borderWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(minDim * 0.022));
        ctx.save();
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#FFD700';
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFD700';
        ctx.lineWidth = borderWidth;
        ctx.strokeRect(borderInset, borderInset, w - borderInset * 2, h - borderInset * 2);

        // İnce iç altın çerçeve (Alan yeterince büyükse)
        if (minDim >= 36 && borderInset >= 2) {
            const innerInset = borderInset + borderWidth + 2;
            if (w - innerInset * 2 > 10 && h - innerInset * 2 > 10) {
                ctx.lineWidth = 1;
                ctx.strokeRect(innerInset, innerInset, w - innerInset * 2, h - innerInset * 2);
            }
        }

        // 8 Köşeli Selçuklu Yıldızı Fonksiyonu (Tam geometrik altın yıldız)
        function drawSeljukCornerStar(cx, cy, r) {
            if (r < 2) return;
            ctx.save();
            ctx.fillStyle = '#FFD700';
            ctx.beginPath();
            for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
                const angle = i * Math.PI / 8;
                const rad = (i % 2 === 0) ? r : r * 0.44;
                const sx = cx + Math.cos(angle) * rad;
                const sy = cy + Math.sin(angle) * rad;
                if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(sx, sy);
                else ctx.lineTo(sx, sy);
            }
            ctx.closePath();
            ctx.fill();
            // Ortasında kırmızı yakut nokta
            if (r >= 4) {
                ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.arc(cx, cy, r * 0.32, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                ctx.fill();
            }
            ctx.restore();
        }

        // 4 Köşeye Selçuklu Yıldızları
        const starCornerR = Math.max(2, Math.round(minDim * 0.045));
        const cOffset = borderInset + starCornerR + 1;
        drawSeljukCornerStar(cOffset, cOffset, starCornerR);
        drawSeljukCornerStar(w - cOffset, cOffset, starCornerR);
        drawSeljukCornerStar(cOffset, h - cOffset, starCornerR);
        drawSeljukCornerStar(w - cOffset, h - cOffset, starCornerR);
        ctx.restore();

        // 3. ÜST MERKEZ: GEOMETRİK EL ÇİZİMİ GÖKTÜRKÇE "𐱅𐰇𐰼𐰰" (TÜRK) RUNLARI
        // Sistem fontlarına bağımlı değildir; her ekranda ve cihazda jilet gibi kusursuz çizer!
        if (w >= 50 && h >= 32) {
            ctx.save();
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#FFD700';
            ctx.fillStyle = '#FFD700';
            const runeThick = Math.max(1, Math.round(minDim * 0.024));
            ctx.lineWidth = runeThick;
            ctx.lineCap = 'round';
            ctx.lineJoin = 'round';

            const runeAreaW = Math.round(minDim * 0.38);
            const runeH = Math.max(6, Math.round(minDim * 0.11));
            const runeY = borderInset + borderWidth + 2;
            const runeSpacing = runeAreaW / 4;
            const startRuneX = (w - runeAreaW) / 2 + runeSpacing * 0.5;

            // Orhun alfabesi sağdan sola okunur: 𐱅 (T), 𐰇 (Ü), 𐰼 (R), 𐰰 (K)
            // 1. Rün: 𐱅 (T)
            const r1X = startRuneX;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(r1X, runeY);
            ctx.lineTo(r1X, runeY + runeH);
            ctx.moveTo(r1X - runeSpacing * 0.35, runeY + runeH * 0.25);
            ctx.lineTo(r1X + runeSpacing * 0.35, runeY + runeH * 0.25);
            ctx.moveTo(r1X - runeSpacing * 0.35, runeY + runeH * 0.25);
            ctx.lineTo(r1X - runeSpacing * 0.35, runeY);
            ctx.moveTo(r1X + runeSpacing * 0.35, runeY + runeH * 0.25);
            ctx.lineTo(r1X + runeSpacing * 0.35, runeY);
            ctx.stroke();

            // 2. Rün: 𐰇 (Ü)
            const r2X = startRuneX + runeSpacing;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(r2X, runeY);
            ctx.lineTo(r2X, runeY + runeH);
            ctx.moveTo(r2X - runeSpacing * 0.30, runeY + runeH * 0.35);
            ctx.lineTo(r2X, runeY + runeH * 0.70);
            ctx.lineTo(r2X + runeSpacing * 0.30, runeY + runeH * 0.35);
            ctx.stroke();

            // 3. Rün: 𐰼 (R)
            const r3X = startRuneX + runeSpacing * 2;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(r3X - runeSpacing * 0.25, runeY);
            ctx.lineTo(r3X - runeSpacing * 0.25, runeY + runeH);
            ctx.moveTo(r3X - runeSpacing * 0.25, runeY + runeH * 0.15);
            ctx.lineTo(r3X + runeSpacing * 0.25, runeY + runeH * 0.35);
            ctx.lineTo(r3X - runeSpacing * 0.25, runeY + runeH * 0.55);
            ctx.moveTo(r3X - runeSpacing * 0.10, runeY + runeH * 0.55);
            ctx.lineTo(r3X + runeSpacing * 0.28, runeY + runeH);
            ctx.stroke();

            // 4. Rün: 𐰰 (K)
            const r4X = startRuneX + runeSpacing * 3;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(r4X, runeY + runeH * 0.35);
            ctx.lineTo(r4X, runeY + runeH);
            ctx.moveTo(r4X - runeSpacing * 0.30, runeY + runeH * 0.40);
            ctx.lineTo(r4X, runeY + runeH * 0.05);
            ctx.lineTo(r4X + runeSpacing * 0.30, runeY + runeH * 0.40);
            ctx.stroke();

            ctx.restore();
        }

        // 4. SOL KANAT: ASİL VE GÜÇLÜ KAYI BOYU TAMGASI (" I Y I ")
        // Osmanlı Devleti'nin kurucusu Kayı Boyu'nun efsanevi tamgası
        if (w >= 36 && h >= 24) {
            ctx.save();
            const iyiCx = w * 0.20;
            const iyiCy = h * 0.54;
            const iyiH = Math.round(minDim * 0.46);
            const iyiW = Math.round(minDim * 0.28);
            const kThick = Math.max(2, Math.round(minDim * 0.034));

            ctx.strokeStyle = '#FFD700';
            ctx.fillStyle = '#FFD700';
            ctx.lineWidth = kThick;
            ctx.lineCap = 'round';
            ctx.lineJoin = 'round';

            // Sol Ok (|)
            const leftX = iyiCx - iyiW * 0.46;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(leftX, iyiCy - iyiH * 0.50);
            ctx.lineTo(leftX, iyiCy + iyiH * 0.50);
            ctx.stroke();
            // Sol sivri ok ucu
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(leftX - kThick * 1.6, iyiCy - iyiH * 0.50 + kThick * 2.2);
            ctx.lineTo(leftX, iyiCy - iyiH * 0.50);
            ctx.lineTo(leftX + kThick * 1.6, iyiCy - iyiH * 0.50 + kThick * 2.2);
            ctx.stroke();

            // Sağ Ok (|)
            const rightX = iyiCx + iyiW * 0.46;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(rightX, iyiCy - iyiH * 0.50);
            ctx.lineTo(rightX, iyiCy + iyiH * 0.50);
            ctx.stroke();
            // Sağ sivri ok ucu
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(rightX - kThick * 1.6, iyiCy - iyiH * 0.50 + kThick * 2.2);
            ctx.lineTo(rightX, iyiCy - iyiH * 0.50);
            ctx.lineTo(rightX + kThick * 1.6, iyiCy - iyiH * 0.50 + kThick * 2.2);
            ctx.stroke();

            // Orta Kavisli Yay
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(iyiCx - iyiW * 0.12, iyiCy - iyiH * 0.48);
            ctx.bezierCurveTo(
                iyiCx + iyiW * 0.40, iyiCy - iyiH * 0.22,
                iyiCx + iyiW * 0.40, iyiCy + iyiH * 0.22,
                iyiCx - iyiW * 0.12, iyiCy + iyiH * 0.48
            );
            ctx.stroke();

            // Yayın gergin kirişi
            ctx.save();
            ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(kThick * 0.55));
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(iyiCx - iyiW * 0.12, iyiCy - iyiH * 0.48);
            ctx.lineTo(iyiCx - iyiW * 0.12, iyiCy + iyiH * 0.48);
            ctx.stroke();
            ctx.restore();

            ctx.restore();
        }

        // 5. MERKEZ: GÖRKEMLİ ALTIN HARELİ BEYAZ AY-YILDIZ
        const cy = h * 0.53;
        const cx1 = w * 0.50;
        const r1 = minDim * 0.25;

        ctx.save();
        // A. Altın Dış Kontur (Hilal'in arkasında asil bir altın çerçeve oluşturur)
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFD700';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx1, cy, r1 * 1.10, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // B. Beyaz Hilal Ana Gövdesi (Saf Kar Beyazı #FFFFFF)
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx1, cy, r1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // C. Hilal İç Kesim Dairesi (Arka plan kırmızısıyla kusursuz kesilir, asla gradyan/leke yapmaz!)
        const cx2 = cx1 + r1 * 0.25;
        const r2 = r1 * 0.79;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx2, cy, r2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // D. 5 Köşeli Türk Yıldızı
        const starX = cx2 + r2 + r1 * 0.28;
        const starR = r1 * 0.50;
        const starInnerR = starR * 0.382;

        // Yıldız Altın Konturu
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFD700';
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            const angle = Math.PI + (i * Math.PI / 5);
            const r = (i % 2 === 0) ? starR * 1.15 : starInnerR * 1.15;
            const sx = starX + Math.cos(angle) * r;
            const sy = cy + Math.sin(angle) * r;
            if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(sx, sy);
            else ctx.lineTo(sx, sy);
        }
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        // Yıldız Beyaz Gövdesi
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            const angle = Math.PI + (i * Math.PI / 5);
            const r = (i % 2 === 0) ? starR : starInnerR;
            const sx = starX + Math.cos(angle) * r;
            const sy = cy + Math.sin(angle) * r;
            if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(sx, sy);
            else ctx.lineTo(sx, sy);
        }
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();

        // 6. SAĞ KANAT: ASİL ALTIN BOZKURT (GÖKTÜRK KURT BAŞI SİLÜETİ)
        // Hilale doğru gururla bakan asil Türk Bozkurdu
        if (w >= 36 && h >= 24) {
            ctx.save();
            const wolfCx = w * 0.82;
            const wolfCy = h * 0.54;
            const wolfS = minDim * 0.40;

            ctx.fillStyle = '#FFD700';
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#FFD700';
            ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(minDim * 0.02));
            ctx.lineJoin = 'round';

            ctx.beginPath();
            // Kulak tepesinden başla (sol kulak - dik ve uyanık)
            ctx.moveTo(wolfCx - wolfS * 0.08, wolfCy - wolfS * 0.50);
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx - wolfS * 0.02, wolfCy - wolfS * 0.28);
            // Sağ kulak arkası
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx + wolfS * 0.08, wolfCy - wolfS * 0.46);
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx + wolfS * 0.14, wolfCy - wolfS * 0.22);
            // Ense ve yele
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx + wolfS * 0.24, wolfCy - wolfS * 0.08);
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx + wolfS * 0.32, wolfCy + wolfS * 0.12);
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx + wolfS * 0.36, wolfCy + wolfS * 0.38);
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx + wolfS * 0.18, wolfCy + wolfS * 0.48);
            // Göğüs ve gerdan tüyleri
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx + wolfS * 0.04, wolfCy + wolfS * 0.42);
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx - wolfS * 0.06, wolfCy + wolfS * 0.48);
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx - wolfS * 0.10, wolfCy + wolfS * 0.32);
            // Alt çene
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx - wolfS * 0.22, wolfCy + wolfS * 0.18);
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx - wolfS * 0.34, wolfCy + wolfS * 0.10);
            // Ağız / dudak çizgisi (hafif açık gururlu ifade)
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx - wolfS * 0.24, wolfCy + wolfS * 0.04);
            // Burun ucu ve namlu
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx - wolfS * 0.42, wolfCy - wolfS * 0.04);
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx - wolfS * 0.38, wolfCy - wolfS * 0.12);
            // Burun kemeri ve alın
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx - wolfS * 0.20, wolfCy - wolfS * 0.18);
            ctx.lineTo(wolfCx - wolfS * 0.12, wolfCy - wolfS * 0.30);
            ctx.closePath();
            ctx.fill();

            // Kurt Gözü (Keskin kırmızı göz yarığı)
            if (minDim >= 38) {
                ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.moveTo(wolfCx - wolfS * 0.16, wolfCy - wolfS * 0.12);
                ctx.lineTo(wolfCx - wolfS * 0.08, wolfCy - wolfS * 0.14);
                ctx.lineTo(wolfCx - wolfS * 0.12, wolfCy - wolfS * 0.08);
                ctx.closePath();
                ctx.fill();
            }

            ctx.restore();
        }

        return off;
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 🇨🇦 KANADA BAYRAĞI (11 KÖŞELİ RESMÎ AKÇAAĞAÇ YAPRAĞI - L'UNIFOLIÉ)
    // - Resmî 1:2 Kırmızı-Beyaz-Kırmızı Triband
    // - 11 Köşeli Hükümet Standardı Akçaağaç Yaprağı Vektörü
    // =========================================================================
    function praytDrawCanadaFlag(off, w, h) {
        const ctx = (off && off.getContext) ? off.getContext('2d') : off;
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

        const rW = Math.round(w * 0.25);
        const mW = Math.round(w * 0.50);

        // Sol Kırmızı Şerit (#D80027)
        ctx.fillStyle = '#D80027';
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, rW, h);

        // Orta Beyaz Kare (#FFFFFF)
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.fillRect(rW, 0, mW, h);

        // Sağ Kırmızı Şerit (#D80027)
        ctx.fillStyle = '#D80027';
        ctx.fillRect(rW + mW, 0, w - (rW + mW), h);

        // 11 Köşeli Resmî Akçaağaç Yaprağı (The National 11-pointed Maple Leaf)
        const maplePoints = [
            [0.0, -0.96],
            [0.09, -0.62],
            [0.25, -0.69],
            [0.19, -0.44],
            [0.52, -0.45],
            [0.38, -0.27],
            [0.63, -0.14],
            [0.39, -0.05],
            [0.47, 0.12],
            [0.23, 0.08],
            [0.05, 0.25],
            [0.035, 0.70],  // Sap sağ kenarı
            [-0.035, 0.70], // Sap sol kenarı
            [-0.05, 0.25],
            [-0.23, 0.08],
            [-0.47, 0.12],
            [-0.39, -0.05],
            [-0.63, -0.14],
            [-0.38, -0.27],
            [-0.52, -0.45],
            [-0.19, -0.44],
            [-0.25, -0.69],
            [-0.09, -0.62]
        ];

        ctx.save();
        const leafScale = Math.min(mW * 0.88, h * 0.78) * 0.52;
        const cx = w * 0.5;
        const cy = h * 0.5;

        // Akçaağaç yaprağı
        ctx.fillStyle = '#D80027';
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i < maplePoints.length; i++) {
            const px = cx + maplePoints[i][0] * leafScale;
            const py = cy + maplePoints[i][1] * leafScale;
            if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py);
            else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
        }
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        // Yaprak üzerinde hafif asil gölgelendirme (Merkez damar derinliği)
        if (leafScale >= 15) {
            ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.25)';
            ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(leafScale * 0.04));
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(cx, cy - leafScale * 0.85);
            ctx.lineTo(cx, cy + leafScale * 0.65);
            ctx.stroke();
        }
        ctx.restore();

        // Zarif İmza: Made by Prayt
        try {
            ctx.save();
            const signSize = Math.max(7, Math.round(h * 0.036));
            ctx.font = `bold ${signSize}px sans-serif`;
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.80)';
            ctx.textAlign = 'right';
            ctx.fillText('Made by Prayt', w - 4, h - 3);
            ctx.restore();
        } catch (_) {}

        return off;
    }

    // =========================================================
    // HARİTADA CANLI ÖNİZLEME (PREVIEW) YÖNETİCİLERİ
    // =========================================================
    function praytRenderFlagPreview(area) {
        if (!area) area = state.savedArea;
        if (!area) return;
        const layer = getAreaLayer();
        if (!layer) return;

        let previewEl = document.getElementById('w3x-flag-preview-canvas');
        if (!state.prayt || !state.prayt.flagPreviewVisible || !state.prayt.flagPreviewActive) {
            if (previewEl) previewEl.remove();
            return;
        }

        const w = Math.max(1, area.x2 - area.x1 + 1);
        const h = Math.max(1, area.y2 - area.y1 + 1);

        if (!previewEl) {
            previewEl = document.createElement('canvas');
            previewEl.id = 'w3x-flag-preview-canvas';
            previewEl.className = 'w3x-flag-preview';
            layer.appendChild(previewEl);
        }

        previewEl.style.position = 'absolute';
        previewEl.style.left = area.x1 + 'px';
        previewEl.style.top = area.y1 + 'px';
        previewEl.style.width = w + 'px';
        previewEl.style.height = h + 'px';
        previewEl.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
        previewEl.style.zIndex = '15';
        previewEl.style.opacity = '' + (state.prayt.flagPreviewOpacity || 0.88);
        previewEl.style.imageRendering = 'pixelated';

        if (previewEl.width !== w || previewEl.height !== h) {
            previewEl.width = w;
            previewEl.height = h;
        }

        praytDrawTurkishFlagArtwork(previewEl, w, h);
    }

    function praytCloseFlagPreview() {
        if (state.prayt) state.prayt.flagPreviewActive = false;
        const el = document.getElementById('w3x-flag-preview-canvas');
        if (el) el.remove();
    }

    function praytShowTurkishFlagPreview(active) {
        if (!state.prayt) return;
        state.prayt.flagPreviewActive = (active !== false);
        state.prayt.flagPreviewVisible = true;
        if (state.savedArea) {
            praytRenderFlagPreview(state.savedArea);
        }
    }

    function praytSyncDimensionHubUI() {
        const textEl = document.getElementById('w3x-dim-wh-text-sanat');
        const badgeEl = document.getElementById('w3x-dim-px-badge-sanat');
        const inputW = document.getElementById('w3x-input-width-sanat');
        const inputH = document.getElementById('w3x-input-height-sanat');

        if (!state.savedArea) {
            if (textEl) textEl.textContent = '0 × 0 px';
            if (badgeEl) badgeEl.textContent = '0 px';
            if (inputW) inputW.value = 0;
            if (inputH) inputH.value = 0;
            return;
        }
        const area = state.savedArea;
        const w = Math.max(1, area.x2 - area.x1 + 1);
        const h = Math.max(1, area.y2 - area.y1 + 1);
        const total = w * h;

        if (textEl) textEl.textContent = w + ' × ' + h + ' px';
        if (badgeEl) badgeEl.textContent = total.toLocaleString() + ' px';
        if (inputW) inputW.value = w;
        if (inputH) inputH.value = h;
    }

    // =========================================================
    // 🗺️ DOĞAL TÜRKİYE HARİTASI (COĞRAFİ VE GERÇEKÇİ SINIRLAR & AY-YILDIZ)
    // =========================================================
    function praytDrawTurkeyMapFlag(off, w, h) {
        const ctx = (off && off.getContext) ? off.getContext('2d') : off;
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

        // 1. Derin Deniz / Okyanus Arka Planı (Doğal Lacivert - Karadeniz, Akdeniz, Ege)
        const seaGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w, h);
        seaGrad.addColorStop(0, '#071526');
        seaGrad.addColorStop(0.5, '#0b1d33');
        seaGrad.addColorStop(1, '#061322');
        ctx.fillStyle = seaGrad;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

        // Doğal Coğrafi Oran (Türkiye en-boy oranı ~2.15:1)
        // Boyut bozulmasını önlemek için seçilen alan içinde en-boy oranını korur
        const targetAspect = 2.15;
        let mapW = w * 0.92;
        let mapH = mapW / targetAspect;
        if (mapH > h * 0.88) {
            mapH = h * 0.88;
            mapW = mapH * targetAspect;
        }
        const padX = (w - mapW) / 2;
        const padY = (h - mapH) / 2;

        const toX = (nx) => padX + nx * mapW;
        const toY = (ny) => padY + ny * mapH;

        // Anadolu Koordinatları (Gerçek Kıyı & Sınır Poligonu)
        const anadoluPts = [
            [0.12, 0.28], [0.16, 0.25], [0.18, 0.22], [0.21, 0.25], [0.24, 0.27],
            [0.27, 0.28], [0.29, 0.26], [0.32, 0.25], [0.30, 0.22], [0.28, 0.20],
            [0.30, 0.17], [0.35, 0.17], [0.40, 0.15], [0.45, 0.13], [0.51, 0.11],
            [0.56, 0.06], // Sinop İnceburun (En kuzey uç)
            [0.58, 0.12], [0.61, 0.12], [0.65, 0.15], [0.68, 0.14], [0.72, 0.17],
            [0.76, 0.18], [0.82, 0.20], [0.87, 0.21], [0.92, 0.21],
            [0.94, 0.24], [0.96, 0.28], [0.97, 0.33], [0.98, 0.38],
            [1.00, 0.41], // Ağrı Dağı / Dilucu (En doğu uç)
            [0.97, 0.46], [0.95, 0.51], [0.95, 0.56], [0.94, 0.61], [0.96, 0.66],
            [0.96, 0.72], // Şemdinli (Güneydoğu en uç)
            [0.91, 0.73], [0.86, 0.71], [0.82, 0.70], [0.78, 0.70], [0.73, 0.71],
            [0.68, 0.72], [0.63, 0.72], [0.58, 0.71], [0.54, 0.71],
            [0.53, 0.75], [0.54, 0.81], [0.52, 0.88],
            [0.49, 0.94], // Hatay Yayladağı (En güney uç)
            [0.47, 0.90], [0.49, 0.80], [0.50, 0.74],
            [0.47, 0.74], [0.45, 0.78], [0.41, 0.75], [0.37, 0.79], [0.34, 0.84],
            [0.32, 0.88], // Anamur Burnu (Anadolu en güney)
            [0.29, 0.84], [0.26, 0.78], [0.23, 0.73], [0.21, 0.78], [0.19, 0.84],
            [0.16, 0.84], [0.14, 0.80], [0.12, 0.76], [0.10, 0.75],
            [0.03, 0.77], // Datça Yarımadası
            [0.07, 0.73], [0.03, 0.70], [0.07, 0.67], [0.04, 0.63], [0.03, 0.59],
            [0.06, 0.57], [0.04, 0.53],
            [0.01, 0.49], // Çeşme Yarımadası
            [0.02, 0.43], // Karaburun ucu
            [0.06, 0.46], [0.05, 0.41], [0.04, 0.37], [0.07, 0.35],
            [0.03, 0.32], // Bababurnu (Anadolu en batı ucu)
            [0.05, 0.29], [0.09, 0.28]
        ];

        // Trakya Koordinatları
        const trakyaPts = [
            [0.09, 0.08], [0.14, 0.07], [0.18, 0.08], [0.22, 0.11], [0.25, 0.14],
            [0.27, 0.16], [0.26, 0.19], [0.24, 0.20], [0.21, 0.21], [0.17, 0.23],
            [0.13, 0.25], [0.10, 0.26], [0.06, 0.27], [0.05, 0.25], [0.07, 0.22],
            [0.05, 0.20], [0.07, 0.17], [0.08, 0.12]
        ];

        // 2. Anadolu ve Trakya Karası - Al Bayrak Kırmızısı (#E30A17)
        ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(toX(anadoluPts[0][0]), toY(anadoluPts[0][1]));
        for (let i = 1; i < anadoluPts.length; i++) {
            ctx.lineTo(toX(anadoluPts[i][0]), toY(anadoluPts[i][1]));
        }
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(toX(trakyaPts[0][0]), toY(trakyaPts[0][1]));
        for (let i = 1; i < trakyaPts.length; i++) {
            ctx.lineTo(toX(trakyaPts[i][0]), toY(trakyaPts[i][1]));
        }
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        // 3. İnce ve Net Kıyı Konturu (Piksel Tuvalde Canlı Görünüm İçin)
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff3b49';
        ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(Math.min(w, h) * 0.006));
        ctx.stroke();

        // 4. İç Göller
        // Van Gölü (Doğu Anadolu'da Turkuaz/Mavi İnci)
        const vanPts = [
            [0.85, 0.51], [0.88, 0.52], [0.89, 0.55],
            [0.87, 0.57], [0.83, 0.57], [0.82, 0.53]
        ];
        ctx.fillStyle = '#0288D1';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(toX(vanPts[0][0]), toY(vanPts[0][1]));
        for (let i = 1; i < vanPts.length; i++) {
            ctx.lineTo(toX(vanPts[i][0]), toY(vanPts[i][1]));
        }
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        // Tuz Gölü (İç Anadolu'da Berrak Beyazımsı Göl)
        const tuzPts = [
            [0.43, 0.44], [0.45, 0.45], [0.46, 0.50], [0.44, 0.53], [0.42, 0.50]
        ];
        ctx.fillStyle = '#E1F5FE';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(toX(tuzPts[0][0]), toY(tuzPts[0][1]));
        for (let i = 1; i < tuzPts.length; i++) {
            ctx.lineTo(toX(tuzPts[i][0]), toY(tuzPts[i][1]));
        }
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        // 5. İç Anadolu'da Şanlı Ay-Yıldız (2893 Sayılı Kanun Oranları)
        const cy = toY(0.42);
        const cx1 = toX(0.46);
        const r1 = Math.max(2.5, Math.min(mapW * 0.09, mapH * 0.17));

        // Beyaz Hilal Dışı
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx1, cy, r1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Kırmızı Hilal İçi (Ay Kesimi)
        const cx2 = cx1 + r1 * 0.25;
        const r2 = r1 * 0.80;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx2, cy, r2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // 5 Köşeli Beyaz Yıldız (Hilale Yönelen Tepe Noktası)
        const starX = cx2 + r2 + r1 * 0.24;
        const starR = Math.max(1.5, r1 * 0.48);
        const starInnerR = starR * 0.382;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            const angle = Math.PI + (i * Math.PI / 5);
            const r = (i % 2 === 0) ? starR : starInnerR;
            const sx = starX + Math.cos(angle) * r;
            const sy = cy + Math.sin(angle) * r;
            if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(sx, sy);
            else ctx.lineTo(sx, sy);
        }
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        return off;
    }

    function praytDrawOfficialTurkishFlag(off, w, h) {
        const ctx = off.getContext('2d');
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

        // Al zemin
        ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

        // Geometrik Kusursuz Hilal (Resmî Türk Bayrağı Kanunu Standartları)
        const cy = h * 0.50;
        const cx1 = w * 0.38;
        const r1 = Math.min(w, h) * 0.28;

        // Dış beyaz çember
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx1, cy, r1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // İç kırmızı kesme
        const cx2 = cx1 + r1 * 0.28;
        const r2 = r1 * 0.80;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx2, cy, r2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Beyaz 5 Köşeli Yıldız (Hilalin ağzına doğru 180 derece hizalı)
        const starX = cx2 + r2 + r1 * 0.28;
        const starR = r1 * 0.50;
        const starInnerR = starR * 0.382;

        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            const angle = Math.PI + (i * Math.PI / 5);
            const r = (i % 2 === 0) ? starR : starInnerR;
            const sx = starX + Math.cos(angle) * r;
            const sy = cy + Math.sin(angle) * r;
            if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(sx, sy);
            else ctx.lineTo(sx, sy);
        }
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        return off;
    }

    // 2. Gazi Mustafa Kemal Atatürk (Asil Kalpaklı Portre Silüeti & İmzası)
    function praytDrawAtaturkArt(off, w, h) {
        praytDrawOfficialTurkishFlag(off, w, h);
        const ctx = off.getContext('2d');

        const ax = w * 0.05;
        const ay = h * 0.08;
        const aw = w * 0.38;
        const ah = h * 0.84;

        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';

        // Kalpak
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(ax + aw * 0.48, ay + ah * 0.22, aw * 0.38, ah * 0.19, -0.06, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Yüz profili
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(ax + aw * 0.42, ay + ah * 0.28);
        ctx.lineTo(ax + aw * 0.62, ay + ah * 0.32);
        ctx.lineTo(ax + aw * 0.58, ay + ah * 0.38);
        ctx.lineTo(ax + aw * 0.72, ay + ah * 0.46);
        ctx.lineTo(ax + aw * 0.58, ay + ah * 0.50);
        ctx.lineTo(ax + aw * 0.62, ay + ah * 0.54);
        ctx.lineTo(ax + aw * 0.56, ay + ah * 0.58);
        ctx.lineTo(ax + aw * 0.66, ay + ah * 0.68);
        ctx.lineTo(ax + aw * 0.48, ay + ah * 0.76);
        ctx.lineTo(ax + aw * 0.82, ay + ah * 0.98);
        ctx.lineTo(ax + aw * 0.08, ay + ah * 0.98);
        ctx.lineTo(ax + aw * 0.20, ay + ah * 0.48);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        // Göz detayı
        ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(ax + aw * 0.58, ay + ah * 0.41, Math.max(1, aw * 0.04), 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // K. Atatürk İmzası (Sağ alt köşe)
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(Math.min(w, h) * 0.015));
        ctx.beginPath();
        const sigX = w * 0.62, sigY = h * 0.82;
        ctx.moveTo(sigX, sigY);
        ctx.lineTo(sigX + w * 0.06, sigY - h * 0.08);
        ctx.lineTo(sigX + w * 0.12, sigY + h * 0.04);
        ctx.lineTo(sigX + w * 0.22, sigY - h * 0.04);
        ctx.lineTo(sigX + w * 0.30, sigY);
        ctx.stroke();

        ctx.restore();
        return off;
    }

    // 3. Görkemli Anıtkabir Mozolesi & Sütunlar
    function praytDrawAnitkabirArt(off, w, h) {
        const ctx = off.getContext('2d');
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

        // Gökyüzü (Alaturka Kırmızı & Lacivert geçiş)
        const skyGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h);
        skyGrad.addColorStop(0, '#1a237e');
        skyGrad.addColorStop(0.55, '#c2185b');
        skyGrad.addColorStop(1, '#b71c1c');
        ctx.fillStyle = skyGrad;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

        // Hilal ve Yıldız gökyüzünde
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(w * 0.82, h * 0.22, Math.min(w, h) * 0.12, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = '#1a237e';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(w * 0.85, h * 0.22, Math.min(w, h) * 0.10, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Anıtkabir Kaidesi (Kademeli Merdivenler)
        const bY = h * 0.68;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#cfd8dc';
        ctx.fillRect(w * 0.10, bY, w * 0.80, h * 0.32);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#90a4ae';
        ctx.fillRect(w * 0.14, bY + h * 0.06, w * 0.72, h * 0.26);

        // Mozole Ana Kütle (Şeref Holü)
        const mX = w * 0.22, mW = w * 0.56, mY = h * 0.32, mH = h * 0.36;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#f5f5f5';
        ctx.fillRect(mX, mY, mW, mH);

        // Çatı Kornişi (Alınlık)
        ctx.fillStyle = '#eceff1';
        ctx.fillRect(mX - w * 0.03, mY - h * 0.06, mW + w * 0.06, h * 0.07);

        // 8 Adet İhtişamlı Kolon / Sütun
        const cols = 8;
        const colW = mW / (cols * 1.8);
        const colGap = (mW - cols * colW) / (cols - 1);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#b0bec5';
        for (let i = 0; i < cols; i++) {
            const cx = mX + i * (colW + colGap);
            ctx.fillRect(cx, mY, colW, mH);
            // Sütun başlığı
            ctx.fillStyle = '#cfd8dc';
            ctx.fillRect(cx - 1, mY, colW + 2, Math.max(2, h * 0.025));
            ctx.fillStyle = '#b0bec5';
        }

        // Göndere Çekilmiş Türk Bayrağı
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#cfd8dc';
        ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(w * 0.012));
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(w * 0.12, h * 0.15);
        ctx.lineTo(w * 0.12, bY);
        ctx.stroke();

        ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
        ctx.fillRect(w * 0.12, h * 0.15, w * 0.08, h * 0.06);

        return off;
    }

    // 4. Osmanlı Selatin Camii (4 Minareli & Kubbeli)
    function praytDrawMosqueArt(off, w, h) {
        const ctx = off.getContext('2d');
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

        // Akşam Alaturka Gökyüzü
        const sky = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h);
        sky.addColorStop(0, '#0d1b2a');
        sky.addColorStop(0.5, '#415a77');
        sky.addColorStop(1, '#e0a96d');
        ctx.fillStyle = sky;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

        // Parlayan Ay
        ctx.fillStyle = '#fff9c4';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(w * 0.5, h * 0.18, Math.min(w, h) * 0.11, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = '#0d1b2a';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(w * 0.53, h * 0.16, Math.min(w, h) * 0.09, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Cami Silüeti (Koyu Lacivert / Siyah)
        ctx.fillStyle = '#0b0c10';

        // Ana Gövde
        ctx.fillRect(w * 0.22, h * 0.58, w * 0.56, h * 0.42);

        // Büyük Merkez Kubbe
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(w * 0.50, h * 0.58, w * 0.20, Math.PI, 0);
        ctx.fill();

        // Merkez Kubbe Alemi
        ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd700';
        ctx.fillRect(w * 0.495, h * 0.34, Math.max(1, w * 0.01), h * 0.04);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(w * 0.50, h * 0.33, Math.max(1, w * 0.015), 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        ctx.fillStyle = '#0b0c10';
        // Yan Yarım Kubbeler
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(w * 0.32, h * 0.62, w * 0.12, Math.PI, 0);
        ctx.arc(w * 0.68, h * 0.62, w * 0.12, Math.PI, 0);
        ctx.fill();

        // 4 Minare (2 İç, 2 Dış)
        const minarets = [w * 0.14, w * 0.24, w * 0.76, w * 0.86];
        minarets.forEach((mx, idx) => {
            const isOuter = (idx === 0 || idx === 3);
            const topY = isOuter ? h * 0.22 : h * 0.18;
            const mw = Math.max(2, w * 0.022);
            ctx.fillRect(mx - mw / 2, topY, mw, h - topY);

            // Külah (Sivri tepe)
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(mx - mw, topY);
            ctx.lineTo(mx + mw, topY);
            ctx.lineTo(mx, topY - h * 0.08);
            ctx.closePath();
            ctx.fill();

            // Şerefe (Balkonlar)
            ctx.fillRect(mx - mw * 1.5, topY + h * 0.14, mw * 3, Math.max(1, h * 0.02));
            ctx.fillRect(mx - mw * 1.5, topY + h * 0.28, mw * 3, Math.max(1, h * 0.02));
        });

        return off;
    }

    // 5. Asil Bozkurt & Hilal (Türk Dünyası Bağımsızlık Sembolü)
    function praytDrawBozkurtArt(off, w, h) {
        const ctx = off.getContext('2d');
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

        // Gece Türk Mavisi & Alacakaranlık
        const bg = ctx.createRadialGradient(w * 0.65, h * 0.35, 2, w * 0.5, h * 0.5, Math.max(w, h));
        bg.addColorStop(0, '#1565c0');
        bg.addColorStop(0.6, '#0d274c');
        bg.addColorStop(1, '#050c18');
        ctx.fillStyle = bg;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

        // Dev Parlayan Dolunay / Hilal
        const moonR = Math.min(w, h) * 0.32;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#e3f2fd';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(w * 0.65, h * 0.38, moonR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        ctx.fillStyle = '#0d274c';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(w * 0.72, h * 0.34, moonR * 0.88, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Bozkurt Silüeti (Uluyan Asil Kurt Başı)
        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
        const bx = w * 0.12, by = h * 0.30;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(bx + w * 0.10, by + h * 0.65); // Göğüs
        ctx.lineTo(bx + w * 0.18, by + h * 0.38); // Boyun altı
        ctx.lineTo(bx + w * 0.28, by + h * 0.24); // Çene
        ctx.lineTo(bx + w * 0.44, by + h * 0.04); // Burun ucu (yukarı kalkık)
        ctx.lineTo(bx + w * 0.40, by + h * 0.12); // Burun üstü
        ctx.lineTo(bx + w * 0.36, by + h * 0.16); // Alın
        ctx.lineTo(bx + w * 0.34, by + h * 0.02); // Kulak önü
        ctx.lineTo(bx + w * 0.30, by + h * 0.08); // Kulak arkası
        ctx.lineTo(bx + w * 0.26, by + h * 0.18); // Ense başı
        ctx.lineTo(bx + w * 0.28, by + h * 0.05); // İkinci kulak
        ctx.lineTo(bx + w * 0.23, by + h * 0.16);
        ctx.lineTo(bx + w * 0.18, by + h * 0.35); // Ense yelesi
        ctx.lineTo(bx + w * 0.12, by + h * 0.50);
        ctx.lineTo(bx, by + h * 0.65); // Sırt
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        // Parlayan Göz
        ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd54f';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(bx + w * 0.34, by + h * 0.17, Math.max(1, w * 0.015), 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        ctx.restore();
        return off;
    }

    // 6. Geleneksel İnce Belli Türk Çayı
    function praytDrawTeaArt(off, w, h) {
        const ctx = off.getContext('2d');
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

        // Arka plan: Türk Kırmızısı & Ahşap Doku
        const bg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h);
        bg.addColorStop(0, '#3e2723');
        bg.addColorStop(1, '#b71c1c');
        ctx.fillStyle = bg;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

        const cx = w * 0.50, cy = h * 0.58;
        const gw = w * 0.32, gh = h * 0.58;

        // Çay Tabağı (Saucer)
        ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(cx, cy + gh * 0.48, gw * 1.1, gh * 0.18, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = '#b71c1c';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(cx, cy + gh * 0.48, gw * 0.95, gh * 0.13, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // İnce Belli Bardak Gövdesi (Tavşan Kanı Çay)
        ctx.save();
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(cx - gw * 0.45, cy - gh * 0.45);
        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx - gw * 0.18, cy, cx - gw * 0.40, cy + gh * 0.42);
        ctx.lineTo(cx + gw * 0.40, cy + gh * 0.42);
        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx + gw * 0.18, cy, cx + gw * 0.45, cy - gh * 0.45);
        ctx.closePath();

        // Çay sıvısı
        const teaGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, cy - gh * 0.2, 0, cy + gh * 0.4);
        teaGrad.addColorStop(0, '#ff1744');
        teaGrad.addColorStop(0.5, '#c62828');
        teaGrad.addColorStop(1, '#4a0007');
        ctx.fillStyle = teaGrad;
        ctx.fill();

        // Bardak Cam Parlaması
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)';
        ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(w * 0.015));
        ctx.stroke();

        // Çay Dumanı (Buhar)
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.45)';
        ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(w * 0.012));
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(cx - gw * 0.15, cy - gh * 0.48);
        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx - gw * 0.35, cy - gh * 0.70, cx - gw * 0.10, cy - gh * 0.85);
        ctx.moveTo(cx + gw * 0.10, cy - gh * 0.48);
        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx + gw * 0.30, cy - gh * 0.68, cx + gw * 0.05, cy - gh * 0.88);
        ctx.stroke();

        ctx.restore();
        return off;
    }

    // 7. Can Azerbaycan Bayrağı
    function praytDrawAzerbaijanArt(off, w, h) {
        const ctx = off.getContext('2d');
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
        const sh = h / 3;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#00B5E2'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, sh);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#EF3340'; ctx.fillRect(0, sh, w, sh);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#509E2F'; ctx.fillRect(0, sh * 2, w, sh);

        // Beyaz Hilal ve 8 Köşeli Yıldız
        const cy = h * 0.5, cx = w * 0.46, r1 = sh * 0.42;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r1, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = '#EF3340';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx + r1 * 0.3, cy, r1 * 0.82, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();

        // 8 Köşeli Yıldız
        const sx = cx + r1 * 1.35, sr = r1 * 0.52, sir = sr * 0.54;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
            const angle = (i * Math.PI / 8);
            const r = (i % 2 === 0) ? sr : sir;
            const px = sx + Math.cos(angle) * r, py = cy + Math.sin(angle) * r;
            if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py); else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
        }
        ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
        return off;
    }

    // 8. KKTC Bayrağı
    function praytDrawKktcArt(off, w, h) {
        const ctx = off.getContext('2d');
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

        const barH = h * 0.10, barPad = h * 0.12;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
        ctx.fillRect(0, barPad, w, barH);
        ctx.fillRect(0, h - barPad - barH, w, barH);

        const cy = h * 0.5, cx = w * 0.44, r1 = h * 0.20;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r1, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx + r1 * 0.28, cy, r1 * 0.80, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();

        const sx = cx + r1 + r1 * 0.28, sr = r1 * 0.50, sir = sr * 0.382;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            const angle = Math.PI + (i * Math.PI / 5);
            const r = (i % 2 === 0) ? sr : sir;
            const px = sx + Math.cos(angle) * r, py = cy + Math.sin(angle) * r;
            if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py); else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
        }
        ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
        return off;
    }

    // 9. Retro 8-Bit Pixel Kalp
    function praytDrawHeartArt(off, w, h) {
        const ctx = off.getContext('2d');
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#ff1744';
        const cx = w * 0.5, cy = h * 0.45;
        const hr = Math.min(w, h) * 0.32;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(cx, cy + hr);
