        ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx - hr * 1.5, cy, cx - hr * 1.5, cy - hr * 1.2, cx, cy - hr * 0.5);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx + hr * 1.5, cy - hr * 1.2, cx + hr * 1.5, cy, cx, cy + hr);
        ctx.fill();
        return off;
    }

    // 10. 8 Köşeli Selçuklu Yıldızı
    function praytDrawStarArt(off, w, h) {
        const ctx = off.getContext('2d');
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#0d47a1'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
        const cx = w * 0.5, cy = h * 0.5, r = Math.min(w, h) * 0.42, ir = r * 0.54;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd54f';
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
            const angle = (i * Math.PI / 8);
            const cr = (i % 2 === 0) ? r : ir;
            const px = cx + Math.cos(angle) * cr, py = cy + Math.sin(angle) * cr;
            if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py); else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
        }
        ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
        return off;
    }

    // 11. Özel İnternet Resmi (Filtreler, Chroma Key & Dither)
    function praytDrawCustomImageArt(off, w, h) {
        const ctx = off.getContext('2d');
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
        const img = state.prayt && state.prayt.customArtImage;
        if (!img) {
            return praytDrawOfficialTurkishFlag(off, w, h);
        }

        // 1. Canlı Filtreler (Brightness, Contrast, Saturation)
        const br = 100 + ((state.prayt && state.prayt.imgBrightness) || 0);
        const ct = 100 + ((state.prayt && state.prayt.imgContrast) || 0);
        const sat = (state.prayt && state.prayt.imgSaturation !== undefined) ? state.prayt.imgSaturation : 100;
        ctx.filter = `brightness(${br}%) contrast(${ct}%) saturate(${sat}%)`;
        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, w, h);
        ctx.filter = 'none';

        // 2. Chroma Key (Arka Plan Silme) & Dithering
        const imgData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h);
        const d = imgData.data;
        const chroma = (state.prayt && state.prayt.chromaKey) || 'none';

        if (chroma === 'white') {
            for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i += 4) {
                if (d[i] > 235 && d[i+1] > 235 && d[i+2] > 235) d[i+3] = 0;
            }
        } else if (chroma === 'black') {
            for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i += 4) {
                if (d[i] < 20 && d[i+1] < 20 && d[i+2] < 20) d[i+3] = 0;
            }
        }

        const pal = getPaletteColors();
        const mode = (state.prayt && state.prayt.ditherMode) || 'none';
        if (pal && pal.length > 0 && mode !== 'none') {
            let processed;
            if (mode === 'atkinson') processed = praytAtkinson(imgData, w, h, pal);
            else if (mode === 'bayer') processed = praytBayer4x4(imgData, w, h, pal);
            else processed = praytFloydSteinberg(imgData, w, h, pal);
            ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(processed.data, w, h), 0, 0);
        } else {
            ctx.putImageData(imgData, 0, 0);
        }

        return off;
    }

    // Şablon Yönlendirici (Artwork Router)
    function praytDrawTurkishFlagArtwork(canvasOrCtx, w, h) {
        let off;
        if (canvasOrCtx && canvasOrCtx.getContext) {
            off = canvasOrCtx;
            if (off.width !== w) off.width = w;
            if (off.height !== h) off.height = h;
        } else {
            off = document.createElement('canvas');
            off.width = w;
            off.height = h;
        }

        const template = (state.prayt && state.prayt.currentArtTemplate) ? state.prayt.currentArtTemplate : 'tr_history';

        if (template === 'tr_official') {
            return praytDrawOfficialTurkishFlag(off, w, h);
        } else if (template === 'turkey_map') {
            return praytDrawTurkeyMapFlag(off, w, h);
        } else if (template === 'custom_url') {
            return praytDrawCustomImageArt(off, w, h);
        }

        // 'tr_history' veya varsayılan: İhtişamlı Tarihî Türk Sancağı
        return praytDrawGrandTurkishHistoryFlag(off, w, h);
    }

    function praytLoadImageFromUrl(url) {
        if (!url) return;
        setStatus('🌐 RESİM YÜKLENİYOR...');
        praytShowToast('🌐 İnternetten resim indiriliyor...', 'info');
        const img = new Image();
        img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
        img.onload = () => {
            state.prayt.customArtImage = img;
            state.prayt.currentArtTemplate = 'custom_url';
            const sel = document.getElementById('w3x-sanat-template-select');
            if (sel) sel.value = 'custom_url';
            if (state.savedArea) {
                praytRenderFlagPreview(state.savedArea);
            }
            setStatus(`✅ RESİM YÜKLENDİ (${img.width}×${img.height} px)`);
            praytShowToast(`✅ Resim hazır (${img.width}×${img.height} px)`, 'info');
            praytAudio.playStart();
        };
        img.onerror = () => {
            setStatus('❌ RESİM YÜKLENEMEDİ (CORS veya Hatalı Link)');
            praytShowToast('❌ Resim yüklenemedi (CORS veya link hatalı)', 'danger');
            praytAudio.playAlert();
        };
        img.src = url;
    }

    // =========================================================
    // RESMÎ TÜRK BAYRAĞI & ŞABLON BAŞLATICILARI (GENUINE FUNCTIONS)
    // =========================================================
    function praytGenerateTurkishFlag() {
        const template = (state.prayt && state.prayt.currentArtTemplate) ? state.prayt.currentArtTemplate : 'tr_history';
        if (template === 'tr_official') {
            praytGenerateNormalTrFlag();
        } else {
            praytGenerateTurkishHistoryFlag();
        }
    }

    function praytGenerateTurkishHistoryFlag() {
        if (state.prayt) state.prayt.currentArtTemplate = 'tr_history';
        praytAudio.playStart();
        praytRunOffscreenCanvasTemplate('tr_history', '🏛️ 🇹🇷 TARİHÎ TÜRK SANCAĞI', 'tr_history', (ctx, w, h) => {
            praytDrawGrandTurkishHistoryFlag(ctx.canvas || ctx, w, h);
        });
    }

    function praytGenerateNormalTrFlag() {
        if (state.prayt) state.prayt.currentArtTemplate = 'tr_official';
        praytAudio.playStart();
        praytRunOffscreenCanvasTemplate('tr_official', '🇹🇷 SADE RESMÎ TÜRK BAYRAĞI', 'tr_official', (ctx, w, h) => {
            praytDrawOfficialTurkishFlag(ctx.canvas || ctx, w, h);
        });
    }

    function praytGenerateTurkeyMap() {
        if (state.prayt) state.prayt.currentArtTemplate = 'turkey_map';
        praytAudio.playStart();
        praytRunOffscreenCanvasTemplate('turkey_map', '🗺️ DOĞAL TÜRKİYE HARİTASI', 'turkey_map', (ctx, w, h) => {
            praytDrawTurkeyMapFlag(ctx.canvas || ctx, w, h);
        });
    }

    function praytGenerateLgbtFlag() {
        if (!state.savedArea) {
            state.prayt.pendingFlag = 'lgbt';
            setStatus('🏳️‍🌈 ALAN SEÇİN...');
            beginAreaSelection();
            const help = $('#w3x-select-help');
            if (help) {
                help.textContent = '🏳️‍🌈 LGBT Bayrağı çizilecek alanı haritada sürükleyerek seçin!';
                help.classList.add('active');
            }
            return;
        }
        if (!state.wsReady) {
            setStatus('NO WS');
            return;
        }
        const palette = getPaletteColors();
        if (!palette || palette.length === 0) {
            setStatus('NO PALETTE');
            return;
        }

        const area = state.savedArea;
        const w = area.x2 - area.x1 + 1;
        const h = area.y2 - area.y1 + 1;
        if (w < 2 || h < 4) {
            setStatus('AREA TOO SMALL');
            return;
        }

        // Yeni bayrak çizimi başlarken eski koruma kilitlerini sıfırla ki kendi çizimine saldırmasın!
        state.bot.protect = false;
        state.bot.humanProtect = false;
        if (state.prayt) {
            state.prayt.protectImage = null;
            state.prayt.protectOrigin = null;
            state.prayt.realtimeProtect = false;
            state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect = false;
            state.prayt.godDefense = false;
        }
        if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') {
            praytGodDefense.active = false;
            praytGodDefense.syncButtons();
            praytGodDefense.updateUI();
        }

        takeSnapshot(null, true);
        const handColor = getSelectedColorId();
        if (handColor !== null) state.prayt.currentHandColor = handColor;

        const lgbtColors = [
            [255, 0, 24],     // Red
            [255, 165, 44],   // Orange
            [255, 255, 65],   // Yellow
            [0, 128, 24],     // Green
            [0, 0, 249],      // Blue
            [134, 0, 125]     // Purple
        ];
        const lgbtColorIds = lgbtColors.map(([r, g, b]) => nearestPaletteId(r, g, b, palette));

        const targetData = new Int16Array(w * h);
        let queue = [];
        let seaSkipped = 0;

        for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
            const stripeIdx = Math.min(5, Math.floor(dy * 6 / h));
            const colorId = lgbtColorIds[stripeIdx];
            const ay = area.y1 + dy;

            for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
                const ax = area.x1 + dx;
                if (state.prayt.seaSkip && isCanvasSeaPixel(ax, ay)) {
                    targetData[dy * w + dx] = -1;
                    seaSkipped++;
                    continue;
                }
                targetData[dy * w + dx] = colorId;
                if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(ax, ay, colorId)) {
                    queue.push({ x: ax, y: ay, colorId });
                }
            }
        }

        if (queue.length === 0 && seaSkipped === w * h) {
            for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
                const stripeIdx = Math.min(5, Math.floor(dy * 6 / h));
                const colorId = lgbtColorIds[stripeIdx];
                const ay = area.y1 + dy;
                for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
                    const ax = area.x1 + dx;
                    targetData[dy * w + dx] = colorId;
                    if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(ax, ay, colorId)) {
                        queue.push({ x: ax, y: ay, colorId });
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        state.prayt.areaTargetMap = {
            x1: area.x1,
            y1: area.y1,
            x2: area.x2,
            y2: area.y2,
            w,
            h,
            data: targetData,
            type: 'lgbt_flag'
        };
        updatePraytProtectInfo();

        if (queue.length === 0) {
            state.bot.running = false;
            state.bot.protect = true;
            state.bot.humanProtect = false;
            if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') {
                praytGodDefense.active = true;
                state.prayt.godDefense = true;
                praytGodDefense.syncButtons();
                praytGodDefense.updateUI();
            }
            praytMxoEngine.markAreaAsFullyDrawn();
            if (typeof _globalSyncAreaProtectUI === 'function') _globalSyncAreaProtectUI();
            updatePraytProtectInfo();
            praytMxoEngine.start();
            setStatus('🏳️‍🌈 LGBT MXO 0MS KORUMA AKTİF');
            return;
        }

        queue = praytSortQueue(queue);

        state.bot.running = true;
        state.bot.paused = false;
        state.bot.placed = 0;
        state.bot.skipped = 0;
        state.bot.total = queue.length;
        state.prayt.statsStartTime = Date.now();

        praytMxoEngine.stop();
        for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
            for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
                const expCid = targetData[dy * w + dx];
                if (expCid >= 0) {
                    const ax = area.x1 + dx;
                    const ay = area.y1 + dy;
                    if (isPixelAlreadyColor(ax, ay, expCid)) {
                        praytMxoEngine.drawnPixels.set(`${ax},${ay}`, expCid);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        for (const p of queue) {
            praytMxoEngine.putPixel(p.x, p.y, p.colorId, {
                isSlowCleanup: !!p.isSlowCleanup,
                strokePause: !!p.strokePause,
                colorSwitchPause: !!p.colorSwitchPause
            });
        }
        praytMxoEngine.start();
        updateBotUI();
        setStatus(`🏳️‍🌈 LGBT BAYRAK (${queue.length} px)`);
    }

    function praytRunOffscreenCanvasTemplate(pendingKey, label, typeName, drawCallback) {
        if (!state.savedArea) {
            state.prayt.pendingFlag = pendingKey;
            setStatus(`${label} ALAN SEÇİN...`);
            beginAreaSelection();
            const help = $('#w3x-select-help');
            if (help) {
                help.textContent = `${label} çizilecek alanı haritada sürükleyerek seçin!`;
                help.classList.add('active');
            }
            return;
        }
        if (!state.wsReady) {
            setStatus('NO WS');
            return;
        }
        const palette = getPaletteColors();
        if (!palette || palette.length === 0) {
            setStatus('NO PALETTE');
            return;
        }

        const area = state.savedArea;
        const w = area.x2 - area.x1 + 1;
        const h = area.y2 - area.y1 + 1;
        if (w < 3 || h < 3) {
            setStatus('AREA TOO SMALL');
            return;
        }

        // Yeni bayrak çizimi başlarken eski koruma kilitlerini sıfırla ki kendi çizimine saldırmasın!
        state.bot.protect = false;
        state.bot.humanProtect = false;
        if (state.prayt) {
            state.prayt.protectImage = null;
            state.prayt.protectOrigin = null;
            state.prayt.realtimeProtect = false;
            state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect = false;
            state.prayt.godDefense = false;
        }
        if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') {
            praytGodDefense.active = false;
            praytGodDefense.syncButtons();
            praytGodDefense.updateUI();
        }

        takeSnapshot(null, true);

        const handColor = getSelectedColorId();
        if (handColor !== null) state.prayt.currentHandColor = handColor;

        const off = document.createElement('canvas');
        off.width = w;
        off.height = h;
        const ctx = off.getContext('2d');

        drawCallback(ctx, w, h, palette);

        const imgData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h).data;
        const targetData = new Int16Array(w * h);
        let queue = [];
        let seaSkipped = 0;

        for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
            for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
                const ax = area.x1 + dx;
                const ay = area.y1 + dy;
                const idx = (dy * w + dx) * 4;
                const a = imgData[idx + 3];
                if (a < 128) {
                    targetData[dy * w + dx] = -1;
                    continue;
                }
                const colorId = nearestPaletteId(imgData[idx], imgData[idx + 1], imgData[idx + 2], palette);
                if (colorId === null || colorId === undefined) {
                    targetData[dy * w + dx] = -1;
                    continue;
                }

                if (state.prayt.seaSkip && isCanvasSeaPixel(ax, ay)) {
                    targetData[dy * w + dx] = -1;
                    seaSkipped++;
                    continue;
                }

                targetData[dy * w + dx] = colorId;
                if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(ax, ay, colorId)) {
                    queue.push({ x: ax, y: ay, colorId, forcePlace: true });
                }
            }
        }

        const redColorId = nearestPaletteId(227, 10, 23, palette);
        const whiteColorId = nearestPaletteId(255, 255, 255, palette);

        if ((typeName === 'tr_flag' || typeName === 'tr_official') && redColorId !== null && whiteColorId !== null) {
            // SADE RESMÎ TÜRK BAYRAĞI (Önce %100 düz kırmızı zemin, sonra üstüne beyaz hilal & yıldız)
            // Denizler kesinlikle boyanmaz!
            const redLayer = [];
            const whiteLayer = [];

            for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
                for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
                    const ax = area.x1 + dx;
                    const ay = area.y1 + dy;
                    if (state.prayt.seaSkip && isCanvasSeaPixel(ax, ay)) continue;

                    const expectedCId = targetData[dy * w + dx];
                    if (expectedCId < 0) continue;

                    // Katman 0: Yalnızca kırmızı olması gereken zemin pikselleri
                    if (expectedCId === redColorId) {
                        if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(ax, ay, redColorId)) {
                            redLayer.push({ x: ax, y: ay, colorId: redColorId, layer: 0, isLayered: true, forcePlace: true });
                        }
                    } else if (expectedCId === whiteColorId) {
                        // Katman 1: Beyaz Hilal & Yıldız pikselleri
                        if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(ax, ay, whiteColorId)) {
                            whiteLayer.push({ x: ax, y: ay, colorId: whiteColorId, layer: 1, isLayered: true, forcePlace: true });
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            if (whiteLayer.length > 0) {
                whiteLayer[0] = { ...whiteLayer[0], colorSwitchPause: true };
            }

            queue = [...redLayer, ...whiteLayer];
        } else if (typeName === 'tr_history' && redColorId !== null) {
            // İHTİŞAMLI TARİHÎ TÜRK SANCAĞI (Katman 0: Kırmızı Zemin -> Katman 1: Altın Tezhip, Selçuklu Yıldızları, Kayı IYI, Göktürkçe ve Ay-Yıldız)
            // Denizler kesinlikle boyanmaz!
            const redLayer = [];
            const fgByColor = new Map();

            for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
                for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
                    const ax = area.x1 + dx;
                    const ay = area.y1 + dy;
                    if (state.prayt.seaSkip && isCanvasSeaPixel(ax, ay)) continue;

                    const expectedCId = targetData[dy * w + dx];
                    if (expectedCId < 0) continue;

                    // Katman 0: Asil kırmızı alt zemin pikselleri
                    if (expectedCId === redColorId) {
                        if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(ax, ay, redColorId)) {
                            redLayer.push({ x: ax, y: ay, colorId: redColorId, layer: 0, isLayered: true, forcePlace: true });
                        }
                    } else {
                        // Katman 1: Kırmızı harici tüm semboller (Altın Kayı, Selçuklu Yıldızları, Beyaz Ay-Yıldız, Çift Başlı Kartal)
                        if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(ax, ay, expectedCId)) {
                            if (!fgByColor.has(expectedCId)) fgByColor.set(expectedCId, []);
                            fgByColor.get(expectedCId).push({ x: ax, y: ay, colorId: expectedCId, layer: 1, isLayered: true, forcePlace: true });
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Foreground katmanlarını renklere göre sıralı bağla: Önce tüm Altın Sarısı, sonra Saf Beyaz Ay-Yıldız
            const fgLayer = [];
            const goldColorId = nearestPaletteId(255, 215, 0, palette);
            if (goldColorId !== null && fgByColor.has(goldColorId)) {
                const gPx = fgByColor.get(goldColorId);
                if (gPx.length > 0) {
                    gPx[0] = { ...gPx[0], colorSwitchPause: true };
                    fgLayer.push(...gPx);
                }
                fgByColor.delete(goldColorId);
            }
            if (whiteColorId !== null && fgByColor.has(whiteColorId)) {
                const wPx = fgByColor.get(whiteColorId);
                if (wPx.length > 0) {
                    wPx[0] = { ...wPx[0], colorSwitchPause: true };
                    fgLayer.push(...wPx);
                }
                fgByColor.delete(whiteColorId);
            }
            for (const [cId, pixels] of fgByColor.entries()) {
                if (pixels.length > 0) {
                    pixels[0] = { ...pixels[0], colorSwitchPause: true };
                    fgLayer.push(...pixels);
                }
            }

            queue = [...redLayer, ...fgLayer];
        }

        state.prayt.areaTargetMap = {
            x1: area.x1,
            y1: area.y1,
            x2: area.x2,
            y2: area.y2,
            w,
            h,
            data: targetData,
            type: typeName
        };
        updatePraytProtectInfo();

        if (queue.length === 0) {
            if (state.prayt && state.prayt.noProtectWhileDrawing) {
                setStatus(`${label} ALAN ZATEN TAM`);
                updateBotUI(true);
                return;
            }
            state.bot.running = false;
            state.bot.protect = true;
            state.bot.humanProtect = false;
            if (typeof praytGodDefense !== 'undefined') {
                praytGodDefense.active = true;
                state.prayt.godDefense = true;
                praytGodDefense.syncButtons();
                praytGodDefense.updateUI();
            }
            praytMxoEngine.markAreaAsFullyDrawn();
            if (typeof _globalSyncAreaProtectUI === 'function') _globalSyncAreaProtectUI();
            updatePraytProtectInfo();
            praytMxoEngine.start();
            setStatus(`${label} MXO 0MS KORUMA AKTİF`);
            return;
        }

        queue = praytSortQueue(queue);

        state.bot.running = true;
        state.bot.paused = false;
        state.bot.placed = 0;
        state.bot.skipped = 0;
        state.bot.total = queue.length;
        state.prayt.statsStartTime = Date.now();

        praytMxoEngine.stop();
        for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
            for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
                const expCid = targetData[dy * w + dx];
                if (expCid >= 0) {
                    const ax = area.x1 + dx;
                    const ay = area.y1 + dy;
                    if (isPixelAlreadyColor(ax, ay, expCid)) {
                        praytMxoEngine.drawnPixels.set(`${ax},${ay}`, expCid);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        for (const p of queue) {
            praytMxoEngine.putPixel(p.x, p.y, p.colorId, {
                forcePlace: !!p.forcePlace,
                isLayered: !!p.isLayered,
                layer: p.layer,
                isSlowCleanup: !!p.isSlowCleanup,
                strokePause: !!p.strokePause,
                colorSwitchPause: !!p.colorSwitchPause
            });
        }
        praytMxoEngine.start();

        updateBotUI();
        setStatus(`${label} (${queue.length} px)`);
    }

    // 🇦🇿 Azerbaycan Bayrağı (Mavi - Kırmızı - Yeşil + Beyaz Hilal ve 8 Köşeli Yıldız)
    function praytGenerateAzerbaijanFlag() {
        praytRunOffscreenCanvasTemplate('az', '🇦🇿 AZ BAYRAK', 'az_flag', (ctx, w, h) => {
            const h3 = h / 3;
            ctx.fillStyle = '#00B5E2';
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, Math.ceil(h3));
            ctx.fillStyle = '#EF3340';
            ctx.fillRect(0, Math.floor(h3), w, Math.ceil(h3));
            ctx.fillStyle = '#509E2F';
            ctx.fillRect(0, Math.floor(h3 * 2), w, h - Math.floor(h3 * 2));

            const cy = h / 2;
            const cx1 = w * 0.465;
            const r1 = Math.min(w, h) * 0.145;
            ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(cx1, cy, r1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();

            const cx2 = cx1 + r1 * 0.24;
            const r2 = r1 * 0.82;
            ctx.fillStyle = '#EF3340';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(cx2, cy, r2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();

            // 8-Pointed White Star
            const starX = cx2 + r2 + r1 * 0.32;
            const starR = r1 * 0.56;
            const starInnerR = starR * 0.42;
            ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
            ctx.beginPath();
            for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
                const angle = -Math.PI / 2 + (i * Math.PI / 8);
                const r = (i % 2 === 0) ? starR : starInnerR;
                const sx = starX + Math.cos(angle) * r;
                const sy = cy + Math.sin(angle) * r;
                if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(sx, sy);
                else ctx.lineTo(sx, sy);
            }
            ctx.closePath();
            ctx.fill();
        });
    }

    // 🇹🇷 KKTC Bayrağı (Beyaz Zemin + Üst/Alt Kırmızı Şerit + Kırmızı Hilal ve Yıldız)
    function praytGenerateKktcFlag() {
        praytRunOffscreenCanvasTemplate('kktc', '🇹🇷 KKTC BAYRAK', 'kktc_flag', (ctx, w, h) => {
            ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

            const stripeH = Math.max(1, Math.round(h * 0.10));
            const topY = Math.max(1, Math.round(h * 0.10));
            const botY = Math.min(h - stripeH - 1, Math.round(h * 0.80));
            ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
            ctx.fillRect(0, topY, w, stripeH);
            ctx.fillRect(0, botY, w, stripeH);

            const cy = h / 2;
            const cx1 = w * 0.42;
            const r1 = Math.min(w, h) * 0.22;
            ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(cx1, cy, r1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();

            const cx2 = cx1 + r1 * 0.26;
            const r2 = r1 * 0.80;
            ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(cx2, cy, r2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();

            const starX = cx2 + r2 + r1 * 0.25;
            const starR = r1 * 0.48;
            const starInnerR = starR * 0.382;
            ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17';
            ctx.beginPath();
            for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
                const angle = Math.PI + (i * Math.PI / 5);
                const r = (i % 2 === 0) ? starR : starInnerR;
                const sx = starX + Math.cos(angle) * r;
                const sy = cy + Math.sin(angle) * r;
                if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(sx, sy);
                else ctx.lineTo(sx, sy);
            }
            ctx.closePath();
            ctx.fill();
        });
    }

    // 🇨🇦 Kanada Bayrağı (11 Köşeli Resmî Akçaağaç Yaprağı)
    function praytGenerateCanadaFlag() {
        praytRunOffscreenCanvasTemplate('canada', '🇨🇦 KANADA BAYRAK', 'canada_flag', (ctx, w, h) => {
            praytDrawCanadaFlag(ctx.canvas || ctx, w, h);
        });
    }

    // ❤️ Piksel Kalp Sembolü (Kırmızı Kalp + Parlama + Koyu Kontur)
    function praytGenerateHeartSymbol() {
        praytRunOffscreenCanvasTemplate('heart', '❤️ PİKSEL KALP', 'heart_symbol', (ctx, w, h) => {
            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
            for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
                for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
                    const nx = ((dx + 0.5) / w) * 2.4 - 1.2;
                    const ny = 1.15 - ((dy + 0.5) / h) * 2.4;
                    const eq = Math.pow(nx * nx + ny * ny - 1, 3) - nx * nx * Math.pow(ny, 3);
                    if (eq <= 0) {
                        if (eq > -0.18) {
                            ctx.fillStyle = '#220008'; // Crisp dark outline
                        } else if (nx < -0.25 && ny > 0.28 && (nx + 0.48) * (nx + 0.48) + (ny - 0.48) * (ny - 0.48) < 0.07) {
                            ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF'; // Glossy white heart shine
                        } else {
                            ctx.fillStyle = '#E30A17'; // Bright crimson heart body
                        }
                        ctx.fillRect(dx, dy, 1, 1);
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    // ⭐ Ay-Yıldız / Altın Arma
    function praytGenerateStarEmblem() {
        praytRunOffscreenCanvasTemplate('star', '⭐ AY-YILDIZ ARMA', 'star_emblem', (ctx, w, h) => {
            ctx.fillStyle = '#111116';
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

            // Outer golden border
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#FFD700';
            ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(Math.min(w, h) * 0.06));
            ctx.strokeRect(0.5, 0.5, w - 1, h - 1);

            const cy = h / 2;
            const cx1 = w * 0.41;
            const r1 = Math.min(w, h) * 0.28;
            ctx.fillStyle = '#FFD700';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(cx1, cy, r1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();

            const cx2 = cx1 + r1 * 0.28;
            const r2 = r1 * 0.80;
            ctx.fillStyle = '#111116';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(cx2, cy, r2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();

            const starX = cx2 + r2 + r1 * 0.24;
            const starR = r1 * 0.52;
            const starInnerR = starR * 0.382;
            ctx.fillStyle = '#FFD700';
            ctx.beginPath();
            for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
                const angle = Math.PI + (i * Math.PI / 5);
                const r = (i % 2 === 0) ? starR : starInnerR;
                const sx = starX + Math.cos(angle) * r;
                const sy = cy + Math.sin(angle) * r;
                if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(sx, sy);
                else ctx.lineTo(sx, sy);
            }
            ctx.closePath();
            ctx.fill();
        });
    }

    // =========================================================
    // ⏰ CANLI DİJİTAL SAAT BOTU (AUTO-UPDATING HH:MM:SS PIXEL CLOCK)
    // =========================================================

    const CLOCK_FONT_3X5 = {
        '0': ['111', '101', '101', '101', '111'],
        '1': ['010', '110', '010', '010', '111'],
        '2': ['111', '001', '111', '100', '111'],
        '3': ['111', '001', '111', '001', '111'],
        '4': ['101', '101', '111', '001', '001'],
        '5': ['111', '100', '111', '001', '111'],
        '6': ['111', '100', '111', '101', '111'],
        '7': ['111', '001', '001', '010', '010'],
        '8': ['111', '101', '111', '101', '111'],
        '9': ['111', '101', '111', '001', '111'],
        ':': ['0', '1', '0', '1', '0']
    };

    const CLOCK_FONT_5X7 = {
        '0': ['01110', '10001', '10001', '10001', '10001', '10001', '01110'],
        '1': ['00100', '01100', '00100', '00100', '00100', '00100', '01110'],
        '2': ['01110', '10001', '00001', '01110', '10000', '10000', '11111'],
        '3': ['11110', '00001', '00001', '01110', '00001', '00001', '11110'],
        '4': ['10010', '10010', '10010', '11111', '00010', '00010', '00010'],
        '5': ['11111', '10000', '10000', '11110', '00001', '00001', '11110'],
        '6': ['01110', '10000', '10000', '11110', '10001', '10001', '01110'],
        '7': ['11111', '00001', '00010', '00100', '01000', '01000', '01000'],
        '8': ['01110', '10001', '10001', '01110', '10001', '10001', '01110'],
        '9': ['01110', '10001', '10001', '01111', '00001', '00001', '01110'],
        ':': ['0', '0', '1', '0', '1', '0', '0']
    };

    function praytSyncClockUI() {
        const active = !!(state.prayt && state.prayt.liveClockActive);
        const tStr = state.prayt.clockLastTimeStr || 'ON';
        const btnTop = $('#w3x-top-clock');
        const btnBot = $('#w3x-bot-clock-toggle');
        if (btnTop) {
            btnTop.textContent = active ? `⏰ ${tStr}` : '⏰ CANLI SAAT';
            btnTop.classList.toggle('protecting', active);
            btnTop.style.background = active ? 'rgba(0,230,118,0.35)' : 'rgba(0,230,118,0.18)';
            btnTop.style.boxShadow = active ? '0 0 10px rgba(0,230,118,0.6)' : 'none';
        }
        if (btnBot) {
            btnBot.textContent = active ? `⏰ CANLI SAATİ DURDUR (${tStr})` : '⏰ CANLI SAATİ BAŞLAT';
            btnBot.classList.toggle('protecting', active);
            btnBot.style.background = active ? 'rgba(255, 23, 68, 0.22)' : 'rgba(0, 230, 118, 0.22)';
            btnBot.style.borderColor = active ? '#ff1744' : '#00e676';
            btnBot.style.color = active ? '#ff5252' : '#00e676';
        }
    }

    function praytToggleLiveClock() {
        if (state.prayt.liveClockActive) {
            praytStopLiveClock(true);
        } else {
            praytStartLiveClock();
        }
    }

    function praytStopLiveClock(updateStatus = true) {
        state.prayt.liveClockActive = false;
        state.prayt.pendingClock = false;
        if (state.prayt.clockTimer) {
            clearInterval(state.prayt.clockTimer);
            state.prayt.clockTimer = null;
        }
        state.prayt.clockLastTimeStr = '';
        state.prayt.clockActiveMask = new Map();
        state.prayt.clockPrevData = null;
        state.bot.running = false;
        if (praytMxoEngine.clearClockTasks) praytMxoEngine.clearClockTasks();
        praytMxoEngine.stop();
        praytSyncClockUI();
        if (updateStatus) {
            setStatus('⏰ CANLI SAAT DURDURULDU');
        }
    }

    function praytStartLiveClock() {
        if (!state.savedArea) {
            state.prayt.pendingClock = true;
            state.prayt.pendingFlag = null;
            state.prayt.pendingHumanProtect = false;
            state.prayt.pendingMxoProtect = false;
            setStatus('⏰ SAAT İÇİN HARİTADA ALAN SEÇİN (MİN 27x7)...');
            beginAreaSelection();
            const help = $('#w3x-select-help');
            if (help) {
                help.textContent = '⏰ Canlı Saatin (SS:DD:SN) yazılacağı alanı haritada sürükleyerek seçin (En az 27×7 piksel)!';
                help.classList.add('active');
            }
            return;
        }
        if (!state.wsReady) {
            setStatus('NO WS');
            return;
        }
        const palette = getPaletteColors();
        if (!palette || palette.length === 0) {
            setStatus('NO PALETTE');
            return;
        }

        const area = state.savedArea;
        const w = area.x2 - area.x1 + 1;
        const h = area.y2 - area.y1 + 1;
        if (w < 15 || h < 5) {
            setStatus('ALAN ÇOK KÜÇÜK (MİN 27x7) - ALAN SEÇİN...');
            state.prayt.pendingClock = true;
            state.prayt.pendingFlag = null;
            state.prayt.pendingHumanProtect = false;
            state.prayt.pendingMxoProtect = false;
            beginAreaSelection();
            const help = $('#w3x-select-help');
            if (help) {
                help.textContent = '⏰ Canlı Saat için haritada en az 27×7 piksel bir alan sürükleyin!';
                help.classList.add('active');
            }
            return;
        }

        state.bot.protect = false;
        state.bot.humanProtect = false;
        state.prayt.realtimeProtect = false;
        state.prayt.realtimeHumanProtect = false;
        if (typeof _globalSyncAreaProtectUI === 'function') _globalSyncAreaProtectUI();

        // Resolve Foreground (Digit) and Background Colors
        const blackId = nearestPaletteId(10, 10, 14, palette);
        const whiteId = nearestPaletteId(255, 255, 255, palette);
        const greenId = nearestPaletteId(0, 230, 118, palette);

        if (state.prayt.clockBgColorId === null || state.prayt.clockBgColorId === undefined) {
            state.prayt.clockBgColorId = blackId;
        }
        if (state.prayt.clockFgColorId === null || state.prayt.clockFgColorId === undefined) {
            const selected = getSelectedColorId();
            if (selected !== null && selected !== state.prayt.clockBgColorId) {
                state.prayt.clockFgColorId = selected;
            } else {
                state.prayt.clockFgColorId = (greenId !== null && greenId !== state.prayt.clockBgColorId) ? greenId : whiteId;
            }
        }
        if (state.prayt.clockFgColorId === state.prayt.clockBgColorId) {
            state.prayt.clockFgColorId = (state.prayt.clockBgColorId === whiteId) ? blackId : whiteId;
        }

        state.prayt.liveClockActive = true;
        state.prayt.clockLastTimeStr = '';
        state.prayt.clockActiveMask = new Map();
        state.prayt.clockPrevData = null;

        if (state.prayt.clockTimer) clearInterval(state.prayt.clockTimer);

        // Render first full clock frame immediately
        praytRenderLiveClockTick(true);

        // Check clock every 100ms for instant second changes
        state.prayt.clockTimer = setInterval(() => {
            praytRenderLiveClockTick(false);
        }, 100);
    }

    function praytRenderLiveClockTick(forceFull = false) {
        if (!state.prayt.liveClockActive || !state.savedArea || !state.wsReady) return;

        const now = new Date();
        const hh = String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
        const mm = String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
        const ss = String(now.getSeconds()).padStart(2, '0');

        const area = state.savedArea;
        const w = area.x2 - area.x1 + 1;
        const h = area.y2 - area.y1 + 1;

        // Choose HH:MM:SS if width >= 27, otherwise compact HH:MM
        const fullTimeStr = (w >= 27) ? `${hh}:${mm}:${ss}` : `${hh}:${mm}`;
        if (!forceFull && fullTimeStr === state.prayt.clockLastTimeStr) {
            return;
        }
        state.prayt.clockLastTimeStr = fullTimeStr;
        praytSyncClockUI();

        // Choose 5x7 font if area is at least 41x9, otherwise 3x5 font
        const use5x7 = (w >= 41 && h >= 9 && fullTimeStr.length === 8);
        const font = use5x7 ? CLOCK_FONT_5X7 : CLOCK_FONT_3X5;
        const fontH = use5x7 ? 7 : 5;

        // Precompute glyph widths and total base width
        let baseW = 0;
        const charWidths = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < fullTimeStr.length; i++) {
            const ch = fullTimeStr[i];
            const glyph = font[ch] || font['0'];
            const gw = glyph[0].length;
            charWidths.push(gw);
            baseW += gw;
            if (i < fullTimeStr.length - 1) baseW += 1;
        }

        // Compute scaling (capped at 3 for instant <0.2s live response)
        const availW = Math.max(baseW, w - 2);
        const availH = Math.max(fontH, h - 2);
        const scaleX = Math.max(1, Math.floor(availW / baseW));
        const scaleY = Math.max(1, Math.floor(availH / fontH));
        const scale = Math.max(1, Math.min(scaleX, scaleY, 3));
        const sx = scale;
        const sy = scale;

        const totalDrawW = baseW * sx;
        const totalDrawH = fontH * sy;
        const offsetX = Math.max(0, Math.floor((w - totalDrawW) / 2));
        const offsetY = Math.max(0, Math.floor((h - totalDrawH) / 2));

        const fgId = state.prayt.clockFgColorId;
        const bgId = state.prayt.clockBgColorId;

        // Helper to compute { fg: Map<string, {x,y}>, bg: Map<string, {x,y}> } for a character at position charIndex
        function computeCharPixelMap(charIndex, ch) {
            const glyph = font[ch] || font['0'];
            const gw = glyph[0].length;
            let curBaseX = 0;
            for (let k = 0; k < charIndex; k++) {
                curBaseX += charWidths[k] + 1;
            }
            const fg = new Map();
            const bg = new Map();
            for (let gy = 0; gy < fontH; gy++) {
                const rowStr = glyph[gy] || '';
                for (let gx = 0; gx < gw; gx++) {
                    const isFg = (rowStr[gx] === '1');
                    for (let py = 0; py < sy; py++) {
                        for (let px = 0; px < sx; px++) {
                            const relX = offsetX + (curBaseX + gx) * sx + px;
                            const relY = offsetY + gy * sy + py;
                            if (relX >= 0 && relX < w && relY >= 0 && relY < h) {
                                const ax = area.x1 + relX;
                                const ay = area.y1 + relY;
                                if (!(state.prayt.seaSkip && isCanvasSeaPixel(ax, ay))) {
                                    const key = `${ax},${ay}`;
                                    if (isFg) fg.set(key, { x: ax, y: ay });
                                    else bg.set(key, { x: ax, y: ay });
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            return { ch, fg, bg };
        }

        const prevCharStates = state.prayt.clockCharStates;
        const needsFullInit = forceFull || !prevCharStates || prevCharStates.length !== fullTimeStr.length;

        if (needsFullInit) {
            state.prayt.clockCharStates = [];
            state.bot.running = true;
            praytMxoEngine.tasks = [];
            praytMxoEngine.head = 0;
            praytMxoEngine.tokens = 8.0;
            let totalQueued = 0;

            for (let i = 0; i < fullTimeStr.length; i++) {
                const ch = fullTimeStr[i];
                const stateObj = computeCharPixelMap(i, ch);
                state.prayt.clockCharStates.push(stateObj);

                // 1. RAKAM (FOREGROUND) PİKSELLERİ ÖNCELİKLİ: Rakam vuruşlarını anında ve ilk önce bas!
                for (const pt of stateObj.fg.values()) {
                    if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(pt.x, pt.y, fgId)) {
                        praytMxoEngine.putPixel(pt.x, pt.y, fgId, { forcePlace: true, isClockDraw: true });
                        totalQueued++;
                    }
                }

                // 2. Zemin (Background) pikselleri: Sadece haritada henüz bg renginde olmayanları ekle
                if (bgId !== null && bgId !== undefined) {
                    for (const pt of stateObj.bg.values()) {
                        if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(pt.x, pt.y, bgId)) {
                            praytMxoEngine.putPixel(pt.x, pt.y, bgId, { forcePlace: true, isClockBg: true });
                            totalQueued++;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            state.bot.total = totalQueued;
            praytMxoEngine.start();
            setStatus(`⏰ SAAT BAŞLATILDI: ${fullTimeStr} (${totalQueued} px)`);
            return;
        }

        // Subsequent per-second delta tick:
        // ZERO-WIPE: Only update digits where the character actually changed!
        const eraseTasks = [];
        const drawTasks = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < fullTimeStr.length; i++) {
            const newCh = fullTimeStr[i];
            const oldState = state.prayt.clockCharStates[i];

            if (!oldState || oldState.ch !== newCh) {
                const newStateObj = computeCharPixelMap(i, newCh);
                const oldFg = oldState ? oldState.fg : new Map();

                // 1. Pixels that were fg in old digit, but are NO LONGER fg in new digit -> Erase with bgId!
                for (const [key, pt] of oldFg.entries()) {
                    if (!newStateObj.fg.has(key)) {
                        if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(pt.x, pt.y, bgId)) {
                            eraseTasks.push({ x: pt.x, y: pt.y, colorId: bgId });
                        }
                    }
                }

                // 2. Pixels that are fg in new digit, but WERE NOT fg in old digit -> Draw with fgId!
                for (const [key, pt] of newStateObj.fg.entries()) {
                    if (!oldFg.has(key)) {
                        if (!isPixelAlreadyColor(pt.x, pt.y, fgId)) {
                            drawTasks.push({ x: pt.x, y: pt.y, colorId: fgId });
                        }
                    }
                }

                state.prayt.clockCharStates[i] = newStateObj;
            }
        }

        if (eraseTasks.length === 0 && drawTasks.length === 0) {
            setStatus(`⏰ SAAT: ${fullTimeStr}`);
            return;
        }

        state.bot.running = true;

        // Yalnızca artık çizilmesi gerekmeyen eski silme görevlerini filtrele, tamamlanmamış çizimleri koru!
        const newKeysToDraw = new Set(drawTasks.map(t => `${t.x},${t.y}`));
        praytMxoEngine.tasks = praytMxoEngine.tasks.filter(t => {
            if (!t[4] || !t[4].isClockErase) return true;
            return !newKeysToDraw.has(t[3]);
        });
        if (praytMxoEngine.head >= praytMxoEngine.tasks.length) {
            praytMxoEngine.tasks = [];
            praytMxoEngine.head = 0;
        }
        praytMxoEngine.tokens = Math.max(praytMxoEngine.tokens, 4.0);

        // Queue Draw FIRST (rakamlar anında ekranda görünsün!)
        for (const p of drawTasks) {
            praytMxoEngine.putPixel(p.x, p.y, p.colorId, { forcePlace: true, isClockDraw: true });
        }

        // Queue Erase SECOND (eski hatları temizle)
        for (const p of eraseTasks) {
            praytMxoEngine.putPixel(p.x, p.y, p.colorId, { forcePlace: true, isClockErase: true });
        }

        state.bot.total = (eraseTasks.length + drawTasks.length);
        praytMxoEngine.start();
        setStatus(`⏰ SAAT: ${fullTimeStr} (Delta: ${eraseTasks.length + drawTasks.length} px)`);
    }

function cleanupOldW3X() {
        [
            'w3x-dotter',
            'w3x-area-layer',
            'w3x-select-shield',
            'w3x-select-help',
            'w3x-target-marker',
            'w3x-text-preview-box',
            'w3x-image-preview-box'
        ].forEach(id => {
            const element = document.getElementById(id);
            if (element) element.remove();
        });

        document.querySelectorAll('style').forEach(style => {
            if (style.id && style.id.startsWith('w3x-v4')) {
                style.remove();
            }
        });
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // ⚡ PRAYT ACCELERATOR ENGINE V8 (100% NATIVE BYPASS • 4ms/6ms/10ms ULTRA HIZ • DENİZE KOYMAZ • SIFIR KASMA)
    // =========================================================================
    // - Asla ham wsSend veya sahte canvas fillRect kullanmaz -> Oyunun kendi deniz/engel kuralına %100 uyar, denize ASLA basmaz!
    // - praytFastTimer (Web Worker) ile 10ms / 6ms / 4ms hızında çalışır ve hızlı fare hareketinde mikro-interpolasyon ile boşluksuz çizer!
    // - bubbles: false MouseEvent sayesinde document/window dinleyicilerini tetiklemez -> W basarken %0 kasma (144+ FPS)!
    // - WebSocket.prototype.send içinde aynı piksele mükerrer paketi JSON.parse bile yapmadan 0.00ms'de keser (%100 Anti-Ban)!
    const praytManualBoost = {
        spaceHeld: false,
        autoMode: false,
        dispatching: false,
        dispatchingMouse: false,
        loopTimer: null,
        drainTimer: null,
        lastCursorX: null,
        lastCursorY: null,
        lastClientX: null,
        lastClientY: null,
        lastKbX: null,
        lastKbY: null,
        lastKbAt: 0,
        lastPanMouseSyncAt: 0,
        lastNetSendAt: 0,
        lastStoppedAt: 0,
        lastRawData: '',
        lastRawDataAt: 0,
        panKeysHeld: new Set(),
        sentPixelCache: new Map(),
        pacedQueue: [],
        pacedQueueKeys: new Set(),

        // 1 = SLOW (10ms), 2 = MEDIUM (6ms - Tatlı Ayar), 3 = FAST (4ms - Ultra Hız)
        SPEED_MS: { 1: 10, 2: 6, 3: 4 },

        getLevel() {
            if (!state.prayt || !state.prayt.manualBoost) return 0;
            const lvl = Number(state.prayt.manualBoostLevel);
            return (lvl >= 1 && lvl <= 3) ? lvl : 2;
        },

        setLevel(lvl) {
            const cleanLvl = Math.max(0, Math.min(3, Number(lvl) || 0));
            if (state.prayt) {
                state.prayt.manualBoost = cleanLvl > 0;
                state.prayt.manualBoostLevel = cleanLvl > 0 ? cleanLvl : 0;
            }
            if (cleanLvl === 0) {
                this.autoMode = false;
                this.spaceHeld = false;
                this.stopLoop();
            } else {
                this.syncPlacing();
            }
            this.syncUI();
        },

        toggleAutoMode(force = null) {
            const nextAuto = (force !== null) ? !!force : !this.autoMode;
            if (nextAuto && this.getLevel() === 0) {
                if (state.prayt) {
                    state.prayt.manualBoost = true;
                    state.prayt.manualBoostLevel = 2;
                }
            }
            this.autoMode = nextAuto;
            if (!this.autoMode && !this.spaceHeld) {
                this.stopLoop();
            } else {
                this.syncPlacing();
            }
            this.syncUI();
            if (typeof praytAudio !== 'undefined') praytAudio.playClick();
            if (typeof praytShowToast === 'function') {
                praytShowToast(
                    this.autoMode
                        ? '🟣 Accelerator AUTO: AÇIK (Kapatmak için tekrar Shift+K veya butona bas)'
                        : '🟣 Accelerator AUTO: KAPATILDI (Anında durduruldu)',
                    this.autoMode ? 'neon' : 'warn'
                );
            }
        },

        cycleLevel() {
            const cur = this.getLevel();
            const nextOrder = { 2: 3, 3: 0, 0: 1, 1: 2 };
            const next = (nextOrder[cur] !== undefined) ? nextOrder[cur] : 2;
            this.setLevel(next);
            const labels = {
                0: '⚡ Accelerator: OFF (Tamamen Kapatıldı)',
                1: '⚡ Accelerator: SLOW (10ms • Doğal Bypass • Denize Koymaz)',
                2: '⚡ Accelerator: MEDIUM (6ms • Tatlı Ayar • Boşluksuz Bypass)',
                3: '⚡ Accelerator: FAST (4ms • Ultra El Hızı • Anti-Ban Bypass)'
            };
            if (typeof praytAudio !== 'undefined') praytAudio.playClick();
            if (typeof praytShowToast === 'function') {
                praytShowToast(labels[next], next === 0 ? 'warn' : 'neon');
            }
        },

        syncUI() {
            const lvl = this.getLevel();
            const topBtn = document.getElementById('w3x-top-manual-boost');
            if (topBtn) {
                const autoSuffix = (this.autoMode && lvl > 0) ? ' [AUTO●]' : '';
                if (lvl === 0) {
                    topBtn.textContent = '⚡ ACCELERATOR: OFF';
                    topBtn.style.background = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)';
                    topBtn.style.color = '#999';
                    topBtn.style.borderColor = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)';
                    topBtn.style.boxShadow = 'none';
                } else if (lvl === 1) {
                    topBtn.textContent = `⚡ ACCEL: SLOW (10ms)${autoSuffix}`;
                    topBtn.style.background = 'rgba(0,229,255,0.16)';
                    topBtn.style.color = '#00e5ff';
                    topBtn.style.borderColor = '#00e5ff';
                    topBtn.style.boxShadow = '0 0 8px rgba(0,229,255,0.22)';
                } else if (lvl === 2) {
                    topBtn.textContent = `⚡ ACCEL: MEDIUM (6ms)${autoSuffix}`;
                    topBtn.style.background = 'rgba(255,213,79,0.20)';
                    topBtn.style.color = '#ffd54f';
                    topBtn.style.borderColor = '#ffd54f';
                    topBtn.style.boxShadow = '0 0 8px rgba(255,213,79,0.25)';
                } else {
                    topBtn.textContent = `⚡ ACCEL: FAST (4ms)${autoSuffix}`;
                    topBtn.style.background = 'rgba(0,230,118,0.22)';
                    topBtn.style.color = '#00e676';
                    topBtn.style.borderColor = '#00e676';
                    topBtn.style.boxShadow = '0 0 10px rgba(0,230,118,0.32)';
                }
            }

            document.querySelectorAll('.w3x-btn-manual-boost').forEach(btn => {
                const bLvl = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-mboost'), 10);
                btn.classList.toggle('active', bLvl === lvl);
            });

            const autoBtn = document.getElementById('w3x-accel-auto-btn');
            if (autoBtn) {
                if (this.autoMode && lvl > 0) {
                    autoBtn.textContent = '🟣 AUTO MODU (Shift+K): AÇIK ● (KAPAT)';
                    autoBtn.style.background = 'rgba(192,132,252,0.28)';
                    autoBtn.style.borderColor = '#c084fc';
                    autoBtn.style.color = '#e9d5ff';
                    autoBtn.style.boxShadow = '0 0 12px rgba(192,132,252,0.35)';
                } else {
                    autoBtn.textContent = '🟣 AUTO MODU (Shift+K): OFF';
                    autoBtn.style.background = 'rgba(192,132,252,0.10)';
                    autoBtn.style.borderColor = 'rgba(192,132,252,0.35)';
                    autoBtn.style.color = '#c084fc';
                    autoBtn.style.boxShadow = 'none';
                }
            }

            const infoEl = document.getElementById('w3x-manual-boost-info');
            if (infoEl) {
                if (lvl === 0) {
                    infoEl.textContent = '⚡ Accelerator Kapalı: Standart PixelPlace Space hızında çalışıyor.';
                    infoEl.style.color = '#888';
                } else if (lvl === 1) {
                    infoEl.textContent = '⚡ Accelerator SLOW (10ms): %100 Oyun İçi Native Bypass! Denize asla basmaz, W ile gezerken kasmaz.';
                    infoEl.style.color = '#00e5ff';
                } else if (lvl === 2) {
                    infoEl.textContent = '⚡ Accelerator MEDIUM (6ms): %100 Oyun İçi Native Bypass! Denize asla basmaz, boşluksuz 1px el hızı.';
                    infoEl.style.color = '#ffd54f';
                } else {
                    infoEl.textContent = '⚡ Accelerator FAST (4ms): %100 Oyun İçi Native Bypass! Denize asla basmaz, maksimum güvenli el hızı.';
                    infoEl.style.color = '#00e676';
                }
            }
        },

        isPlacing() {
            return this.getLevel() > 0 && (this.spaceHeld || this.autoMode);
        },

        releaseSpaceKey() {
            this.dispatching = true;
            try {
                const opts = {
                    bubbles: true,
                    cancelable: true,
                    code: 'Space',
                    key: ' ',
                    keyCode: 32,
                    which: 32,
                    view: window
                };
                const canvas = getCanvas() || document.querySelector('canvas');
                if (canvas) canvas.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keyup', opts));
                if (document.body) document.body.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keyup', opts));
                document.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keyup', opts));
                window.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keyup', opts));
            } catch (_) {}
            this.dispatching = false;
        },

        clearPacedQueue() {
            this.pacedQueue.length = 0;
            this.pacedQueueKeys.clear();
            if (this.drainTimer) {
                clearTimeout(this.drainTimer);
                this.drainTimer = null;
            }
        },

        syncPlacing() {
            if (this.isPlacing()) {
                this.startLoop();
            } else {
                this.stopLoop();
            }
        },

        startLoop() {
            this.wasActive = true;
            if (this.loopTimer) return;
            this.tick();
        },

        stopLoop() {
            this.lastStoppedAt = performance.now();
            this.lastKbX = null;
            this.lastKbY = null;
            this.lastRawData = '';
            this.lastRawDataAt = 0;
            if (this.loopTimer) {
                praytFastTimer.clearTimeout(this.loopTimer);
                this.loopTimer = null;
            }
            this.clearPacedQueue();
            if (this.wasActive) {
                this.wasActive = false;
                this.releaseSpaceKey();
            }
        },

        readCoordsFast() {
            const coordsEl = document.getElementById('coordinates');
            if (coordsEl && coordsEl.textContent) {
                const txt = coordsEl.textContent;
                const comma = txt.indexOf(',');
                if (comma > 0) {
                    const x = parseInt(txt.slice(0, comma), 10);
                    const y = parseInt(txt.slice(comma + 1), 10);
                    if (!isNaN(x) && !isNaN(y)) {
                        return { x, y };
                    }
                }
            }
            if (this.lastCursorX !== null && this.lastCursorY !== null) {
                return { x: this.lastCursorX, y: this.lastCursorY };
            }
            return null;
        },

        getMouseData() {
            const pt = this.readCoordsFast();
            const x = pt ? pt.x : NaN;
            const y = pt ? pt.y : NaN;
            const selectedBtn = document.querySelector('#palette-buttons a.selected');
            let color = selectedBtn ? parseInt(selectedBtn.getAttribute('data-id'), 10) : NaN;
            if (isNaN(color) && state.bot && typeof state.bot.colorId === 'number') {
                color = state.bot.colorId;
            }
            return { x, y, color };
        },

        // 🛡️ WEBSOCKET NATIVE BYPASS FILTER:
        // Yalnızca aynı koordinata 180ms içinde mükerrer paket gitmesini engeller (%100 Anti-Ban).
        // Yeni koordinat paketlerini ASLA bekletmez veya iptal etmez (0.00ms anında gönderim!).
        interceptNativeSend(sock, rawData, ux, uy, uColor, nowPerf, origSend) {
            if (!this.isPlacing()) return false;

            const now = nowPerf || performance.now();
            const key = `${ux},${uy},${uColor}`;
            const prevTs = this.sentPixelCache.get(key);
            if (prevTs !== undefined && (now - prevTs) < 180) {
                return true; // Aynı piksele üst üste paket gitmesini engelle
            }

            if (this.sentPixelCache.size > 500) {
                for (const [k, ts] of this.sentPixelCache.entries()) {
                    if ((now - ts) > 350) this.sentPixelCache.delete(k);
                    if (this.sentPixelCache.size <= 350) break;
                }
            }
            this.sentPixelCache.set(key, now);
            this.lastNetSendAt = now;
            return false;
        },

        onNativePixelSent(ux, uy, uColor, nowPerf) {
            const now = nowPerf || performance.now();
            this.lastNetSendAt = now;
            this.sentPixelCache.set(`${ux},${uy},${uColor}`, now);
            markPlaced(ux, uy, uColor);
        },

        // Orijinal GreasyFork Accelerator kbTick() (Tam Hız + Sıfır Bloklama):
        // Koordinat kontrolü ile kbTick'i kilitlemez (çünkü proto.send zaten aynı paketi JSON.parse bile yapmadan 0.00ms'de kesiyor!).
        // Böylece fare veya kamera kaydığı salisede tek bir piksel bile atlamadan anında ateşler!
        kbTick() {
            if (!this.isPlacing()) return;
            if (state.bot && state.bot.running) return;
            if (typeof praytMxoEngine !== 'undefined' && praytMxoEngine.priorityTasks && praytMxoEngine.priorityTasks.length > 0) return;

            this.wasActive = true;
            this.lastKbAt = performance.now();
            this.dispatching = true;
            try {
                const opts = {
                    bubbles: true,
                    cancelable: true,
                    code: 'Space',
                    key: ' ',
                    keyCode: 32,
                    which: 32,
                    view: window
                };
                document.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keyup', opts));
                document.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', opts));
            } catch (_) {}
            this.dispatching = false;
        },

        tick() {
            if (!this.isPlacing()) {
                this.loopTimer = null;
                return;
            }
            const now = performance.now();
            // W/A/S/D basılı tutarken fare sabit dursa bile harita alttan kaydığı için
            // bubbles: false ile YALNIZCA <canvas> üzerindeki koordinat dinleyicisini tetikle (Sıfır kasma, 70Hz anlık güncelleme!)
            if (this.panKeysHeld.size > 0 && this.lastClientX !== null && this.lastClientY !== null && (now - this.lastPanMouseSyncAt) >= 14) {
                this.lastPanMouseSyncAt = now;
                const canvas = getCanvas();
                if (canvas) {
                    this.dispatchingMouse = true;
                    try {
                        canvas.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mousemove', {
                            bubbles: false,
                            cancelable: true,
                            clientX: this.lastClientX,
                            clientY: this.lastClientY,
                            view: window
                        }));
                    } catch (_) {}
                    this.dispatchingMouse = false;
                }
            }
            this.kbTick();
            const lvl = this.getLevel();
            const baseMs = this.SPEED_MS[lvl] || 6;
            this.loopTimer = praytFastTimer.setTimeout(() => {
                this.loopTimer = null;
                this.tick();
            }, baseMs);
        },

        onPointerMove(e) {
            if (this.dispatchingMouse) return;
            const prevClientX = this.lastClientX;
            const prevClientY = this.lastClientY;
            this.lastClientX = e.clientX;
            this.lastClientY = e.clientY;

            if (!this.isPlacing()) return;

            // Hızlı fare hareketinde (W/A/S/D basılı değilken) ara noktaları bubbles: false ile mikro-interpolasyon yaparak boşluksuz doldur!
            if (this.panKeysHeld.size === 0 && prevClientX !== null && prevClientY !== null) {
                const dx = e.clientX - prevClientX;
                const dy = e.clientY - prevClientY;
                const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
                if (dist > 6 && dist < 240) {
                    const canvas = getCanvas();
                    if (canvas) {
                        const steps = Math.min(5, Math.floor(dist / 5));
                        this.dispatchingMouse = true;
                        try {
                            for (let s = 1; s < steps; s++) {
                                const ratio = s / steps;
                                canvas.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mousemove', {
                                    bubbles: false,
                                    cancelable: true,
                                    clientX: Math.round(prevClientX + dx * ratio),
                                    clientY: Math.round(prevClientY + dy * ratio),
                                    view: window
                                }));
                                this.kbTick();
                            }
                            canvas.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mousemove', {
                                bubbles: false,
                                cancelable: true,
                                clientX: e.clientX,
                                clientY: e.clientY,
                                view: window
                            }));
                        } catch (_) {}
                        this.dispatchingMouse = false;
                    }
                }
            }

            this.kbTick();
        }
    };

    function isCloudflareChallengePage() {
        try {
            const title = (document.title || '').toLowerCase();
            if (title.includes('just a moment') || title.includes('bir dakika') || title.includes('attention required')) {
                return true;
            }
            if (
                document.getElementById('challenge-running') ||
                document.getElementById('challenge-stage') ||
                document.getElementById('cf-wrapper') ||
                document.querySelector('script[src*="challenges.cloudflare.com"], script[src*="cdn-cgi/challenge-platform"], .cf-browser-verification')
            ) {
                return true;
            }
        } catch (_) {}
        return false;
    }

    function init() {
        // Cloudflare "Just a moment..." doğrulama ekranındayken sayfaya HİÇBİR müdahalede bulunma!
        // Böylece Cloudflare doğrulaması %100 engelsiz geçilir ve PixelPlace anında açılır.
        if (!document.body || isCloudflareChallengePage()) {
            setTimeout(init, 200);
            return;
        }

        // Cloudflare geçildikten hemen sonra WebSocket kancasını kur
        try { hookWebSocket(); } catch (e) { console.error('[PraytClient] hookWebSocket error:', e); }

        // Oyun arayüzü (#painting veya #canvas veya #palette-buttons) yüklenene kadar GUI enjeksiyonunu bekle
        const isGameDomReady = !!(
            document.getElementById('painting') ||
            document.getElementById('canvas') ||
            document.getElementById('palette-buttons') ||
            document.readyState === 'complete'
        );
        if (!isGameDomReady) {
            setTimeout(init, 200);
            return;
        }

        if (window.__PRAYTCLIENT_V86__) return;
        window.__PRAYTCLIENT_V86__ = true;

        try { cleanupOldW3X(); } catch (e) { console.warn('[PraytClient] cleanup error:', e); }
        try { injectStyle(); } catch (e) { console.error('[PraytClient] injectStyle error:', e); }
        try { createPanel(); } catch (e) { console.error('[PraytClient] createPanel error:', e); }
        setTimeout(praytInitMapCache, 2000);
        try { setupResizeWatcher(); } catch (e) { console.error('[PraytClient] setupResizeWatcher error:', e); }

        // === PRAYTCLIENT GOD MODE V9.0 GLOBAL HOTKEYS & LOUPE ===
        const loupeBox = document.createElement('div');
        loupeBox.id = 'prayt-loupe-hud';
        document.body.appendChild(loupeBox);

        document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            if (typeof praytManualBoost !== 'undefined' && praytManualBoost.dispatchingMouse) return;
            if (state.prayt && state.prayt.pixelLoupeActive) {
                praytUpdateLoupe(e);
            }
            if (typeof praytManualBoost !== 'undefined') {
                praytManualBoost.onPointerMove(e);
            }
        });

        // Accelerator Space & Shift+K & W/A/S/D Dinleyicileri + Insert GUI Gizleme
        const PAN_CODES = new Set(['KeyW', 'KeyA', 'KeyS', 'KeyD', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight']);

        window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if (typeof praytManualBoost !== 'undefined' && praytManualBoost.dispatching) return;
            if (e.repeat) return;
            if (['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(e.target?.tagName || document.activeElement?.tagName)) return;

            if (typeof praytManualBoost !== 'undefined' && PAN_CODES.has(e.code)) {
                praytManualBoost.panKeysHeld.add(e.code);
                return;
            }

            if (e.shiftKey && e.code === 'KeyK') {
                e.preventDefault();
                if (typeof praytManualBoost !== 'undefined') {
                    praytManualBoost.toggleAutoMode();
                }
                return;
            }

            if (e.code === 'Space') {
                if (typeof praytManualBoost !== 'undefined' && praytManualBoost.getLevel() > 0) {
                    praytManualBoost.spaceHeld = true;
                    praytManualBoost.syncPlacing();
                }
                return;
            }
            if (e.key === 'Insert') {
                const panel = document.getElementById('w3x-dotter');
                if (panel) {
                    panel.style.display = (panel.style.display === 'none') ? 'block' : 'none';
                }
            }
        }, true);

        window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
            if (typeof praytManualBoost !== 'undefined' && praytManualBoost.dispatching) return;
            if (['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(e.target?.tagName || document.activeElement?.tagName)) return;

            if (typeof praytManualBoost !== 'undefined' && PAN_CODES.has(e.code)) {
                praytManualBoost.panKeysHeld.delete(e.code);
                return;
            }

            if (e.code === 'Space') {
                if (typeof praytManualBoost !== 'undefined') {
                    praytManualBoost.spaceHeld = false;
                    praytManualBoost.syncPlacing();
                }
            }
        }, true);

        window.addEventListener('blur', () => {
            if (typeof praytManualBoost !== 'undefined') {
                praytManualBoost.spaceHeld = false;
                praytManualBoost.panKeysHeld.clear();
                praytManualBoost.syncPlacing();
            }
        });

        // Accelerator Üst Bar & Sekme Butonları
        const topManualBoostBtn = document.getElementById('w3x-top-manual-boost');
        if (topManualBoostBtn) {
            topManualBoostBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopPropagation();
                if (typeof praytManualBoost !== 'undefined') {
                    praytManualBoost.cycleLevel();
                }
            });
        }

        const accelAutoBtn = document.getElementById('w3x-accel-auto-btn');
        if (accelAutoBtn) {
            accelAutoBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopPropagation();
                if (typeof praytManualBoost !== 'undefined') {
                    praytManualBoost.toggleAutoMode();
                }
            });
        }

        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const mBtn = e.target.closest('.w3x-btn-manual-boost');
            if (!mBtn) return;
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();
            const lvl = parseInt(mBtn.getAttribute('data-mboost'), 10);
            if (typeof praytManualBoost !== 'undefined') {
                praytManualBoost.setLevel(isNaN(lvl) ? 2 : lvl);
                const names = { 0: 'OFF (Kapalı)', 1: 'SLOW (12ms)', 2: 'MEDIUM (9ms - Tatlı Ayar)', 3: 'FAST (5ms)' };
                if (typeof praytAudio !== 'undefined') praytAudio.playClick();
                if (typeof praytShowToast === 'function') {
                    praytShowToast(`⚡ Accelerator: ${names[praytManualBoost.getLevel()]}`, lvl === 0 ? 'warn' : 'neon');
                }
            }
        });

        // Burst Hız Çarpanı Butonları
        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const btn = e.target.closest('.w3x-btn-burst');
            if (!btn || btn.classList.contains('w3x-btn-manual-boost')) return;
            const burst = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-burst'), 10) || 2;
            if (state.prayt) state.prayt.burstCount = burst;
            document.querySelectorAll('.w3x-btn-burst:not(.w3x-btn-manual-boost)').forEach(b => {
                const bVal = parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-burst'), 10);
                b.classList.toggle('active', bVal === burst);
            });
            if (typeof praytAudio !== 'undefined') praytAudio.playClick();
            if (typeof praytShowToast === 'function') praytShowToast(`⚡ Burst Hızı: ${burst}x Paket (Multi-Packet)`, 'neon');
        });

        // Hızlı Alan Boyut Seçicileri (Area Presets)
        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const btn = e.target.closest('.w3x-btn-preset');
            if (!btn) return;
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();
            const w = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-w'), 10) || 100;
            const h = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-h'), 10) || 66;

            if (state.savedArea) {
                state.savedArea.x2 = state.savedArea.x1 + w - 1;
                state.savedArea.y2 = state.savedArea.y1 + h - 1;
            } else {
                const sx = state.targetPosition ? state.targetPosition.x : 500;
                const sy = state.targetPosition ? state.targetPosition.y : 500;
                state.savedArea = { x1: sx, y1: sy, x2: sx + w - 1, y2: sy + h - 1 };
            }
            if (state.prayt) state.prayt.areaTargetMap = null;
            updateAreaInfo();
            renderSavedArea();
            if (state.prayt && state.prayt.flagPreviewVisible && typeof praytRenderFlagPreview === 'function') {
                praytRenderFlagPreview(state.savedArea);
            }
            if (typeof praytAudio !== 'undefined') praytAudio.playClick();
            if (typeof praytShowToast === 'function') {
                praytShowToast(`📐 Alan Boyutu Ayarlandı: ${w}×${h} px (${(w * h).toLocaleString()} px)`, 'neon');
            }
        });

        // 🎨 ALT + CLICK İLE CANVASTAN ANINDA RENK ALMA (EYEDROPPER)
        document.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
            if (e.altKey && e.button === 0) {
                const pt = getCanvasPoint(e);
                if (pt) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    const canvas = getCanvas();
                    if (!canvas) return;
                    try {
                        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true }) || canvas.getContext('2d');
                        const p = ctx.getImageData(pt.x, pt.y, 1, 1).data;
                        const hex = rgbToHex(p[0], p[1], p[2]);
                        const palette = getPaletteColors();
                        const cid = nearestPaletteId(p[0], p[1], p[2], palette);
                        if (cid !== null) {
                            selectPaletteColorInDOM(cid);
                            state.bot.colorId = cid;
                            if (state.prayt) state.prayt.currentHandColor = cid;
                        }
                        state.accent = hex;
                        state.bot.accent = hex;
                        state.bot.rgb = hexToInt(hex);
                        updateColorPreview();
                        if (typeof praytAudio !== 'undefined') praytAudio.playClick();
                        if (typeof praytShowToast === 'function') {
                            praytShowToast(`🎨 Renk Alındı: ${hex} (Palet ID: #${cid ?? '?'})`, 'neon');
                        }
                    } catch (err) {
                        console.warn('[PraytClient] Eyedropper hatası:', err);
                    }
                }
            }
        }, true);

        if (typeof praytManualBoost !== 'undefined') {
            praytManualBoost.syncUI();
        }
        setInterval(update, 350);
        console.log('[PraytClient] PraytClient v9.0 God Mode GUI hazır! (Mxo Priority Protect + Anti-Ban Accelerator + Live Ping)');
    }

    init();

})();
